Method: 提出Ortho-ReID：1）利用VLM文本描述建模低秩服装子空间；2）设计基于Transformer的Basis Maker，通过交叉注意力生成实例自适应的低秩子空间；3）服装子空间由文本嵌入对齐监督；4）身份特征通过可学习投影头提取，并与服装子空间强制正交。

Result: 在PRCC上top-1提升5.9%，Celeb-reID-light提升3.5%，LaST提升5.3%，在LTCC上也取得有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: 直接利用几何正交约束替代对抗学习进行特征解耦是有效的，利用VLM先验知识构建自适应低秩子空间可以更好地处理换衣场景下的外观变化问题。

Abstract: Clothes-changing person re-identification (CC-ReID) aims to recognize individuals despite drastic appearance changes caused by clothing variation. While existing methods rely on adversarial learning to disentangle clothing features, we propose Ortho-ReID, which explicitly models a low-rank clothing subspace from VLM text descriptions and extracts clothing-invariant representations via direct geometric constraints. A critical component is our transformer-based Basis Maker, which refines a shared, low-dimensional clothing prior into an instance-adaptive low-rank subspace through cross-attention with image patches, enabling robust clothing feature extraction even under varying visibility conditions. This instance-adaptive subspace is supervised via alignment with clothing text embeddings, while identity features are extracted via a learnable projection head and geometrically constrained to be strictly orthogonal to it. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on PRCC (+5.9% top-1), Celeb-reID-light (+3.5%), and LaST (+5.3%), with competitive results on LTCC.

</details>


### [74] [ARGUS: Stacked Multi-View Identity Mosaic Injection for Subject-Preserving Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11670)
*Zijie Meng,Jiwen Liu,Yufei Liu,Chengzhuo Tong,Xiaoqiang Liu,Yuanxing Zhang,Yulong Xu,Pengfei Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Argus框架，通过堆叠多视角身份马赛克注入（SMII）机制，将身份从单点静态参考转变为动态分布，解决了视频生成中跨姿态、视角、表情等变化下的身份保持难题，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于单帧参考点的身份保持方法存在根本瓶颈：将身份坍缩为与姿态、配饰、光照等纠缠的静态观测，无法处理运动、大视角变化、表情偏移、遮挡、尺度变化以及文本/首帧/身份参考之间的冲突。

Method: 提出Argus框架，核心是堆叠多视角身份马赛克注入（SMII）：1) MLLM选择信息量最大的图像/视频身份证据并转换为3*3堆叠马赛克；2) 马赛克与当前扩散时间步同步，作为负时间只读内存注入Wan原生token空间。此外包含MLLM身份导演（解决条件冲突）、无交叉对反事实训练、时序身份退火和自适应自相似性引导。

Result: 在OpenS2V-Eval Human-Domain上达到64.38总分、71.86 FaceSim、51.62 NexusScore、79.14 NaturalScore。在HardID-Celeb基准上获得76.80 FaceSim，YawScore和OccScore分别比最强基线提升12.60和15.10分。

Conclusion: 动态身份记忆和大规模反事实自监督对于保持身份的视频生成非常有效，证明了点参考范式的局限性并提供了替代方案。

Abstract: Subject-preserving video generation is not solved by frontal-face similarity alone: a generated person must remain recognizable across motion, large viewpoint changes, expression shifts, occlusion, scale variation, and conflicts among text, first-frame, and identity references. We argue that the central bottleneck is the point-reference paradigm, which collapses identity into a single static observation entangled with pose, accessories, lighting, background, and camera statistics. We introduce Argus, a Wan-based framework centered on Stacked Multi-View Identity Mosaic Injection (SMII). SMII converts MLLM-selected image/video identity evidence into a 3*3 stacked mosaic, synchronizes the mosaic with the current diffusion time, and injects it as negative-time read-only memory in Wan's native token space. This turns identity from an external clean adapter or a single reference image into a compact dynamic distribution. Around SMII, an MLLM Identity Director selects informative identity moments and resolves condition conflicts, while no-cross-pair counterfactual training, Temporal Identity Annealing, and Adaptive Self-Likeness Guidance improve robustness without paired subject-video supervision. We further release HardID-Celeb, a public-figure identity-stress benchmark, and introduce YawScore and OccScore to probe large-yaw and first-frame-occlusion robustness. Argus achieves state-of-the-art results on OpenS2V-Eval Human-Domain, reaching 64.38 Total Score, 71.86 FaceSim, 51.62 NexusScore, and 79.14 NaturalScore. On HardID-Celeb, Argus obtains 76.80 FaceSim and improves YawScore and OccScore by 12.60 and 15.10 points over the strongest baselines, demonstrating that dynamic identity memory and large-scale counterfactual self-supervision are highly effective for subject-preserving video generation.

</details>


### [75] [OpenMedReason: Scientific Reasoning Supervision for Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12169)
*Negin Baghbanzadeh,Pritam Sarkar,Michael Colacci,Abeer Badawi,Adibvafa Fallahpour,Arash Afkanpour,Leonid Sigal,Ali Etemad,Elham Dolatabadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了OpenMedReason，一个包含约45万样本的大规模开放式多模态医学推理语料库，其推理轨迹主要来源于生物医学科学文献而非合成思维链。配套的OpenMedReason-Bench基准从感知、医学知识和推理三个维度对LVLM进行细粒度评估。实验表明，使用该语料库进行SFT和强化对齐训练后，VQA准确率平均提升20%，性能达到最强可比规模医学LVLM的4.2%以内，且86.1%的成对比较中推理轨迹优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险临床场景下，大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）的推理需要基于视觉证据和临床知识，而不仅仅是给出正确的最终答案。现有模型和数据集缺乏高保真度的推理轨迹监督，限制了对模型推理能力的细粒度评估和提升。

Method: 构建OpenMedReason语料库（约45万图像-问题-答案实例，推理轨迹来自生物医学科学文献）和OpenMedReason-Bench基准（从感知、医学知识、推理三个维度评估）。采用监督微调（SFT）和基于强化的对齐训练。

Result: VQA准确率平均提升20%，性能达到最强可比规模医学LVLM的4.2%以内。在感知、医学知识和推理三个维度均有提升，86.1%的成对比较中推理轨迹优于基线模型。

Conclusion: OpenMedReason作为丰富的训练资源，在SFT和强化对齐中均表现出有效性，能全面提升LVLM在医学领域的感知、知识和推理能力，为医学LVLM的诊断式评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: High-stakes clinical use of large vision-language models (LVLMs) requires reasoning that is grounded in visual evidence and clinical knowledge, not just correct final answers. We introduce OpenMedReason, a large-scale, open multimodal medical reasoning corpus comprising approximately 450K image-question-answer instances whose reasoning traces are primarily derived from curated biomedical, human-authored scientific articles. OpenMedReason provides high-fidelity supervision beyond synthetic chains of thought, covering diverse medical domain vision modalities such as radiological scans, microscopic images, visible light photographs, charts, and others. We complement it with OpenMedReason-Bench, a held-out benchmark that allows fine-grained evaluation of LVLMs along three complementary axes of capability, including perception, medical knowledge, and rationale, enabling diagnostic evaluation beyond final-answer accuracy. OpenMedReason is a rich training resource that exhibits its effectiveness in both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement-based alignment. Training with OpenMedReason yields a 20% average improvement in VQA accuracy over the base model and achieves performance within 4.2% of the strongest comparable-scale medical LVLMs. Fine-grained performance analysis confirms that the gains are not concentrated in any single axis: OpenMedReason improves perception, medical knowledge, and rationale jointly, and its reasoning traces are preferred over those of the base model in 86.1% of pairwise comparisons. We release the code and dataset at huggingface.co/datasets/neginb/OpenMedReason.

</details>


### [76] [Parameter-Efficient Adapter Tuning for Tabular-Image Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11682)
*Jiaqi Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TI-Adapter框架，通过冻结预训练表格编码器+适配器，以及图像分支的嵌入层和瓶颈层适配器，实现高效的表格-图像多模态学习，在20个数据集上与全参数微调性能相当或更优，且大幅减少可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格-图像多模态学习中，全参数微调计算成本高，而冻结编码器会限制任务自适应能力，需要一种高效且性能不降的微调方法。

Method: TI-Adapter框架：冻结预训练表格编码器后添加适配器；图像分支采用嵌入级和瓶颈级适配器替代全参数微调，实现模态特定的高效适配。

Result: 在20个表格-图像数据集上，TI-Adapter在预测性能上达到或超越全参数微调，同时显著减少可训练参数数量。消融实验验证了适配器位置对平衡性能与效率的重要性。

Conclusion: TI-Adapter是一种高效的多模态适配框架，通过模态特定的适配器设计，在保持或提升性能的同时大幅降低计算开销，为表格-图像多模态学习提供了实用的微调方案。

Abstract: Tabular-image multimodal learning aims to improve predictive modeling by jointly using structured tabular attributes and visual data. Although pretrained encoders provide strong modality-specific representations, full fine-tuning can be computationally expensive, while keeping encoders frozen may limit task-specific adaptation. We propose the Tabular-Image Adapter (TI-Adapter), a modality-specific adapter-based fine-tuning framework for efficient multimodal adaptation. TI-Adapter freezes the pretrained tabular encoder and learns an adapter after the extracted tabular embedding, while adapting the image branch with embedding-level and bottleneck-level adapters instead of full fine-tuning. Experiments on 20 tabular-image datasets show that TI-Adapter achieves competitive or better predictive performance than full fine-tuning while using substantially fewer trainable parameters. Ablation studies further demonstrate the importance of adapter placement for balancing performance and practical efficiency.

</details>


### [77] [Making Foresight Actionable: Repurposing Representation Alignment in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12217)
*Lu Qiu,Yizhuo Li,Yi Chen,Yuying Ge,Yixiao Ge,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现世界动作模型（WAMs）中视觉重建与动作控制之间存在表征不匹配问题，并提出AGRA方法，通过将中间视频扩散特征与基础视觉编码器的语义表征对齐，使动作解码器聚焦于任务相关交互区域，从而提升机器人操作任务中的动作提取准确性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）虽然能通过视频生成模型预测未来场景变化，但生成合理的视觉未来并不能保证提取出准确的动作。实验发现动作解码器未能关注任务相关的交互区域，且对任务无关区域的扰动敏感，这表明隐藏状态虽然优化了视觉重建，但并未以适合低层动作控制的形式组织，存在表征不匹配问题。

Method: 提出AGRA（Action-Grounded Representation Alignment）方法，通过将中间视频扩散特征与基础视觉编码器中的空间连贯语义表征进行对齐，正则化世界-动作接口。具体包括对动作解码器的注意力分析和因果干预诊断，以及通过表征对齐损失函数引导模型学习动作导向的表征。

Result: 在真实世界机器人操作任务上的实验表明，AGRA使世界模型表征更具动作导向性：动作解码器正确聚焦于交互区域，物体定位准确率和 affordance 理解能力得到提升，策略对任务无关区域扰动更加鲁棒，同时在分布内性能和分布外泛化能力上均持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: AGRA通过表征对齐有效解决了世界动作模型中视觉重建与动作控制之间的表征不匹配问题，为提升基于视频生成模型的机器人操作策略的准确性和鲁棒性提供了有效途径。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) offer a promising route for robot manipulation by using video generation models to model future scene evolution before producing control actions. However, our empirical observations reveal a phenomenon: generating plausible visual futures does not always guarantee the extraction of accurate actions. To diagnose this failure, we conduct action-head attention analysis and causal interventions. We find that the action decoder fails to focus on task-relevant interaction regions and remains sensitive to perturbations in task-irrelevant areas. This reveals a representation mismatch: hidden states optimized for visual reconstruction are not inherently organized in a form useful for low-level action control. In this paper, we propose AGRA, an Action-Grounded Representation Alignment objective that regularizes the world-action interface by aligning intermediate video diffusion features with spatially coherent semantic representations from a foundation visual encoder. We evaluate AGRA on real-world manipulation tasks. Experiments show that AGRA makes world model representations more action-grounded: by focusing the action decoder on the correct interaction regions, it improves object localization accuracy and affordance understanding, and makes the policy more robust to perturbations in task-irrelevant regions. As a result, AGRA consistently improves both in-distribution performance and out-of-distribution generalization over the baseline world action model.

</details>


### [78] [DroneShield-AI: A Multi-Modal Sensor Fusion Framework for Real-Time Autonomous Drone Threat Detection, Behavioral Intent Classification, and Swarm Intelligence in Contested Airspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11687)
*Marius Bayizere*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DroneShield-AI，一个集成六层处理（RF信号分类、声学电机检测、YOLOv8视觉检测、证据加权融合、行为意图分类引擎BICE、图神经网络群体智能模块GNN-SIM）的开放框架用于无人机威胁检测。BICE首次提出六类威胁分类法，可实现30秒预警；GNN-SIM是首个开放的多无人机编队对抗分析框架。在三个公开数据集上，融合管线达到96.1%检测精度、3.2%虚警率、AUC-ROC 0.981、142ms端到端延迟，系统成本约500-780美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机威胁已成为21世纪显著的安全挑战，现有解决方案缺乏统一的开放框架，在多模态融合、行为意图预测和群体智能分析方面存在不足。

Method: 提出DroneShield-AI框架，集成六个处理层：1) RF信号分类；2) 声学电机信号检测；3) YOLOv8视觉检测；4) 证据加权传感器融合；5) 行为意图分类引擎(BICE)，首次提出六类威胁分类法；6) 图神经网络群体智能模块(GNN-SIM)，首个基于图注意力网络的开放多无人机编队分析框架。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，融合管线达到96.1%检测精度、3.2%虚警率、AUC-ROC 0.981、142ms端到端延迟，系统成本约500-780美元。

Conclusion: DroneShield-AI是一个低成本、高效率、开放可复现的统一无人机威胁检测框架，通过多模态融合与行为意图分析实现了高精度低延迟的检测性能。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) threats have emerged as a defining security challenge of the 21st century. This paper presents DroneShield-AI, a unified open framework integrating six processing layers: RF signal classification, acoustic motor-signature detection, YOLOv8-based visual detection, evidence-weighted sensor fusion, a Behavioral Intent Classification Engine (BICE), and a Graph Neural Network Swarm Intelligence Module (GNN-SIM). BICE introduces the first systematic six-class threat taxonomy for drone flight patterns, enabling predictive operator alerts with a 30-second advance-warning horizon. GNN-SIM is the first open framework for adversarial multi-drone formation analysis using Graph Attention Networks. Evaluated on three publicly available real-world datasets, the fused pipeline achieves 96.1% detection accuracy, 3.2% false alarm rate, AUC-ROC: 0.981, and 142ms end-to-end latency on commodity CPU-class hardware at approximately $500-$780 USD total system cost. All code, model weights, and simulation datasets are publicly released at submission.

</details>


### [79] [Adapting Prithvi-EO for Fallow Detection for Food-Water Nexus: ViT-Adapter Necks and Parameter-Efficient Backbone tuning of Geospatial Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12218)
*Sk Muhammad Asif,Orhun Aydin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对 USDA 耕地数据层中休耕地分类精度低的问题，提出结合参数高效微调（LoRA 和混合 PEFT）与不同颈部网络（伪多尺度、Lite ViT-Adapter、Full ViT-Adapter）的休耕地检测管线。最佳配置（Lite ViT-Adapter + 单阶段检测头）在 Diou 损失下达到 mAP@50 0.9479，相比基线无适配器锚框方法提升 25.70%，证明轻量级空间先验融合和选择性骨干网络解冻能有效捕获休耕地局部模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 休耕地的空间分布对于优化粮食-水资源关系至关重要，但 USDA 耕地数据层中休耕地分类精度较低。现有方法存在两个问题：1) 地理空间基础模型 Prithvi-EO 的 Vision Transformer 骨干仅产生单一空间尺度特征，不适合目标检测头所需的多尺度特征；2) 通过缩放单步长 token 构建多尺度金字塔会牺牲空间异质性，且全骨干网络微调计算成本过高。

Method: 提出结合两种参数高效微调方案（LoRA 和混合 PEFT）与三种颈部设计（伪多尺度、Lite ViT-Adapter、Full ViT-Adapter）的休耕地检测管线。采用单阶段检测头，使用 Diou 损失函数进行中心感知定位。

Result: 最佳配置（Lite ViT-Adapter + 单阶段头）在 Diou 损失下达到 mAP@50 0.9479。ViT-Adapter 释放的单阶段检测在 LoRA 下比无适配器锚框方法提升 6.42%，最佳配置相比基线无适配器锚框方法提升 25.70%。

Conclusion: 轻量级空间先验融合和选择性骨干网络解冻使 Prithvi-EO 能更有效地捕获局部休耕地模式，优于依赖重塑单步长 ViT token 的方法。中心感知定位对不规则休耕地检测有效。

Abstract: Understanding spatial distribution of fallow land is important for optimizing the food-water (FW) nexus, given fallowing's role in crop rotation and water conservation. Fallow is a low accuracy class in USDA Cropland Data Layer (CDL). Geospatial foundation model (GFM), Prithvi-EO has shown strong transferability across computer vision tasks. However, its Vision Transformer (ViT) backbone produces features at a single spatial scale that are ill-suited for the multi-scale features required by object detection heads. Existing approaches synthesise multi-scale pyramids through scaling of single stride tokens, sacrificing spatial heterogeneity, and full backbone fine-tuning is computationally prohibitive for GFMs. We evaluate a fallow detection pipeline combining two parameter-efficient fine tuning (PEFT) schemes: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) and a hybrid PEFT, with three neck designs: pseudo multi-scale, Lite ViT-Adapter, and Full ViT-Adapter. Our best configuration, Lite ViT-Adapter with a one-stage head, achieves a mAP@50 of 0.9479 with the Diou loss, suggesting the effectiveness of center-aware localization for irregular fallow field detection. ViT-Adapter free one-stage detection under LoRA improves the adapter-free anchor-based approach by 6.42%, and the best configuration improves baseline adapter-free anchor-based approach by 25.70%. These results demonstrate that lightweight spatial prior fusion and selective backbone unfreezing enable Prithvi-EO to capture local fallow patterns more effectively, outperforming approaches that rely on reshaped single-stride ViT tokens.

</details>


### [80] [RankVR: Low-Rank Structure Perception and Value Recalibration for Robust Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11689)
*Jiale Huang,Zixu Li,Zhiheng Fu,Zhiwei Chen,Qinlei Huang,Yupeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RankVR框架，通过全局结构一致性感知（GSCP）和自适应语义值校准（ASVC）两个模块，解决组合图像检索中噪声三元组对应（NTC）导致的全局结构不一致和难样本判别不确定性问题，在FashionIQ和CIRR基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合图像检索（CIR）中，大规模数据集的噪声三元组对应（NTC）严重制约模型性能。现有去噪方法要么针对二元不匹配，要么依赖标量点估计，忽视了样本群体间的全局结构关联和训练过程中的动态值变化，导致次优结果。本文识别出两个关键未解决挑战：语义关联的全局结构不一致性和难样本判别不确定性。

Method: 提出RankVR框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 全局结构一致性感知（GSCP）模块，利用相关矩阵的有效秩来解耦干净样本与结构噪声，通过测量秩差异识别破坏宏观语义对称性的样本；2) 自适应语义值校准（ASVC）模块，结合训练潜力和可靠性动态量化每个三元组的语义值，区分高价值难干净样本，确保难样本的有效利用同时抑制逻辑冲突噪声。

Result: 在FashionIQ和CIRR两个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，RankVR显著优于现有最先进方法，验证了其在噪声环境下的卓越鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过全局结构一致性和动态值感知，RankVR有效解决了CIR任务中噪声三元组对应的两大挑战，为构建鲁棒的组合图像检索模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) constitutes a pivotal paradigm requiring models to perform joint reasoning on reference images and modification texts. However, the prevalence of Noisy Triplet Correspondence (NTC) in large-scale datasets severely constrains model performance. Existing denoising methods either target binary mismatches or rely on scalar-based point-wise estimation, neglecting rich global structural correlations among sample populations and dynamic value variations during training, thereby yielding suboptimal results. This paper identifies two critical unresolved challenges: Global Structural Inconsistency of Semantic Correlations and Hard Sample Discrimination Uncertainty. To address these, we propose RankVR, a framework designed to construct a robust CIR model via global structure consistency and dynamic value perception. Specifically, we introduce the Global Structure Consistency Perception (GSCP) module, which utilizes the Effective Rank of the Correlation Matrix to decouple clean samples from structural noise. By measuring rank difference, GSCP identifies samples disrupting macroscopic semantic symmetry. Furthermore, we develop the Adaptive Semantic Value Calibration (ASVC) module to distinguish high-value hard clean samples. By integrating training potential and reliability, it dynamically quantifies the semantic value of each triplet, ensuring effective utilization of hard samples while suppressing noise characterized by logical conflicts. Extensive experiments on the FashionIQ and CIRR benchmark datasets demonstrate that RankVR significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods, validating its superior robustness in noisy environments.

</details>


### [81] [MedCTA: A Benchmark for Clinical Tool Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11702)
*Tajamul Ashraf,Hyewon Jeong,Fida Mohammad Thoker,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedCTA是一个基于临床真实多模态输入（放射影像、病理切片、报告）的基准测试，用于评估医疗AI智能体在工具检索、证据获取与整合等多步骤临床任务中的表现。该基准包含107个临床任务及医师验证的可执行轨迹，支持过程感知评估。对18个开源和闭源多模态模型的测试表明，即使最先进的系统在多步骤临床工具使用中仍然脆弱，表现为协议失败、过早停止和工具调用错误，结论是强感知能力不能直接转化为可靠的临床智能体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估孤立的感知能力或单轮问答，无法揭示医疗AI智能体在规划、工具调用及执行可靠性方面的失败模式，缺少针对多步骤、工具驱动的临床决策过程的全面评估。

Method: 构建包含107个真实临床任务的基准MedCTA，每个任务配备医师验证的可执行轨迹，覆盖5种部署工具。支持工具选择、参数有效性、执行稳定性、轨迹忠实度和结果质量五个维度的过程感知评估，并对18个开源和闭源多模态模型进行系统评测。

Result: 前沿多模态模型在多步骤临床工具使用中表现脆弱：自主执行主要受限于协议失败、过早停止和错误工具调用；即使采用黄金标准的工具路由，性能提升仍然不完整。强感知能力并不能转化为可靠的临床智能体行为。

Conclusion: MedCTA为审计、诊断和推进可信赖的医疗AI智能体提供了严格的测试平台，揭示了现有模型在临床多步骤工具使用中的根本性缺陷。

Abstract: To make clinically grounded decisions, medical AI agents are expected to go beyond simple recognition and be capable of tool retrieval, evidence acquisition, and integration. Existing benchmarks largely evaluate isolated perception or single-turn question answering, and therefore provide limited visibility into failures of planning, tool recruitment, and rollout reliability. We introduce MedCTA, a benchmark for evaluating medical tool agents on clinician-validated, step-implicit tasks grounded in realistic multimodal clinical inputs, including radiology images, pathology slides, and reports. MedCTA comprises 107 real-world clinical tasks with clinician-verified executable trajectories over 5 deployed tools, and supports process-aware evaluation of tool selection, argument validity, execution stability, trajectory fidelity, and outcome quality. We benchmark 18 open- and closed-source multimodal models and find that even frontier systems remain brittle in multi-step clinical tool use: autonomous rollouts are dominated by protocol failures, premature stopping, and incorrect tool recruitment, while gold-standard tool routing yields large but still incomplete gains. These results show that strong backbone perception does not translate into reliable agentic behavior in clinical settings. MedCTA provides a rigorous testbed for auditing, diagnosing, and advancing trustworthy medical AI agents. The dataset and evaluation suite are available at https://ivul-kaust.github.io/MedCTA/

</details>


### [82] [ERN-Net : Evolving Reason Node-Net for Document Binarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11710)
*Hsin-Jui Pan,Sheng-Wei Chan,Jen-Shiung Chiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ERN-Net通过演化推理节点和跨尺度推理机制，有效增强文档图像中退化敏感区域（如淡笔迹、断字、噪点背景）的二值化效果。实验表明，ConvNeXt-Tiny在精度与内存之间取得最佳平衡，DIBCO预训练仅增加约1.5小时即可提升性能，在低数据和低内存场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档图像二值化方法对退化敏感区域（如淡笔迹、断字符、噪声背景）处理效果不佳，且存在计算资源消耗大、低数据场景下泛化能力弱的问题。

Method: 提出ERN-Net（演化推理节点网络），包含演化推理节点和跨尺度推理机制以增强退化敏感区域；对比ResNet-101、ConvNeXt-Tiny和ConvNeXt-Base三种骨干网络；采用DIBCO数据集进行预训练。

Result: ConvNeXt-Tiny在精度与内存使用间取得最佳平衡；DIBCO预训练可在不增加模型内存消耗的情况下提升二值化性能，仅需额外约1.5小时训练；在DIBCO基准测试上，ERN-Net在低数据和低内存设置下表现有效。

Conclusion: ERN-Net能有效处理退化敏感区域的二值化问题，适合资源受限和训练数据有限的场景，ConvNeXt-Tiny作为骨干网络具有最佳性价比。

Abstract: This paper presents ERN-Net, an Evolving Reason Node-Net for efficient document image binarization. ERN-Net enhances degradation-sensitive regions, such as faint strokes, broken characters, and noisy backgrounds, through evolving reason nodes and multi-scale reasoning. We further compare ResNet-101, ConvNeXt-Tiny, and ConvNeXt-Base, and find that ConvNeXt-Tiny provides the best practical trade-off between accuracy and memory usage. In addition, DIBCO-based pretraining improves binarization performance without increasing model memory consumption, requiring only about 1.5 additional training hours. Experiments on DIBCO-style benchmarks show that ERN-Net is effective under low-data and low-memory settings.

</details>


### [83] [Ouroboros-Spatial: Closing the Data-Model Loop for Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11719)
*Enhan Zhao,Wei Wu,Yuanrui Zhang,Xueliang Zhao,Di He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Ouroboros-Spatial自进化训练框架，让MLLM同时扮演出题者和解题者，通过闭环反馈机制使训练分布与模型能力共同演化，在6个空间推理基准上显著提升Qwen3-VL-4B/8B性能，且训练样本量比大规模静态数据集少一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM空间推理方法依赖大规模静态数据集，所有训练样本被统一对待，不考虑模型不断变化的能力，导致数据利用率低下——训练容量常浪费在当前阶段对模型过于简单或过于困难的样本上。

Method: 提出Ouroboros-Spatial自进化训练框架：(1) 冻结的出题者从3D场景元数据和原始视频帧生成空间QA对及可执行代码用于获取可靠GT；(2) 可学习的解题者在接受的样本上微调，其逐样本预测置信度作为难度信号；(3) 该难度信号反馈给下一轮出题者，引导其生成更匹配解题者当前能力的问题。形成闭环设计，训练分布与模型能力共同演化。

Result: 在6个空间推理基准上，Ouroboros-Spatial显著提升Qwen3-VL-4B和Qwen3-VL-8B，训练样本量比近期大规模精选数据集少一个数量级。在VSI-Bench上，4B和8B模型分别获得9.9和6.8个绝对增益点，均超越多个强开源和专有基线。

Conclusion: 闭环自进化训练框架能有效解决空间推理中的数据效率问题，通过让训练分布随模型能力动态调整，大幅减少冗余简单样本和模糊无信息样本，实现小样本下的显著性能提升。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning remains a persistent challenge for multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Existing approaches largely rely on large-scale, statically curated datasets, where all training samples are treated uniformly regardless of the model's evolving capabilities. This static paradigm is inherently data-inefficient: training capacity is often spent on samples that are either trivial or overly difficult for the model at its current stage. To address this limitation, we propose Ouroboros-Spatial, a self-evolving training framework in which the model plays dual roles as a proposer and a solver. In each iteration, a frozen proposer generates spatial question-answer (QA) pairs from 3D scene metadata and raw video frames, together with executable code for deriving reliable ground truth. A learnable solver is then fine-tuned on the accepted samples, and its per-sample prediction confidence is used as a difficulty signal. This signal is fed back to the proposer in the next iteration, guiding it to generate questions better matched to the solver's current capabilities. Through this closed-loop design, the training distribution co-evolves with model ability, reducing redundant trivial examples while filtering out ambiguous or uninformative samples with limited learning value. Across six spatial reasoning benchmarks, Ouroboros-Spatial substantially improves Qwen3-VL-4B and Qwen3-VL-8B while using an order of magnitude fewer training examples than recent large-scale curated datasets. On VSI-Bench, it yields absolute gains of 9.9 and 6.8 points for the 4B and 8B models, respectively, enabling both to outperform a wide range of strong open-source and proprietary baselines.

</details>


### [84] [Multi-View In-Cabin Monitoring System for Public Transport Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11739)
*Evgeny Gorelik,Kenny Dean Karrow,Fikret Sivrikaya,Sahin Albayrak,Christian Baumann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向公共交通的多视图车内监控数据集，包含来自四个内视摄像头和旋转LiDAR的同步RGB与深度图像，共9136个带标注样本，并提供3D人体姿态估计和定向3D边界框的校准与伪标注流程，以及nuScenes格式转换和多视图3D检测模型基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共交通车内监控缺乏多视图、多模态的标准化数据集，现有数据集多局限于自动驾驶场景或单视图车内监控，无法充分支持车内3D感知研究。

Method: 在数字化、部分自动化的德国城市公交车上部署四个内视摄像头和旋转LiDAR，采集同步RGB-D数据；设计校准与伪标注流程生成3D人体姿态和定向3D边界框；提供nuScenes格式转换工具，并基于Lift-Splat-Shoot和BEVFusion等模型建立基准。

Result: 构建了包含9136个同步样本的多视图车内数据集，支持多视图3D检测模型的比较评估与小规模训练。

Conclusion: 该数据集和工具链为公共交通车内3D感知研究提供了标准化基准，填补了多视图车内监控数据的空白，可推动相关模型在真实公交场景中的落地应用。

Abstract: We introduce a multi-view in-cabin monitoring dataset for public transportation with synchronized RGB and depth images from four inward-facing cameras and a rotating LiDAR covering the vehicle interior of a digitalized and partly automated German city bus. The dataset contains 9.136 synchronized samples with annotations and is accompanied by a calibration and pseudo-labeling pipeline that generates 3D human pose estimates and oriented 3D bounding boxes for occupants. We further provide a nuScenes-format conversion and benchmark representative multi-view 3D detection models (e.g., Lift-Splat-Shoot and BEVFusion), supporting comparative evaluation and small-scale training of multi-view in-cabin perception models. The dataset and tools are available at https://github.com/EvgenyGorelik/multiview_incabin_dataset.

</details>


### [85] [Natural-Language Temporal Grounding in Hour-Long Videos is a Search Problem: A Benchmark and Empirical Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12300)
*Sukmin Seo,Geewook Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出小时级视频时间定位的瓶颈在于搜索而非识别，发布首个开放域小时级基准ExtremeWhenBench（194视频、2273查询，平均75.7分钟，最长9小时），发现所有视频大模型在长视频上崩溃，而帧级检索基线表现更优；85%失败归因于搜索问题，检索-定位混合方法比单一视频大模型提升6.7倍，类似开放域QA中的检索-阅读范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间定位研究局限于短视频，小时级长视频的自然语言定位动态尚未被探索。作者假设小时级场景下，核心瓶颈是搜索而非识别——视频大模型难以在长视频中定位相关区域，而非无法识别附近事件。

Method: 发布ExtremeWhenBench基准（194视频、2273查询，平均75.7分钟，最长9小时，开放域查询分布）；对比多种视频大模型与帧级检索基线；建立失败分类法；提出检索-定位混合方法（retrieve-then-ground）。

Result: 所有开放视频大模型在小时级任务上性能崩溃；帧级检索基线优于视频大模型；85%失败归因于搜索问题；检索-定位混合方法比单一视频大模型提升6.7倍。

Conclusion: 小时级时间定位的约束是搜索而非识别；检索-定位混合范式（类似开放域QA的检索-阅读范式）是应对长视频定位的有效策略。

Abstract: Temporal grounding--returning the interval $[t_s, t_e]$ for a natural-language query over a video--is the language interface to long-form video, yet has been studied on short videos; the dynamics of hour-scale natural-language grounding remain underexplored. We take the position that at hour-scale, the binding constraint is search, not recognition: Video-LLMs are bottlenecked not by localizing a nearby event, but--given a natural-language query--by searching for the relevant region of a long video. To test this, we release ExtremeWhenBench, the first open hour-scale grounding benchmark (2,273 queries over 194 videos, mean 75.7 min, max 9 hr) with an open-form query distribution. Every open Video-LLM collapses while a frame-level retrieval baseline outperforms them; a failure taxonomy attributes 85% of failures to search; and a retrieve-then-ground hybrid recovers 6.7x over the monolithic Video-LLM--mirroring retrieve-then-read in open-domain QA.

</details>


### [86] [UniReason-Med: A Shared Grounded Reasoning Interface for 2D-to-3D Transfer in Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11740)
*Mengzhuo Chen,Yan Shu,Chi Liu,Hongming Piao,Xidong Wang,Derek Li,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UniReason-Med框架，利用2D医学图像的接地推理监督来提升3D医学VQA能力，通过共享的接地推理接口（边界框语法、区域标记注入、共同接地推理策略）统一处理2D图像和3D体积数据，结合220K指令微调数据集UniMed-CoT和监督微调+强化学习训练，实验表明联合2D+3D接地监督显著提升3D推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究能否利用丰富的2D医学图像中的接地推理监督来提升3D医学视觉问答的性能，弥合2D与3D医学图像推理之间的差距。

Method: 1) 构建UniReason-Med单检查点框架，通过共享边界框语法、区域标记注入和共同接地推理策略统一处理2D图像和切片序列化的3D体积；2) 构建UniMed-CoT数据集（220K指令微调样本，含170K 2D和50K 3D样本）；3) 采用监督微调+结果级强化学习训练，无需IoU/Dice定位奖励。

Result: 联合2D+3D接地监督显著优于仅3D训练；接地和区域标记注入对2D和3D任务均有持续收益；共同接地推理接口能够将推理结构从2D图像迁移到切片序列化的体积医学理解。

Conclusion: 共享的接地推理接口可以有效将2D图像的推理结构迁移至3D体积医学理解，验证了跨模态推理迁移的可行性。

Abstract: We study whether grounded reasoning supervision from abundant 2D medical images can improve 3D medical VQA when both input types are aligned through a common reasoning interface. We introduce UniReason-Med, a single-checkpoint framework that processes either a 2D image or a slice-serialized 3D volume at inference time, generating interleaved textual reasoning and localized visual evidence through shared box syntax, region-token injection, and a common grounded reasoning policy. To train this interface, we construct UniMed-CoT, a 220K instruction-tuning dataset with interleaved textual reasoning and grounded visual evidence, including 170K 2D and 50K 3D samples. Through supervised fine-tuning followed by outcome-level reinforcement learning, UniReason-Med learns to generate grounded reasoning traces without IoU/Dice-based localization rewards during RL. Data-mixture and component ablations show that joint 2D+3D grounded supervision substantially improves 3D reasoning over 3D-only training, while grounding and region-token injection consistently benefit both 2D and 3D tasks. These results suggest that a shared grounded reasoning interface can transfer reasoning structure from 2D images to slice-serialized volumetric medical understanding. The code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/IQuestLab/unireason-med.

</details>


### [87] [Atlas H&E-TME: Scalable AI-Based Tissue Profiling at Expert Pathologist-Level Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12346)
*Kai Standvoss,Miriam Hägele,Rosemarie Krupar,Julika Ribbat-Idel,Jennifer Altschüler,Gerrit Erdmann,Hans Pinckaers,Evelyn Ramberger,Madleen Drinkwitz,Ádám Nárai,Alexander Möllers,Katja Lingelbach,Sebastian Kons,Lukas Hönig,Recepcan Adigüzel,Joana Baião,Alberto Megina Gonzalo,Marius Teodorescu,Marie-Lisa Eich,Paolo Chetta,Shakil Merchant,Verena Aumiller,Simon Schallenberg,Andrew Norgan,Klaus-Robert Müller,Lukas Ruff,Maximilian Alber,Frederick Klauschen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Atlas H&E-TME 是一个基于 Atlas 病理基础模型族的 AI 系统，能够以细胞级分辨率从 H&E 全切片图像中预测组织质量、组织区域和细胞类型标签，每张切片输出超过 4,500 个定量指标。系统采用双重验证框架：基于 IHC 指导的多病理学家共识协议进行深度验证，以及在 200,000+ 条高置信度病理学家标注（覆盖 8 种癌症类型、1,500+ 病例、25+ 数据源、8+ 扫描仪型号）上进行广度验证。结果表明 Atlas H&E-TME 在 H&E-only 条件下达到或超越病理学家水平，实现了 H&E 切片的可扩展定量分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: H&E 染色是组织病理学的基石，但对其全切片图像进行可扩展的定量分析仍然是计算病理学的核心挑战。现有方法受限于 H&E-only 标注的形态模糊性以及免疫组化（IHC）等更具信息量的参考模态的可扩展性不足。

Method: 构建基于 Atlas 病理基础模型族的 AI 系统 Atlas H&E-TME；提出双重验证框架：1）深度验证——IHC 指导的多病理学家共识协议，提高标注者间一致性作为分子层面参考标准；2）广度验证——在超过 200,000 条高置信度 H&E-only 病理学家标注上评测，覆盖 8 种癌症类型、1,500+ 病例、25+ 数据源及 8+ 扫描仪型号。

Result: Atlas H&E-TME 在 IHC 指导的共识参考标准上，匹配或超越了病理学家在 H&E-only 条件下的表现，并在广泛的形态学和技术范围内展现出一致且稳健的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Atlas H&E-TME 将 H&E 切片——病理学中最普遍的数据——转化为可扩展的、定量的肿瘤及肿瘤微环境分析窗口，为转化和临床研究中下一代组织生物标志物奠定了基础。

Abstract: Hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) staining is the cornerstone of histopathology, yet scalable, quantitative analysis of H&E whole-slide images (WSIs) remains a central challenge in computational pathology. We present Atlas H&E-TME, an AI-based system built on the Atlas family of pathology foundation models that predicts tissue quality, tissue region, and cell type labels across multiple cancer types, yielding over 4,500 quantitative readouts per slide at cell-level resolution. A key challenge to validating such systems is overcoming morphological ambiguity inherent to H&E-only ground truth and the limited scalability of more informed references drawing on modalities such as immunohistochemistry (IHC). We address this with a dual validation framework combining biologically grounded depth with technical and morphological breadth. For depth, we propose an IHC-informed multi-pathologist consensus protocol that substantially improves inter-rater agreement over conventional H&E-only annotation. This yields a molecularly grounded reference against which we compare Atlas H&E-TME and pathologists working from H&E alone. For breadth, we benchmark Atlas H&E-TME on over 200,000 high-confidence H&E-only pathologist annotations across 1,500+ cases spanning eight cancer types and their most common metastatic sites, with subtypes covering >90% of clinical cases per cancer type, drawn from 25+ sources and 8+ scanner models. Benchmarked against the IHC-informed consensus, Atlas H&E-TME matches or exceeds pathologist H&E-only performance and generalizes consistently and robustly across this broad morphological and technical scope. In doing so, Atlas H&E-TME turns the H&E slide -- the most ubiquitous data in pathology -- into a scalable, quantitative window into the tumor and its microenvironment, laying a foundation for the next generation of tissue-based biomarkers in translational and clinical research.

</details>


### [88] [Battery detection of XRay images using transfer learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11779)
*Nermeen Abou Baker,David Rohrschneider,Uwe Handmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文证明了迁移学习在电池检测与分类中的应用潜力，使用预训练的YOLOv5m模型检测图像中是否包含电池、定位电池位置，并区分棱柱形、软包和圆柱形三种锂离子电池。相比直接使用预训练权重，该方法在22ms推理时间内将检测精度从89%提升至94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电池检测与分选的需求在许多应用场景中急剧增加，需要高效准确的自动化检测方法。

Method: 采用迁移学习策略，首先在大规模电子设备数据集上预训练YOLOv5m模型，然后将训练好的权重迁移用于电池检测与分类任务，识别三种锂离子电池（棱柱形、软包、圆柱形）。

Result: 电池检测精度达到94%，比直接使用预训练的YOLOv5m权重高出5%，推理时间仅22ms。

Conclusion: 迁移学习能够有效提升电池检测与分类的性能，在保持高速度的同时显著提高检测精度。

Abstract: The need for detecting and sorting batteries is drastically increasing for many applications. This study proves the potential of transfer learning in predicting whether the image contains a battery or not, the location and identifying three types of batteries, namely: prismatic, pouch, and cylindrical Lithium-Ion Batteries (LIB). Particularly, it focuses on the transfer learning method in two applications: Training a large-scale dataset to detect electronic devices using a pre-trained YOLOv5m, then using these latter trained weights to detect and classify the batteries. The precision of battery detection achieves 94%, which outperforms the pretrained YOLOv5m weights with 5%, in 22 ms inference time.

</details>


### [89] [Illumination-Robust Camera-Based Heart-Rate Estimation for Physiological Sensing in Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12378)
*Zhi Wei Xu,Torbjörn E. M. Nordling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向机器人视觉系统的端到端时空Transformer框架，用于在光照变化条件下进行远程心率估计。该框架整合了PRNet三维人脸对齐、剪辑级光照增强、残差时序标准化模块以及混合时频监督。通过Soft-Shifted Pearson波形损失和谱KL散度损失的组合训练，在β=5时达到最优性能：HR MAE为0.79 bpm，HR相关系数为0.982，相比PhysFormer基线，MAE降低93.6%，相关系数从0.088提升至0.982。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理感知对于与服务、社交和辅助机器人互动至关重要，远程光电容积描记术(rPPG)能从RGB摄像头实现非接触式心率估计，是机器人视觉系统有前景的感知方式。然而，光照变化是制约其稳健部署的主要障碍。

Method: 提出了端到端时空Transformer框架，包含PRNet三维人脸对齐、剪辑级光照增强、残差时序标准化模块(Residual Temporal Standardization Module)以及受控混合时频监督。训练目标结合了Soft-Shifted Pearson波形损失与谱KL散度损失，通过权重β调节频域心率引导的贡献。

Result: 在覆盖三种光照水平的静态全级别混合协议上，β=5时取得最优结果：最佳运行HR MAE为0.79 bpm，HR相关系数为0.982。与PhysFormer基线相比，HR MAE降低93.6%，HR相关系数从0.088提升至0.982。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架在光照变化条件下实现了高精度、高相关性的远程心率估计，性能远超基线方法，具备在实际机器人部署中的可用性。

Abstract: Physiological awareness is important for service, social, and assistive robots that interact with humans in everyday environments. Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) enables non-contact heart-rate (HR) estimation from an RGB camera, making it a promising sensing modality for robot-mounted vision systems. However, illumination variation remains a major barrier to robust deployment. This paper presents an end-to-end spatial-temporal transformer framework for remote HR estimation on a new dataset with varied illumination. Our estimator integrates PRNet-based 3D face alignment, clip-level illumination augmentation, the Residual Temporal Standardization Module, and controlled hybrid temporal-frequency supervision. The training objective combines a Soft-Shifted Pearson waveform loss with a spectral Kullback-Leibler divergence loss, where a tuned weight ($\mathbfβ$) controls the contribution of frequency-domain heart-rate guidance. Experiments on a static all-level mix protocol covering three illumination levels show that $\mathbfβ=5$ provides the strongest result among the tested beta settings, achieving a best-run HR mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.79 bpm and an HR correlation of 0.982. Compared with the PhysFormer baseline evaluated on our dataset, our estimator reduces HR MAE by 93.6 %, while increasing HR correlation from 0.088 to 0.982, making it usable when illumination varies.

</details>


### [90] [Seeing What Matters: Perceptual Wrapper with Common Randomness for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11782)
*He-Bi Yang,Jing-Zhong Chen,Yen-Kuan Ho,Sang NguyenQuang,Fan-Yi Hsu,Yun-Yu Lee,Jui-Chiu Chiang,Wen-Hsiao Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对3DGS在合成高频纹理时的不足（尤其在内存受限和率失真优化管道中），提出了一种轻量级2D感知包装器，通过条件化伪随机高斯噪声的合成网络，在Wasserstein距离监督下匹配局部特征统计，以内容感知和视角相关方式增强渲染输出的感知质量，显著降低模型文件大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在实时渲染中表现出色，但在合成高频纹理方面存在困难，这一问题在内存受限和率失真优化（RDO）管道中尤为严重。现有方法在感知质量与压缩效率之间存在权衡。

Method: 提出一种即插即用的2D感知包装器，包含：1) 轻量级合成网络，以伪随机高斯噪声为条件生成感知上合理的纹理；2) 使用Wasserstein距离（Wasserstein Distortion）作为监督信号，匹配局部特征统计而非严格约束逐像素重建损失，从而缓解模糊问题；3) 以内容感知和视角相关的方式增强渲染输出。

Result: 该方法在普通、内存受限和RDO优化的3DGS方法上均显著提升了感知质量，同时在主观和客观实验中优于现有基线，并实现了文件或模型尺寸的大幅缩减。

Conclusion: 所提的2D感知包装器是一种通用、即插即用的增强模块，能有效缓解3DGS在高频纹理合成上的局限性，在感知质量与压缩效率之间取得更优平衡。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves impressive real-time rendering, it frequently struggles to synthesize high-frequency textures, a limitation heavily exacerbated in memory-constrained and rate-distortion-optimized (RDO) pipelines. To address this, we propose a versatile 2D perceptual wrapper that enhances the rendered outputs of existing 3DGS representations in a content- and view-dependent manner. Our method leverages a lightweight synthesis network conditioned on pseudo-random Gaussian noise to synthesize perceptually plausible textures. Supervised by Wasserstein Distortion, the network learns to match local feature statistics rather than strictly enforcing pixel-wise reconstruction fidelity, effectively mitigating the blurriness inherent in standard frameworks. We demonstrate the broad applicability of our plug-and-play approach across vanilla, memory-constrained, and RDO 3DGS methods. Comprehensive subjective and objective experiments confirm that our method significantly improves over existing baselines, yielding superior perceptual quality at sharply reduced file or model sizes.

</details>


### [91] [A Comprehensive Ecosystem for Open-Domain Customized Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11783)
*Jingxu Zhang,Yuqian Hong,Daneul Kim,Kai Qiu,Qi Dai,Jianmin Bao,Yifan Yang,Xiaoyan Sun,Chong Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个百万级身份保持视频生成数据集PexelsCustom-1M（含100万<身份,文本,视频>三元组，覆盖8000+类别），以及轻量级框架CustoMDiT（仅增加8%可学习参数即可适配多模态扩散Transformer），并构建了覆盖1000+类别的OpenCustom基准，显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放域定制化视频生成受限于缺乏大规模、带标注的、捕捉多样身份特定属性的数据集。

Method: 1) 构建PexelsCustom-1M百万级数据集；2) 提出CustoMDiT参数高效框架，仅用8%额外参数适配预训练多模态扩散Transformer；3) 通过ImageNet和MS-COCO跨数据集知识融合构建OpenCustom基准。

Result: CustoMDiT在身份保持视频生成任务上超越先前最先进方法；OpenCustom基准覆盖1000+类别，远超DreamBooth的100类。

Conclusion: 大规模数据集和轻量级框架有效推动了开放域定制化视频生成；将开源整个生态系统以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Recent progress in video generation has shown impressive visual synthesis capabilities. However, open-domain customized video generation remains limited by the lack of large-scale, annotated datasets capturing diverse identity-specific attributes. To address this, we introduce PexelsCustom-1M, the first publicly available million-scale dataset for identity-preserving video generation, containing one million curated <identity, text, video> triplets across 8,000+ categories. Leveraging this, we propose CustoMDiT, a parameter-efficient framework that adapts a pretrained multimodal Diffusion Transformer into a customized video generator with only 8% additional learnable parameters. Our method surpasses prior state-of-the-art. However, benchmarks such as DreamBooth cover only 100 classes, which is insufficient for real-world applications. To overcome this, we construct OpenCustom, a new benchmark with 1,000+ categories, created via cross-dataset knowledge fusion from ImageNet and MS-COCO. Extensive experiments confirm the advantages of both our dataset and model. We will open-source the entire ecosystem--including dataset, pipeline, benchmark, and implementations--to support further research.

</details>


### [92] [Reroute, Don't Remove: Recoverable Visual Token Routing for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12412)
*Cheng-Yu Yang,Shao-Yuan Lo,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Reroute 提出一种可恢复的路由机制替代不可逆的视觉 token 剪枝，通过让低分 token 暂缓当前解码阶段并在下一阶段重新参与候选，在不增加理论计算量和 KV-cache 预算的前提下，提升了 VLM 在激进 token 缩减下的定位能力，同时保持通用 VQA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉 token 缩减方法（如 FastV、PDrop）采用“评分-移除”的不可逆剪枝范式，但视觉 token 的重要性随解码器深度动态变化，早期被移除的低分 token 可能在后几层变得关键，尤其对于需要精细化定位（grounding）的任务，这种不可逆操作会导致信息永久丢失。

Method: 提出 Reroute，一种无需训练的即插即用方法。在每个路由阶段（stage），被选中的视觉 token 正常通过解码器块，而暂缓的 token 则绕过当前块，在下一路由决策时重新进入候选池。该方法复用原有的注意力分数排序规则和阶段级调度策略，不改变理论 TFLOPs 和 KV-cache 预算类。

Result: 在 LLaVA-1.5 和 Qwen 骨干上，对 FastV、PDrop、Nüwa 变体进行增强，在激进 token 缩减条件下，Reroute 显著提升了定位（grounding）性能，同时保持通用 VQA 性能不下降。

Conclusion: VLM 的 token 缩减不应仅视为不可逆剪枝，而应看作可恢复的路由问题。Reroute 以零额外训练开销验证了这一观点，为更灵活的视觉 token 管理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) project images into hundreds to thousands of visual tokens, making decoder inference expensive in both attention computation and KV-cache memory. Existing visual-token reduction methods largely follow a rank-and-remove paradigm: they score visual tokens, keep a compact subset, and permanently discard the rest. We show that this irreversible action is fragile because visual-token importance changes across decoder depth; tokens ranked low at one stage may become relevant in later layers, especially for grounding-sensitive queries. We propose Reroute, a training-free plug-in that replaces removal with recoverable routing. At each routing stage, selected vision tokens pass through decoder blocks, while deferred tokens bypass the stage and re-enter the candidate pool at the next routing decision. Reroute reuses existing attention-score ranking rules and stage-wise schedules, preserving the theoretical TFLOPs and KV-cache budget class of the pruning method it augments. Across FastV, PDrop, and Nüwa variants on LLaVA-1.5 and Qwen backbones, reroute improves grounding under aggressive token reduction while maintaining general VQA performance. These results suggest that VLM token reduction should not be viewed only as irreversible pruning, but also as recoverable routing. The code can be found here: https://github.com/elmma/mllm-reroute/

</details>


### [93] [Plan-and-Verify Video Reward Reasoning with Spatio-Temporal Scene Graph Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11838)
*Hyomin Kim,Junghye Kim,Joanie Hayoun Chung,Yoonjin Oh,Kyungjae Lee,Sungbin Lim,Sungwoong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SG-PVR 提出了一种基于时空场景图的计划-验证视频奖励模型，通过将提示分解为原子声明并利用结构化场景图进行显式视觉证据推理，解决了文本到视频生成中细粒度语义对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于推理的奖励模型在细粒度语义对齐上表现不佳，原因在于：1) 未能系统性地验证提示中描述的每个条件；2) 自由形式的推理中支撑每个判断的视觉证据是隐式的，缺乏结构化支撑。

Method: 提出 SG-PVR：1) 验证计划：将提示分解为原子声明，确保每个要求都被检查；2) 从视频中提取时空场景图，编码实体、属性及时间关联关系，作为推理过程中持久的结构化视觉参考；3) 每个声明同时对照视频和场景图进行验证，将判断锚定于显式视觉证据。

Result: 在语义对齐（包括细粒度时间语义）上取得了强性能；作为测试时重排序器，进一步增强了 T2V 生成中的组合对齐能力。

Conclusion: SG-PVR 通过计划-验证机制和时空场景图的结构化视觉参考，有效提升了视频奖励模型在细粒度语义对齐上的表现，并能作为重排序器改善文本到视频生成的组合对齐。

Abstract: Reward models for text-to-video (T2V) generation guide post-training but often fail at fine-grained semantic alignment. We trace this to two structural weaknesses in existing reasoning-based reward models: they do not systematically verify every condition described in the prompt, and the visual evidence supporting each judgment remains implicit in their free-form reasoning. We propose SG-PVR, a video reward model that addresses these limitations through plan-and-verify reasoning grounded in spatio-temporal scene graphs. The verification plan decomposes the prompt into atomic claims, ensuring every requirement is checked. The spatio-temporal scene graph, encoding entities, attributes, and temporally-grounded relations, is extracted from the video and maintained as a persistent structured visual reference throughout reasoning. Each claim is verified against both the video and the scene graph, anchoring judgments in explicit visual evidence. SG-PVR achieves strong performance on semantic alignment, including fine-grained temporal semantics. As a test-time reranker, it further enhances compositional alignment in T2V generation.

</details>


### [94] [Scene-Adaptive Nonlinear Tone Curves for Pseudo Ground-Truth Generation in Low-Light 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11841)
*Mingzhe Lyu,Jinqiang Cui,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对低光多视图图像中的噪声、弱结构细节和动态范围压缩问题，本文提出一种场景自适应的非线性色调曲线框架，替代传统的均匀线性增益伪GT方法，通过两种互补曲线（ASE和AP3）显著提升低光新颖视图合成的质量，在三个基准测试21个场景中PSNR提升最高达+4.34 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS方法在低光场景下使用均匀线性增益生成伪GT图像，这会导致亮部裁剪和暗部增强不足，限制了重建质量。非线性色调映射虽已在2D低光增强中成熟应用，但尚未被探索用于3D重建中的伪GT生成。

Method: 提出场景自适应非线性色调曲线框架，包含：1）基于百分位数的归一化实现场景无关的曲线应用；2）场景自适应偏移用于自动黑电平调整；3）两种互补曲线：Adaptive SoftExp (ASE) 有界指数曲线 和 Adaptive Poly3 (AP3) 数据驱动三次多项式。该模块仅改变伪GT计算，保持3DGS骨干网络不变。

Result: 在三个基准测试（含21个场景）上，两种曲线均一致优于线性基线，LOM数据集PSNR提升最高达+4.34 dB，RealX3D提升最高达+3.25 dB。两种曲线数学形式不同但性能相似，表明改进是曲线无关的。

Conclusion: 非线性色调曲线可有效替代线性增益用于低光3DGS的伪GT生成，场景自适应策略能显著提升重建质量，且该改进具有曲线无关性，为未来低光3D重建提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Low-light novel view synthesis is challenging because dark multi-view images contain noise, weak structural detail, and compressed dynamic range. Recent 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) methods address these challenges by generating pseudo ground-truth (pseudo-GT) images as supervision targets when paired normal-light references are unavailable. Existing pseudo-GT methods apply a uniform linear gain to all pixels, which clips bright regions while providing insufficient enhancement in dark regions, limiting reconstruction quality. We observe that nonlinear tone mappings, long established in 2D low-light enhancement, have not been explored for pseudo-GT generation in 3D reconstruction. Accordingly, we propose a scene-adaptive nonlinear tone-curve framework that replaces linear pseudo-GT with nonlinear alternatives. The framework introduces percentile-based normalisation for scene-agnostic curve application, a scene-adaptive offset for automatic black-level adjustment, and two complementary curves: Adaptive SoftExp (ASE), a bounded exponential curve, and Adaptive Poly3 (AP3), a data-driven cubic polynomial. The module changes only the pseudo-GT computation and leaves the 3DGS backbone unchanged. Experiments on three benchmarks covering 21 scenes show that both curves consistently outperform the linear baseline with PSNR improvements up to +4.34 dB on LOM and +3.25 dB on RealX3D. Both curves achieve similar performance despite their different mathematical forms, suggesting the improvement is curve-agnostic. Code is available at https://github.com/lvmingzhe/adaptiveToneCurve

</details>


### [95] [SheafStain: Sheaf-Theoretic Schrödinger Bridge for Spatially and Biologically Coherent Virtual Staining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11846)
*Hyeongyeol Lim,Hongjun Yoon,Eunjin Jang,Daeky Jeong,Won June Cho,Hwamin Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SheafStain方法，将视觉基础模型（VFM）特征重新解释为类似层（sheaf）的截面，通过薛定谔桥框架整合类别令牌和区块令牌，解决虚拟染色中因上下文污染导致的拼接伪影问题。该方法在HER2、ER、PR和Ki-67四种生物标志物上优于六种现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前虚拟染色方法在全切片图像（WSI）上进行逐块推理时，无法保持空间连续性，产生拼接伪影；病理视觉基础模型（VFM）的自注意力机制导致同一物理区域因全局上下文变化而产生不一致的嵌入表示，即"上下文污染"问题。

Method: 将VFM特征的形式化问题定义为层论（sheaf theory）中预层（presheaf）违反胶合公理的问题；提出SheafStain方法，将类别令牌和区块令牌作为类层截面整合到薛定谔桥（Schrödinger Bridge）框架中；利用在H&E和IHC上共同预训练的主干网络生成非退化的跨染色茎（stalks），使单一VFM特征空间同时监督输入条件和输出染色对齐。

Result: 在HER2、ER、PR和Ki-67四种生物标志物上，以256×256平移推理并在拼接后的1024×1024输出上评估，SheafStain优于六种现有方法，同时减轻了拼接边界伪影。

Conclusion: SheafStain通过将VFM特征空间重新解释为类层结构，有效解决了虚拟染色中的上下文污染和拼接伪影问题，提升了多标志物定量分析的准确性和空间一致性。

Abstract: Current virtual staining approaches offer the potential for time- and cost-efficient biomarker quantification in cancer diagnostics and prognostics. However, patch-wise inference for gigapixel whole slide images (WSIs) fails to maintain spatial continuity, yielding artifacts that cause catastrophic mismatches with ground-truth images. Although pathology Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) offer rich representations, their self-attention causes varying global contexts to produce inconsistent embeddings for the same physical region. We formalize and validate this ``context contamination'' as a sheaf-theoretic problem where these embeddings form a presheaf that violates the gluing axiom. To address this, we propose SheafStain, a new approach that reinterprets VFM features as sheaf-like sections for spatially and biologically coherent virtual staining. Specifically, SheafStain integrates class and patch tokens into a Schrödinger Bridge framework as sheaf-like sections. While the class token anchors biological consistency, patch tokens form a per-position spatial map. A backbone co-pretrained on Hematoxylin \& Eosin (H\&E) and Immunohistochemistry (IHC) yields non-degenerate cross-stain stalks, so a single VFM feature space supervises both input conditioning and output stain alignment. Departing from prior work that evaluates on isolated $256 \times 256$ patches and either random-crops or resizes the $1024 \times 1024$ ground truth, we translate at $256 \times 256$ and evaluate on the stitched $1024 \times 1024$ outputs across HER2, ER, PR, and Ki-67. SheafStain demonstrates promising results against six prior methods while mitigating patch-boundary stitching artifacts. Code will soon be released.

</details>


### [96] [SG2Loc: Sequential Visual Localization on 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11880)
*Nicole Damblon,Olga Vysotska,Federico Tombari,Marc Pollefeys,Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于3D场景图的轻量级序贯视觉定位方法，利用紧凑的场景图（包含物体粗网格和空间关系）和语义特征匹配，在粒子滤波框架下实现高效定位，大幅减少存储需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉定位方法需要存储大量图像数据库或点云，存储开销大；复杂室内环境中序贯定位对自主智能体至关重要，现有方法难以兼顾轻量化和性能。

Method: 使用3D场景图表示环境（节点为带粗网格的物体，边为空间关系）；提取输入图像的逐补丁语义特征；在粒子滤波框架中，每个粒子（相机位姿）将场景图中的粗网格投影到图像，根据可见性分配物体身份，通过语义特征相似度计算粒子权重，序贯融合多帧图像优化位姿估计。

Result: 在真实数据集上，该方法在保持定位性能的同时显著降低了存储开销。

Conclusion: 基于紧凑场景图和高效语义匹配的轻量级方法可有效解决复杂室内场景的序贯视觉定位问题，兼顾存储效率与定位精度。

Abstract: Visual localization in complex indoor environments remains a critical challenge for robotics and AR applications. Sequential localization, where pose estimates are refined over time, is important for autonomous agents. However, traditional methods often require storing extensive image databases or point clouds, leading to significant overhead. This paper introduces a novel, lightweight approach to sequential visual localization using 3D scene graphs. Our method represents the environment with a compact scene graph, where nodes represent objects (with coarse meshes) and edges encode spatial relationships. For each image in the localization phase, we extract per-patch semantic features, predicting object identities. Localization is performed within a particle filter framework. Each particle, representing a camera pose, projects the coarse object meshes from the scene graph into the image, assigning object identities to patches based on visibility. The similarity of the per-patch features, in the input image, and object features from the scene graph determines the weight of a particle. Subsequent images are incorporated sequentially, refining the pose estimate. By leveraging a compact scene graph and efficient semantic matching, our method significantly reduces storage while maintaining performance on real-world datasets. The code will be available at https://github.com/DmblnNicole/sg2loc.

</details>


### [97] [Wild3R: Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting from Unconstrained Sparse Photo Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11894)
*Yuto Furutani,Takashi Otonari,Kaede Shiohara,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Wild3R提出了一种前馈式3D高斯泼溅方法，用于处理无约束稀疏照片集合中的多样光照和瞬态物体问题。通过构建包含200个场景、170种光照条件和33.75万张图像的WildCity数据集，模型学习跨视角外观一致性并去除瞬态内容，性能优于现有前馈方法，接近逐场景优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式3DGS方法在处理真实世界照片集合时，难以应对多样光照条件和瞬态物体（如行人、车辆）带来的挑战，主要瓶颈在于缺乏包含多视角、多种光照和瞬态变化的大规模训练数据。

Method: 构建WildCity数据集（200场景、170光照条件、33.75万张图像）；提出Wild3R模型，学习基于参考视图条件下的跨视角外观一致性，同时去除瞬态内容。

Result: 该方法在无约束稀疏照片集合上优于现有前馈式方法，性能与基于逐场景优化的方法相当。

Conclusion: 通过大规模多样化数据集和外观一致性学习，前馈式3DGS可以有效处理真实世界中的光照变化和瞬态物体干扰，缩小了与逐场景优化方法的差距。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) removes the need for time-consuming per-scene optimization required by traditional 3DGS. However, existing feed-forward approaches struggle with real-world photo collections that include diverse lighting conditions and transient objects. In this paper, we present Wild3R, a feed-forward approach for unconstrained sparse photo collections. The main bottleneck is the lack of training data that provides multiple viewpoints, a variety of illuminations, and transient variations necessary for learning robust scene representations. To address this, we introduce the WildCity dataset, which comprises 200 scenes, 170 lighting conditions, and transient objects, resulting in 337,500 images in total. By leveraging the dataset, our model learns appearance consistency across viewpoints conditioned on reference views, while removing transient content. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms existing feed-forward approaches and achieves results competitive with prior per-scene optimization-based methods.

</details>


### [98] [From Content to Knowledge: Lightning Fast Long-Video Understanding with Neural Knowledge Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11913)
*Yuchen Guan,Xiao Li,Zongyu Guo,Xiaoyi Zhang,Xiulian Peng,Chun Yuan,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将长视频表示为神经网络知识表征（NKR），通过智能体知识蒸馏（AKD）自动生成密集描述和问答对，将视频语义编码到轻量级网络权重中。推理时将该权重挂载到冻结的VLM上，无需重新加载或编码原始视频，实现查询驱动的长视频理解。在LVBench上达到SOTA性能，同时延迟降低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频理解方法存在推理成本随视频长度线性增长的问题，无论是token流处理还是预组织数据库都难以实现高效的交互式多轮查询。

Method: 提出NKR范式：将视频语义编码为VLM骨干网络的轻量级权重补丁；通过AKD流程，由智能体自动合成密集描述和QA对来蒸馏视频知识；推理时将NKR挂载到冻结VLM上实现查询理解。

Result: 在LVBench上达到与SOTA相当的性能，端到端延迟降低两个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 该方法将视频长度与推理成本解耦，实现了高摊销效率的多轮视频理解，为交互式长视频理解开辟了新可能。

Abstract: We propose a new paradigm for long video understanding by treating a long video as a Neural Knowledge Representation (NKR). NKR represents video contents neither as a stream of tokens nor pre-organized databases, but as an individual small portion of network weights attached to the VLM backbone. The NKR weights are optimized to encapsulate the video's semantic content via a novel Agentic Knowledge Distillation (AKD) process, where an agent automatically synthesizes dense descriptions and question-answer pairs to distill the video's knowledge into the NKR. While AKD serves as a comprehensive, one-time encoding phase, the resulting NKR transforms the video into a portable, reusable asset. At inference, the lightweight NKR is mounted onto a frozen Vision-Language Model (VLM), enabling direct, query-based understanding without reloading or re-encoding the original video. This approach decouples video length from inference cost, offering high amortized efficiency for multi-turn video understanding. Experiments on the LVBench benchmark show our method achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art approaches while reducing end-to-end latency by over two orders of magnitude, opening new possibilities for interactive long-video understanding.

</details>


### [99] [Corpus Augmentation for Sign Language Translation via LLM-Guided Video Stitching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11925)
*Zsolt Robotka,Ádám Rák,Jalal Al-Afandi,András Horváth,György Cserey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需额外人工标注、外部视频语料或生成模型的语料库增强方法，通过从已有标注数据中提取逐词手语片段、利用LLM生成新句子、组装合成视频-文本对，在GFSLT-VLP基线基础上提升了+2.92 BLEU-4，并发现合成数据在视觉预训练阶段存在有害影响、突然边界可作为隐式正则化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量的手语翻译平行视频-文本对稀缺，限制了模型在长尾词汇和未见结构上的泛化能力。现有方法依赖大规模弱对齐数据预训练或无标注方法，但微调阶段的高质量数据瓶颈仍未解决。

Method: 1) 从训练视频中通过CTC强制对齐提取逐词手语片段；2) 利用基于语料库锚定的LLM生成新的手语词-句子对；3) 通过随机句子采样和片段分配组装合成RGB视频-文本对。这些合成数据可直接被RGB-based SLT模型使用，或转换为姿态/特征表示。

Result: 在严格相同的条件下，该方法在GFSLT-VLP基线上实现了+2.92 BLEU-4的提升，而此前五种无标注方法中最大提升仅为0.98 BLEU-4。合成数据在视觉-语言预训练阶段反而有害（尽管改进了目标函数），且优化片段过渡的视觉平滑性在L2准则下适得其反。

Conclusion: 所提语料库增强方法能有效提升手语翻译性能，且架构无关。突变边界可能作为一种隐式正则化机制起作用，而平滑过渡反而有害。该方法无需额外人力或外部资源，实用性强。

Abstract: Sign language translation (SLT) converts sign language video into spoken language text and holds significant promise for improving accessibility and enabling communication between signing and non-signing communities. While large weakly-aligned datasets have enabled pre-training at scale and gloss-free methods have reduced reliance on expert annotation, high-quality parallel sign video-text pairs for fine-tuning remain scarce, limiting generalisation on long-tail vocabulary and unseen constructions. We propose a corpus augmentation approach that requires no additional human annotation, external sign-language video corpora, or generative video models, relying only on the existing gloss-annotated training corpus and an LLM for sentence generation: per-gloss clips are extracted from training videos via CTC forced-alignment, novel gloss-sentence pairs are generated by a corpus-anchored LLM, and synthetic sequences are assembled through random sentence sampling and clip assignment. The resulting synthetic RGB video-text pairs are architecture-agnostic at the downstream training stage and can be consumed directly by RGB-based SLT models, or converted into pose or feature representations by pipelines that derive such inputs from video. Sincan et al. re-evaluated five recent gloss-free methods under strictly identical conditions; the largest verified gain over the GFSLT-VLP baseline was only 0.98 BLEU-4. Our augmentation, applied within the same framework, achieves +2.92 BLEU-4 without any change to architecture or training protocol. We further identify that synthetic data harms vision-language pretraining despite improving its objectives, and that optimising clip transitions for visual smoothness is counter-productive under L2-based criteria; we propose that abrupt boundaries may act as a form of implicit regularisation. Code is available at https://github.com/robizso/slt-datagen.

</details>


### [100] [Feature extraction for plant growth estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11966)
*Simbarashe Aldrin Ngorima,Albert Helberg,Marelie H. Davel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种植物生长阶段估计方法：基于Gabor滤波器和形态学操作的手工特征方法，以及基于预训练CNN和迁移学习的深度特征方法。在公开数据集上的实验表明，CNN特征（特别是VGG-19结合RBF-SVM）在速度和精度上均优于手工特征，达到98.4%的准确率，每张图像处理仅需0.08秒，适合实时农业应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准农业需要实时估计植物生长阶段以减少水肥等资源的浪费，但不同生长阶段的植物具有相似形态特征，使得自主估计存在困难。

Method: 提出两种特征提取方法：（1）基于Gabor滤波器组和形态学操作的手工特征提取；（2）基于预训练CNN和迁移学习的深度特征提取。分别使用SVM和Boosting树作为分类器进行比较。

Result: CNN特征在速度和精度上均优于手工特征。最佳系统（VGG-19特征+RBF-SVM）在油菜和小萝卜两个物种上均达到98.4%的准确率，每张图像处理时间为0.08秒。

Conclusion: 两种方法均适用于实时应用场景，其中基于CNN的迁移学习方法更适合精准农业中的生长阶段估计任务。

Abstract: Precision agriculture requires the estimation of plant growth stages in real-time. When the plant growth stage is known, the wastage of resources in cultivation, such as nutrients and water, is reduced as only the required resources need to be supplied. Plants at different growth stages, however, have similar morphological features, which can make autonomous growth stage estimation difficult. This paper presents two feature extraction methods for growth stage estimation: one that uses a bank of Gabor filters and morphological operations, and the other that uses pre-trained convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and transfer learning. We test these methods on a publicly available plant growth stage dataset (``bccr-segset``) for two species, canola and radish, grown and captured under indoor conditions. The two proposed feature extraction methods are compared, using support vector machines and boosted trees as classifiers. We find that both methods are suitable for real-time applications, and that CNN features outperform the hand-crafted features, both with regard to speed and accuracy. The best system (VGG-19 features, classified with a radial basis function support vector machine) obtained an accuracy of 98.4% for both species, processing an image in 0.08 seconds.

</details>


### [101] [SpecLoR: Spectral Lookahead Rectification for Motion-Coherent Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11969)
*Xu Zhang,Yu Lu,Ruijie Quan,Zhaozheng Chen,Bohan Wang,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpecLoR方法，通过频域中的前向预测和幅度谱修正，在几乎不增加计算开销的情况下减少文本生成视频中的时空伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow Matching在文本生成视频中存在速度近似和数值离散化误差累积导致的采样轨迹漂移问题，生成视频常有严重的时空不一致性，而直接修正漂移的噪声潜变量面临时间步依赖噪声掩盖结构信息和空间干预破坏局部几何结构且计算成本高的挑战。

Method: SpecLoR是一种即插即用的推理方法，在早期采样阶段通过前向预测估计干净潜变量并计算其3D时空频谱，然后修正幅度谱使其匹配自然视频的统计先验（保留相位），最后将修正后的状态重新加噪以继续ODE积分。

Result: 在Wan2.2上的实验表明，SpecLoR显著减少了物理伪影并增强了运动连贯性，仅增加4次NFE的极小计算开销。

Conclusion: 通过在频域利用自然视频的统计先验进行幅度谱修正，能够有效缓解采样漂移问题，是一种轻量高效的视频生成质量增强方法。

Abstract: Flow Matching has enabled robust text-to-video generation via latent ODE sampling. However, velocity approximation and numerical discretization errors inevitably accumulate, causing sampling trajectories to drift. Consequently, generated videos often suffer from severe spatiotemporal inconsistencies. Nevertheless, directly correcting these drifted, noisy latents is challenging: (i) timestep-dependent noise obscures reliable structural cues; (ii) spatial interventions risk disrupting intricate local geometry while incurring heavy computational costs. To address this, we propose Spectral Lookahead Rectification (SpecLoR), a plug-and-play inference method that bypasses noise via lookahead prediction, and circumvents spatiotemporal entanglement by shifting corrections to the frequency domain, where universal statistical priors of natural videos are readily available. First, during early sampling stages, SpecLoR looks ahead to estimate the clean latent $z_{t,0}$ and computes its 3D spatiotemporal spectrum. Next, SpecLoR rectifies the amplitude spectrum to match the prior, leaving the phase intact. Finally, the corrected state is re-noised to resume ODE integration. Experiments on Wan2.2 demonstrate that SpecLoR significantly reduces physical artifacts and enhances motion coherence across multiple benchmarks with minimal computational overhead (4 additional NFEs).

</details>


### [102] [ParseFixer: An Agentic Framework for Document Parsing via Selective Multimodal Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11977)
*LeKai Yu,Hao Liu,Kun Wang,Zhiran Li,Ruping Cao,Fan Liu,Yupeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ParseFixer，一种用于文档解析的智能体框架，包含全页骨干解析（FBP）和智能体选择性纠正（ASC）两个模块，在DataMFM Challenge Track 1中获得第三名（总体得分61.78）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档解析任务需要同时满足准确的内容恢复和忠实的结构重建，现有方法难以兼顾这两方面的互补需求。

Method: ParseFixer包含两个核心模块：1）全页骨干解析（FBP）：使用MinerU2.5 Pro生成稳定的初始Markdown输出；2）智能体选择性纠正（ASC）：检测高价值的解析失败并通过验证-回滚纠正流程进行修复。该框架在开源骨干解析后进行选择性多模态纠正，避免重写可靠的骨干预测。

Result: 在测试集上取得总体得分61.78，排名Track 1第三名。

Conclusion: ParseFixer通过选择性纠正机制有效提升了关键文档元素的恢复质量，验证了在骨干解析后加入选择性多模态纠正的有效性。

Abstract: In this report, we present our third-place solution for the DataMFM Challenge Track 1: Document Parsing. This track requires models to recover structured Markdown documents from document page images while preserving textual content and document structure. To address the complementary requirements of accurate content recovery and faithful structure reconstruction, we propose ParseFixer, an agentic framework for backbone parsing and selective correction. ParseFixer consists of two key modules: Full-Page Backbone Parsing (FBP) and Agentic Selective Correction (ASC). FBP produces stable initial Markdown outputs with MinerU2.5 Pro, while ASC detects high-value parsing failures and repairs them through a verify-and-rollback correction process. By placing selective multimodal correction after open-source backbone parsing, ParseFixer improves the recovery of key document elements without rewriting reliable backbone predictions. On the test set, our final system achieves an overall score of 61.78 and ranks third in Track 1, demonstrating its effectiveness for accurate document parsing. Our code will be released at: https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/CVPRW26-ParseFixer.

</details>


### [103] [From Nominal Intensity to Equivalent Rainfall: A Path-Based Credibility Evaluation Framework for Simulated Rainfall in Autonomous-Driving Perception Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11989)
*Tian Xia,Xin Zhao,Shaolingfeng Ye,Junyi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于路径的模拟降雨可信度评估方法，用于自动驾驶感知测试。通过联合雨滴粒径和速度分布、感知一致性校正，在2.4m×7.2m试验区评估不同路径，识别出Path IV和Path VI为最优路径，实现了降雨强度稳定性、雨滴谱真实性和感知一致性的平衡评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 封闭场测试仅用名义降雨强度或单点测量描述模拟降雨，难以与实际降雨对齐，也无法将测试结果映射到真实场景，亟需路径级的可信度评估方法以支持SOTIF导向的风险评估。

Method: 以真实降雨的雨滴粒径-速度联合分布为参考，用路径等效降雨强度、不确定带和路径平均RRD分数表征每条候选路径；进一步利用激光雷达目标点云计数和平均反射率进行感知一致性校正，量化模拟降雨路径对真实降雨感知效应的代理能力。实验使用约10,000个真实雨滴谱样本、728组RainSense感知样本和45个空间采样点。

Result: 相同名义条件下存在空间非均匀性，验证了路径级评估的必要性。Path IV（11.54±0.31 mm/h, RRD=0.43）和Path VI（8.28±0.34 mm/h, RRD=0.46）为最优路径，在降雨强度稳定性、雨滴谱真实性和感知一致性上表现更均衡。

Conclusion: 该方法支持自动驾驶降雨感知测试中的路径选择、条件描述和结果可信解释。

Abstract: Credible simulated-rainfall conditions are essential for identifying perception-system boundaries and supporting SOTIF-oriented risk assessment in automated driving. However, closed-field tests are often described only by nominal rainfall intensity or single-point measurements, making it difficult to align simulated rain fields with real rainfall and map test results to real-world scenarios. This paper proposes a path-based credibility evaluation method for simulated rainfall in autonomous-driving perception tests. Using the drop size and velocity joint distribution of real rainfall as the reference, each candidate path is represented by path-equivalent rainfall intensity, an uncertainty band, and a path-averaged Realism of Raindrop Distribution (RRD) score. Lidar target point-cloud count and mean reflectivity are further used for perception-consistency correction, quantifying the proxy capability of each simulated-rainfall path for real-rainfall perception effects. Experiments are conducted using about 10,000 real-rainfall raindrop-spectrum samples, 728 RainSense perception samples, and 45 spatial sampling points in a 2.4 m x 7.2 m simulated-rainfall area. Results show that spatial non-uniformity remains under the same nominal condition, confirming the need for path-based evaluation. The method identifies Path IV and Path VI as preferable candidates, with results of 11.54 +/- 0.31 mm/h, RRD = 0.43, and 8.28 +/- 0.34 mm/h, RRD = 0.46, respectively. These paths show more balanced performance in rainfall-intensity stability, raindrop-spectrum realism, and perception consistency. The proposed method supports path selection, condition description, and credible interpretation of autonomous-driving perception tests under rainfall.

</details>


### [104] [FitVTON: Fit-aware Virtual Try-On via Body-Garment Size Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12012)
*Yiqun Ning,Ao Shen,Chenhang He,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FitVTON提出了一种基于扩散模型的体型感知虚拟试穿方法，通过结构化文本提示编码服装-身体尺寸关系，从参数化服装模型生成的模拟试穿三元组中学习，并引入两个辅助头预测服装和暴露身体区域的掩码以改善服装轮廓贴合效果，同时设计了纹理校正阶段提升模拟数据的真实感。此外，构建了FittingEffect3K真实世界数据集，结合VLM评分协议评估贴合保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的虚拟试穿方法大多将任务视为2D修复问题，优先考虑纹理保留而非物理合理性，导致生成的图像虽然视觉上合理，但无法反映不同体型下服装的真实贴合效果。

Method: 1) 通过结构化文本提示编码服装与身体之间的尺寸关系；2) 从参数化服装模型生成的模拟试穿三元组中学习；3) 引入两个辅助头分别预测服装掩码和暴露身体区域掩码，以改善服装轮廓的贴合效果；4) 引入纹理校正阶段提升模拟数据的真实感外观。

Result: 在主观和定量实验上，FitVTON相比现有最先进方法展现出更真实的贴合保真度，在尺寸准确性和形状保持方面显著提升，同时保持有竞争力的图像质量。

Conclusion: FitVTON通过体型感知建模和模拟-真实域适应，有效解决了虚拟试穿中物理贴合不合理的问题，为多样体型下的真实感虚拟试穿提供了有效方案。

Abstract: While diffusion-based virtual try-on has achieved impressive visual realism, most methods treat the task as 2D inpainting, prioritizing texture preservation over physical plausibility. Consequently, they often produce plausible-looking images that fail to reflect authentic garment fit across diverse body shapes. We present FitVTON, a Fit-aware virtual try-on model on different bodies in the wild. FitVTON encodes garment-body size through structured text prompts, and learn from simulated try-on triplets from parameterized garment model. To improve the fitting effects over garment silhouettes, we introduce two auxiliary head to predict the masks for both the garment and the exposed body. We further introduce a texture rectification stage to improve realistic appearance from simulated data. To evaluate the fitting fidelity, we curate a real-world dataset, FittingEffect3K, combining VLM-based scoring protocol. Both subjective and quantitive experiments show that FitVTON demonstrate authentic fitting fidelity, with significant sizing accuracy and shape preservation over state-of-the-art methods while maintaining competitive image quality. Project Page: https://zenoning.github.io/FitVTON/.

</details>


### [105] [ViT-FREE: Efficient Face Recognition via Early Exiting and Synthetic Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12023)
*Tahar Chettaoui,Guray Ozgur,Eduarda Caldeira,Naser Damer,Fadi Boutros*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ViT-FREE，一种基于预训练Vision Transformer的多出口早退框架，无需训练即可在中间层进行人脸验证，在IJB-C基准上第10层退出可实现20%加速且性能仅下降1.5%。同时提出ViT-FREE_FT轻量微调策略，仅适配投影层以提升浅层出口性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers在计算机视觉和人脸识别中表现出色，但其高计算成本限制了在资源受限设备上的部署，亟需一种平衡效率与准确率的方法。

Method: 1) ViT-FREE：利用Transformer各编码器块输出特征维度一致的特点，在预训练ViT上构建多出口早退框架，无需修改或重新训练即可从中间层直接进行人脸验证。2) ViT-FREE_FT：仅适配投影层的轻量级微调策略，使用小型合成数据集在保持骨干网络冻结的情况下提升浅层出口性能。

Result: 实验表明，补丁嵌入和注意力图在深度上逐步演化，相邻ViT块之间高度相似且与最终表示对齐度逐渐增加。在多个FR基准上，后期出口实现良好的精度-效率平衡，第10层退出相比完整推理获得最高20%加速，性能仅下降1.5%。

Conclusion: 预训练ViT的中间层已具备稳定且具有判别性的特征表示，适合用于早退策略。所提出的ViT-FREE框架能在不牺牲过多精度的情况下显著降低推理成本，而ViT-FREE_FT进一步改善了浅层出口的性能。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) have gained significant attention in computer vision and shown strong potential for face recognition (FR). However, their high computational cost makes deployment on resource-constrained devices challenging, motivating the need for methods that balance efficiency and accuracy. In this work, we investigate early exiting in pretrained ViTs as a simple yet effective training-free strategy for efficient FR inference. Leveraging the uniform feature dimensionality across transformer encoder blocks, we introduce ViT-FREE, a multi-exit framework that enables face verification directly from intermediate representations without modifying or retraining the backbone model, and thus, reducing inference cost. Empirically, we show that patch embeddings and attention maps evolve progressively across depth, exhibiting high similarity between consecutive ViT blocks and increasing alignment with the final representation. This indicates gradual feature refinement and attention convergence, suggesting that intermediate layers already provide stable and discriminative representations suitable for early exiting. Through extensive experiments on multiple FR benchmarks, we systematically analyze the accuracy-efficiency trade-off across exit depths. Our results demonstrate that later exits achieve a highly favorable balance, with exiting at layer 10 yielding up to a 20% speedup while incurring only a 1.5 drop in verification performance on benchmarks such as IJB-C. Also, we propose ViT-FREE_FT, a lightweight exit-specific fine-tuning strategy that adapts only the projection layers using a small synthetic dataset while keeping the transformer backbone frozen. This approach improves the performance of shallow exits while preserving the efficiency benefits and leaving deeper exits largely unaffected.

</details>


### [106] [SpikeTAD: Spiking Neural Networks for End-to-End Temporal Action Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12033)
*Min Yang,Mi Zhou,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SpikeTAD，首个基于脉冲神经网络（SNN）的端到端时序动作检测架构，在保持极低功耗的同时，在 THUMOS14 和 ActivityNet-1.3 数据集上分别达到 67.2% 和 37.42% 的平均 mAP，证明了低功耗时序动作检测模型的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解模型体积大、功耗高，难以部署在移动设备上；SNN 虽具有低功耗优势，但存在转换时间步长过长和性能严重下降的问题，限制了其在时序动作检测任务中的应用。

Method: 探索 SNN 在时序动作检测（TAD）中的应用，提出首个基于 SNN 的端到端 TAD 架构 SpikeTAD。

Result: 在 THUMOS14 上平均 mAP 达 67.2%，在 ActivityNet-1.3 上平均 mAP 达 37.42%，同时保持极低功耗。

Conclusion: SpikeTAD 证明了在低功耗条件下实现有效的时序动作检测是可行的，为 SNN 在视频理解领域的部署提供了新方向。

Abstract: Video understanding is a crucial part of computer vision, with numerous application scenarios. With the increasing popularity of mobile devices, an increasing number of efforts are trying to deploy video understanding models on them. However, existing video understanding models are difficult to deploy due to their large size and prohibitive power consumption. Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have shown bioplausibility and low power advantages over Artificial Neural Networks (ANNs), especially on neuromorphic chips which are regarded as essential components of future mobile devices. However, excessively long conversion time-steps and severe performance degradation problems limit their application. To solve the problems above, we explore the application of SNNs on temporal action detection (TAD), which is an important task in video understanding, and propose the first SNN-based end-to-end TAD architecture coined as SpikeTAD. While maintaining extremely low power consumption, SpikeTAD achieves an average mAP of 67.2% in THUMOS14 and 37.42% in ActivityNet-1.3, demonstrating the feasibility of a low-power TAD model. Our code is available at https://github.com/MCG-NJU/SpikeTAD.

</details>


### [107] [Vision Transformers for Face Recognition Need More Registers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12036)
*Tahar Chettaoui,Guray Ozgur,Eduarda Caldeira,Naser Damer,Fadi Boutros*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出在基于CPE的人脸识别ViT模型中引入寄存器令牌（register tokens），有效消除了注意力图中的伪影，显著提升了模型的可解释性和识别性能。最终模型ViT-8R在IJB-B和IJB-C基准上达到了ViT-based FR模型的SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPE-based ViT在人脸识别中虽优于CLS-token范式，但其注意力图存在伪影，限制了模型的可解释性。作者旨在解决这一可解释性问题，同时保持甚至提升识别性能。

Method: 在CPE-based ViT中引入寄存器令牌（register tokens）——可学习的令牌与初始patch嵌入拼接，通过ViT编码器块联合处理，以产生更结构化、更可解释的注意力图。

Result: 1) 伪影在不同规模ViT骨干中普遍存在；2) 寄存器令牌有效缓解了伪影；3) 4或8个寄存器显著增强可解释性，8个寄存器同时获得最高验证精度和最平滑的注意力结构；4) ViT-8R在IJB-B和IJB-C上达到ViT-based FR模型SOTA。

Conclusion: ViT-8R（CPE-based ViT-B + 8个寄存器令牌）实现了最佳性能与可解释性的平衡，其清晰的注意力图为理解模型行为提供了更深层次的洞察。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision Transformers (ViTs) for face recognition (FR) have moved beyond the standard CLS-token paradigm. In this paradigm, a special classification token (CLS) is prepended to the patch embeddings and used as a representation of the input for downstream tasks. An alternative approach, Concatenated Patch Embeddings (CPE), instead leverages all patch tokens by concatenating them into a single vector, which is then projected into a compact face representation. CPE has been shown to improve recognition performance in comparison to CLS-based ones, but our qualitative analysis of attention maps showed the presence of artifacts that limit their interpretability. To address this issue, we incorporate register tokens, learnable tokens concatenated to the initial patch embeddings, and processed jointly through the ViT encoder blocks. This mechanism has been shown to produce more structured and interpretable attention maps compared to baseline ViT. We empirically demonstrate that these artifacts consistently appear across various ViT backbones, including small and large models, and that introducing register tokens effectively mitigates them. Adding four or eight registers significantly enhances interpretability, with eight registers providing the highest verification accuracies and smoothest attention structures. Our resulting model, ViT-8R, corresponds to a CPE-based ViT-B architecture augmented with eight register tokens achieves state-of-the-art performance among ViT-based FR models on large-scale IJB-B and IJB-C benchmarks. Also, ViT-8R produces substantially clearer attention maps compared with the baseline model, which offer deeper insight into the model's attention behavior (https://github.com/TaharChettaoui/ViT-FR-Registers)

</details>


### [108] [MFEN:Multi-Frequency Expert Network for Visible-Infrared Person Re-ID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12051)
*Xulin Li,Yan Lu,Bin Liu,Qinhong Yang,Qi Chu,Tao Gong,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对可见光-红外行人重识别中因光照差异导致的模态不匹配问题，本文提出多频专家网络（MFEN），通过多频调制和混合专家机制自适应组合不同频段，并引入随机频率增强（RFA）和频率辅助优化（FAO）进行训练，在三个数据集上取得优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见光-红外行人重识别中，可见光与红外图像间的模态差异主要由光照条件（波长、光源类型）不同导致。现有频率方法要么未区分不同频段，要么只关注单一频段，难以应对多样化的光照环境。

Method: 提出多频专家网络（MFEN），通过混合专家设计实现多频调制和自适应频段组合；同时引入随机频率增强（RFA）和频率辅助优化（FAO）辅助训练。

Result: 在三个VI-ReID数据集上的大量实验证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 三个模块相互补充，共同捕获关键频域细节，实现鲁棒的特征学习。

Abstract: Visible-infrared person re-identification (VI-ReID) is challenging due to the large modality discrepancy between visible and infrared images. We contend that this discrepancy is largely related to differing lighting conditions, including differences in light wavelength and light source type. Recently, frequency-based VI-ReID approaches have achieved notable success because frequency information can better extract identity-relevant contours and details while excluding irrelevant lighting and color. However, existing methods either do not distinguish different frequency bands or focus on only one band, which is insufficient under diverse lighting conditions. To perform comprehensive frequency domain learning, we propose a Multi-Frequency Expert Network (MFEN) that enables multi-frequency modulation and adaptively combines different bands through a mixture-of-experts design. We further introduce Random Frequency Augmentation (RFA) and Frequency Auxiliary Optimization (FAO) to better train MFEN. The three modules are complementary and jointly capture critical frequency-domain details for robust representation learning. Extensive experiments on three VI-ReID datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [109] [Performance Analysis of YOLOv11 and YOLOv8 for Mixed Traffic Object Detection under Adverse Weather Conditions in Developing Countries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12066)
*Quoc Thuan Nguyen,Ha Anh Vu,Ngo Dang Thanh Ngan,Minh Phuc Hoang Ngoc*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对YOLOv11 Nano与YOLOv8 Nano在融合数据集（IDD + BDD100K）上进行了全面评估，重点关注高熵场景下的检测精度、推理速度和计算效率。YOLOv11n在mAP@50达46.6%，精度提升3.2%，FLOPs减少22%（6.3G vs 8.1G），在Tesla T4上保持70.9 FPS的实时推理速度，为安全关键型边缘部署提供了更优的权衡方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代车辆系统在恶劣条件下的鲁棒性能已成为自动驾驶的关键问题，亟需评估新一代YOLO架构在高熵场景中的表现。

Method: 将YOLOv11 Nano与YOLOv8 Nano在融合数据集（IDD + BDD100K）上进行基准对比，分析密集混合交通、降雨和低光照等高熵场景下的检测精度、推理速度和计算效率的权衡。
