Result: YOLOv11n的mAP@50为46.6%，精度比基线提升3.2%；FLOPs减少22%（6.3G vs 8.1G），在Tesla T4上达到70.9 FPS的实时推理速度。

Conclusion: YOLOv11n在减少误报、提升能效的同时保持了实时推理能力，为安全关键型边缘部署提供了更优的精度-速度-效率权衡。

Abstract: In modern vehicular systems, robust performance under harsh conditions has become a critical problem of autonomous driving. Our study delivers a comprehensive evaluation of the newest iteration of the YOLO series, which is YOLOv11 Nano architecture benchmarked against the widely adopted YOLOv8 Nano as a baseline on a custom fused dataset that combines the Indian Driving Dataset (IDD) [1] and Berkeley Deep Drive Dataset (BDD100K) [2]. We have analyzed the trade-offs among detection accuracy, inference speed, and computational efficiency in high-entropy scenarios involving dense mixed traffic, rain, and low-light conditions. Specifically, YOLOv11n achieves a mean Average Precision (mAP@50) of 46.6%, with a notable 3.2% improvement in Precision over the baseline, effectively reducing false positives in cluttered scenes. Furthermore, the proposed model exhibits enhanced energy efficiency, requiring 22% fewer FLOPs (6.3G vs. 8.1G) while maintaining real-time inference speed of 70.9 FPS on a Tesla T4 GPU, offering an optimal trade-off for safety-critical edge deployment.

</details>


### [110] [Tac-DINO: Learning Vision-Tactile Features with Patch Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12069)
*Hong Li,Yankang Dong,Yue Xu,Yihan Tang,Mingzhu Li,Jiamin Qiu,Qihang Yao,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Nan Xue,Yong-Lu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含505个真实物体、超过2万次触觉接触的大规模触觉数据集，并设计了视觉-触觉全息匹配基准（Vis-Tac Holographic Matching Benchmark）来评估局部到全局的跨模态对齐能力，提出了视觉-触觉补丁对齐（VTPA）方法，实验证明该方法优于无对齐的方法，并能与整体物体图像对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前触觉学习主要聚焦于图像级预训练或对齐，但触觉信号对应的是局部物体接触，而尺度对齐和全息匹配的研究仍十分有限，且缺乏合适的数据集和基准。为填补这一空白，作者构建了大规模触觉数据集并提出了相应的对齐方法和评估基准。

Method: 首先构建数据采集系统获取大规模触觉数据集（505个物体，2万+触觉接触）；在此基础上设计Vis-Tac全息匹配基准用于评估视觉-触觉局部到全局的对齐能力；提出视觉-触觉补丁对齐（VTPA）方法进行视觉-触觉表征学习。

Result: 实验结果表明VTPA方法超越了无对齐方法，并能够与整体物体图像实现对齐。

Conclusion: 本文通过构建大规模触觉数据集、设计全息匹配基准以及提出VTPA对齐方法，有效提升了视觉-触觉局部到全局的对齐能力，为触觉表征学习提供了新的思路和资源。

Abstract: Touch is the primary medium through which humans interact with the environment. Currently, tactile learning mainly focuses on image-level pretraining or alignment. However, tactile signals correspond to local object contact, while research into scale alignment and holographic matching remains limited and proper datasets and benchmarks also lack. To bridge this gap, we first construct a data collection system to acquire a large-scale tactile dataset, with over 20 K tactile contacts from 505 real-world objects. Building on this dataset, we design a Vis-Tac Holographic Matching Benchmark to evaluate vision-tactile local-to-global alignment ability. Then we propose Vision-Tactile Patch Alignment (VTPA) methods for vision-tactile representation learning. Experiments demonstrate that these exceed the performance of methods without alignment and align with whole-object images.

</details>


### [111] [World Model Self-Distillation: Training World Models to Solve General Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12072)
*Sebastian Stapf,Pablo Acuaviva Huertos,Aram Davtyan,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个结合自蒸馏和强化学习的可扩展框架，使预训练视频生成模型具备任务求解能力，无需昂贵的任务-视频配对数据。通过VLM生成任务方案→视频扩散模型演示→自蒸馏到执行器→强化学习优化，在WorldTasks-Benchmark和DreamGen机器人基准上超越原模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视频生成模型虽具备涌现任务求解能力，但依赖详细文本描述，限制了其直接用于规划和决策；现有方法要么将推理外包给语言/视觉语言模型，要么依赖监督微调需要昂贵的任务-执行视频配对数据，难以扩展。

Method: 提出可扩展框架：(1) VLM生成候选任务及详细步骤解；(2) 预训练视频扩散模型（Demonstrator）根据解条件生成视频；(3) 将Demonstrator行为自蒸馏到Executor（仅以图像和简短任务提示为条件）；(4) 使用来自VLM反馈的强化学习进一步优化Executor，利用判断视频是否满足任务与生成解决方案之间的不对称性。

Result: 在WorldTasks-Benchmark和DreamGen机器人基准上，Executor在基于VLM的评估协议下超越了Demonstrator，并在机器人任务上具有竞争力的迁移表现。

Conclusion: 所提框架无需人工标注的任务-视频配对数据，即可有效将视频生成模型中的任务求解能力蒸馏到指令条件化的执行器中，并通过VLM反馈的强化学习进一步提升性能，展现出在机器人任务上的迁移潜力。

Abstract: Pretrained video generators are promising visual world models that exhibit emergent task-solving abilities; however, their reliance on detailed textual descriptions limits their direct use for planning and decision-making. Existing approaches either outsource this reasoning to language or vision-language models, or rely on supervised fine-tuning with paired task-execution videos, which are costly to collect and difficult to scale. We propose a scalable framework that elicits task-solving ability in such models by combining self-distillation with reinforcement learning. Given an unlabeled scene image, a vision-language model generates a candidate task and a detailed step-by-step solution. The solution conditions a pretrained video diffusion model, the Demonstrator; we distill its behavior into an Executor conditioned only on the image and a short task prompt. This transfers execution knowledge from caption-guided generation to instruction-conditioned task solving without curated task-video supervision. We further improve the Executor with reinforcement learning from VLM feedback, exploiting the asymmetry between judging whether a sampled video satisfies a task and generating the solution. Experiments on our proposed WorldTasks-Benchmark and the DreamGen robotics benchmark show that the Executor surpasses the Demonstrator under our VLM-based evaluation protocol and transfers competitively to robotic tasks.

</details>


### [112] [ISAP-3D: Identity-Slot Aligned Part-Aware 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12099)
*Junlin Hao,Haoshuai Fu,Xibin Song,Wei Li,Ruigang Yang,Xinggong Zhang,Jinchuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对部件感知3D生成中存在的结构歧义问题（由于身份-布局纠缠导致的部件分配不稳定），本文提出身份-槽对齐框架ISAP-3D，通过语义身份令牌锚定每个部件并执行身份条件的一对一布局预测，再结合布局条件的几何合成，显著提升了生成的结构稳定性、可控性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部件感知3D生成方法存在结构歧义问题，根源在于身份-槽排列自由度：缺乏显式的身份-槽对齐导致训练过程中语义部件与生成槽之间的对应关系不可识别，多种槽分配都能拟合相同监督信号，从而产生不一致的分解结果（如槽交换或部件合并）。

Method: 提出ISAP-3D框架：1) 用语义身份令牌锚定每个部件；2) 进行身份条件的一对一布局预测；3) 执行布局条件的几何合成；4) 设计结构化局部-全局条件机制，在语义、空间和几何阶段保持身份对齐；5) 构建了具有统一语义协议的部件级数据集以实现可学习的身份-槽对齐。

Result: 大量实验表明，ISAP-3D在结构稳定性、可控性和鲁棒性方面均优于当前最先进的部件感知生成基线方法。

Conclusion: 稳定的部件感知生成需要身份对齐的一对一槽建模，通过显式身份-槽对齐可以有效解决结构歧义问题，提升生成质量。

Abstract: Part-aware 3D generation aims to synthesize structured objects with semantically meaningful components, yet often suffers from structural ambiguity due to identity-layout entanglement. Existing methods either infer part identity and spatial layout implicitly, which can lead to unstable part allocation (e.g., slot swapping or part merging), or rely on strong layout conditions that are difficult to obtain in practice. We attribute this ambiguity to identity-slot permutation freedom: without explicit identity-slot alignment, the correspondence between semantic parts and generation slots is not identifiable during training, allowing multiple slot assignments to fit the same supervision and leading to inconsistent decomposition. Based on this insight, we argue that stable part-aware generation requires identity-aligned one-to-one slot modelling. We therefore propose an identity-slot aligned framework, ISAP-3D, which anchors each part with semantic identity tokens and performs identity-conditioned one-to-one layout prediction, followed by layout-conditioned geometry synthesis. Structured local-global conditioning maintains identity alignment across semantic, spatial, and geometric stages. We also construct a part-level dataset with a unified semantic protocol to enable learnable and consistent identity-slot alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved structural stability, controllability, and robustness over state-of-the-art part-aware generation baselines.

</details>


### [113] [Q-Fold: Query-Aware Focus-Context Spatio-Temporal Folding for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12125)
*Biao Tang,Xu Chen,Shuxiang Gou,Jingyi Yuan,Yuhan Zhang,Chenqiang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Q-Fold是一种免训练的长视频输入构建框架，通过将连续时间片段组织为异构的Focus-Context表示，在有限视觉预算下同时保留高保真视觉证据和广泛时间覆盖，在多个长视频基准上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频理解方法仍遵循以帧为中心范式，对保留内容采用相似表示，难以在有限视觉预算下同时兼顾高保真视觉证据和广泛时间覆盖。

Method: Q-Fold将连续时间片段而非孤立帧作为基本建模单元，在查询引导下构建异构的Focus-Context表示：与查询相关的片段保留为高保真Focus帧，不相关的片段折叠为保留时间顺序的上下文布局。

Result: 在四个长视频基准和多个Video-MLLM上，Q-Fold在不增加输入预算的情况下持续提升性能，在超长视频基准上达到9.1个百分点的提升。

Conclusion: Q-Fold通过片段级异构表示有效平衡了视觉保真度与时间覆盖率，是一种免训练且有效的长视频输入构建方案。

Abstract: Long-video understanding remains challenging for multimodal large language models, because temporally extended videos often contain thousands of frames and are therefore expensive to process exhaustively. Existing methods usually construct compact visual inputs from long videos under a limited visual budget. However, most of them still follow a frame-centric paradigm and apply similar representations to retained content regardless of its importance. This makes it difficult to preserve both high-fidelity visual evidence and broad temporal coverage. To address this issue, we propose Q-Fold, a training-free input construction framework for long-video understanding. Instead of treating isolated frames as the basic modeling unit, Q-Fold operates on contiguous temporal segments and constructs a heterogeneous Focus--Context representation under query guidance. Query-relevant segments are preserved as high-fidelity Focus Frames, while less relevant segments are folded into chronology-preserving contextual layouts. In this way, Q-Fold preserves critical visual evidence and broad temporal coverage, while better maintaining local temporal continuity within short segments. Experiments on four long-video benchmarks with multiple Video-MLLMs show that Q-Fold consistently improves performance without increasing the input budget. Notably, it achieves gains of up to 9.1 percentage points on an ultra-long video benchmark. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [114] [AGE-MIL: Anchor-Guided Evidence Learning for Patient-Level Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12126)
*Jiawei Niu,Jian Chen,Di Zhang,Junbo Lu,Zhangcheng Liao,Xuhao Liu,Honglin Zhong,Mireia Crispin-Ortuzar,Chen Li,Zeyu Gao,Yi Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AGE-MIL是一个锚点引导的证据多示例学习框架，通过在患者级别构建锚点捕获全局病理上下文，将患者级别风险建模为证据累积过程，在六个患者级预测任务上优于八种SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算病理方法主要基于WSI级别的MIL范式，而患者级建模未被充分探索。临床实践中病理学家通过整合多个WSI的证据得出诊断结论，直接将患者级监督施加于传统MIL框架会导致优化不稳定和预测可靠性下降。

Method: 提出AGE-MIL框架：1) 从切片表示构建患者级锚点以捕获全局病理上下文；2) 通过锚点引导检索和整合诊断相关的局部图块；3) 将患者级风险建模为证据累积过程，在弱监督下实现稳定优化。

Result: 在两个独立队列的六个临床相关患者级预测任务上评估，AGE-MIL一致优于八种SOTA MIL方法。

Conclusion: AGE-MIL通过锚点引导的证据累积机制有效桥接了患者级监督与MIL框架之间的根本性错配问题，实现了稳健的患者级病理预测。

Abstract: Existing computational pathology methods predominantly operate within whole-slide image (WSI)-level multiple instance learning (MIL) paradigms, while patient-level modeling remains underexplored. In routine pathological practice, however, pathologists derive diagnostic and prognostic conclusions by integrating evidence across multiple WSIs rather than relying on any single slide. This discrepancy creates a fundamental misalignment when patient-level supervision is directly imposed on conventional MIL frameworks, often leading to unstable optimization and degraded predictive reliability. To address this issue, we propose Anchor-Guided Evidence MIL (AGE-MIL), a weakly supervised framework for patient-level prediction. AGE-MIL constructs a patient-level anchor from slide representations to capture global pathological context and guide the retrieval and integration of diagnostically relevant local patches, enabling robust patient-level modeling. Patient-level risk is further modeled as an evidence accumulation process, promoting stable optimization under weak supervision. AGE-MIL is evaluated on six clinically relevant patient-level prediction tasks from two independent cohorts. Experimental results show that the proposed framework consistently outperforms eight state-of-the-art MIL methods. Code is available at https://github.com/wodeniua/AGE-MIL.

</details>


### [115] [Time-Conditioned and Multi-Time Survival Prediction from 2D PET/CT Projections in Lung Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12140)
*Ashish Chauhan,Sambit Tarai,Elin Lundström,Johan Öfverstedt,Håkan Ahlström,Joel Kullberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种时间建模方法（ATCS和MTS）用于PET/CT生存预测，在848例NSCLC患者中验证，平均AUC分别达0.794和0.793，优于基线模型（0.767），并揭示了时间离散化粒度对短期和长期预测性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间建模对影像学生存预测的影响尚未被充分探索，现有方法缺乏对不同时间公式化表达的对比研究。

Method: 提出Attention-guided Time-Conditioned Survival (ATCS) 和 Multi-Time Survival (MTS) 两种方法，回顾性分析848例NSCLC患者的预处理PET/CT图像（556例开发，292例测试），以Time-Conditioned Survival (TCS) 为基线，5折交叉验证训练，6个月间隔评估时间依赖AUC（0.5-5年）。

Result: ATCS和MTS的均值AUC分别为0.794和0.793，优于基线TCS的0.767；ATCS在早期（0.5-3年）表现更好，MTS在后期（3.5-5年）表现更好；肿瘤与组织联合特征优于单一特征；较细时间离散化改善短期预测，较粗离散化提供更稳定的长期估计。

Conclusion: 时间建模方式和输入特征设计显著影响PET/CT生存预测性能，所提方法可实现治疗前影像的时间特异性生存估计，有助于风险分层和临床决策。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of overall survival (OS) from positron emission tomography/computed tomography (PET/CT) can support personalized treatment and follow-up strategies in oncology. However, the impact of temporal modeling on imaging-based survival prediction remains insufficiently explored. We investigate how different temporal formulations influence survival prediction by developing two complementary approaches: Attention-guided Time-Conditioned Survival (ATCS) and Multi-Time Survival (MTS). We retrospectively analyzed pre-treatment PET/CT images from 848 patients with non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC), including 556 for model development and 292 for held-out testing. A previously proposed Time-Conditioned Survival (TCS) model was used as a baseline. Models were trained using 5-fold cross-validation and evaluated on the test set using time-dependent area under the curve (AUC) at 6-month intervals from 0.5 to 5 years. Both ATCS and MTS outperformed the baseline TCS model, achieving mean AUCs of 0.794 and 0.793, respectively, compared to 0.767. ATCS performed better at earlier time points (0.5-3 years), whereas MTS performed better at later intervals (3.5-5 years). Combining tumor-specific and tissue-wise PET/CT features improved performance over either input alone. Finer temporal discretization improved short-term prediction, while coarser intervals provided more stable long-term estimates. These findings demonstrate that temporal modeling and input design influence PET/CT-based survival prediction. The proposed approaches enable time-specific survival estimation from pre-treatment imaging and may support improved risk stratification and clinical decision-making.

</details>


### [116] [TopoCap: Learning Topology-Agnostic Motion Priors for Monocular Video-to-Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12153)
*Cheng-Feng Pu,Jia-Peng Zhang,Meng-Hao Guo,Yan-Pei Cao,Shi-Min Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TopoCap提出首个统一框架，能从单目视频提取运动并重定向到任意骨骼拓扑的角色上（从双足到六足及无生命物体），无需测试时优化。核心是通过图CVAE学习通用运动流形，将异构运动链压缩为固定长度潜码，并利用条件流匹配从视频预测拓扑无关的运动码。配合包含5000+独特骨骼拓扑和200万帧的Mobjaverse数据集，该方法在人类和四足基准上超越专家模型，并实现零样本重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作捕捉方法局限于特定物种模板（如SMPL）或需要繁琐的手动绑定，无法泛化到任意骨骼拓扑的角色。随着3D资产生成爆发，亟需一种无需测试时优化、能从单目视频提取运动并重定向到任意拓扑的统一框架。

Method: 两阶段生成流程：1）通用运动流形：使用图CVAE将异构运动链压缩为固定长度潜码，解码器以目标骨架的结构嵌入为条件，实现运动动力学与骨骼拓扑的解耦；2）视频到动画：将问题建模为条件流匹配，从视觉特征预测拓扑无关的运动码。同时构建Mobjaverse数据集（5000+拓扑，200万帧）以学习通用先验。

Result: 在人类和四足动物基准上超越专家模型，实现零样本重定向到长尾3D生物（如六足动物、无生命物体）。

Conclusion: TopoCap证明了通过连续运动流形和拓扑解耦，可以统一处理任意骨骼拓扑的动作捕捉与重定向，为大规模3D资产动画提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The explosion of generative 3D assets has created a massive demand for animation, yet current motion capture methods remain brittle, restricted to species-specific templates (e.g., SMPL) or requiring labor-intensive manual rigging. We introduce TopoCap, the first unified framework capable of extracting motion from monocular video and retargeting it onto characters with arbitrary, unseen skeletal topologies, i.e., from bipeds to hexapods and inanimate objects, without test-time optimization. Our key insight is that while skeletal structures are combinatorial and discrete, the underlying physics of motion occupy a continuous, low-dimensional manifold. We materialize this insight via a two-stage generative pipeline. First, we learn a Universal Motion Manifold using a Graph CVAE that compresses heterogeneous kinematic chains into a shared, fixed-length latent code. By explicitly conditioning the decoder on a structural embedding of the target rig, we disentangle motion dynamics from skeletal topology. Second, we treat video-to-animation as a conditional flow matching problem, predicting these topology-agnostic codes from visual features. To learn this generalized prior, we introduce Mobjaverse, a massive-scale dataset curated from Objaverse-XL. Comprising over 5,000 unique skeletal topologies and 2 million frames, it exceeds the structural diversity of existing datasets by two orders of magnitude. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \MethodMotion outperforms specialist models on human and quadruped benchmarks while enabling zero-shot retargeting for the long tail of 3D creatures. Dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/duckduckplz/Mobjaverse.

</details>


### [117] [Beyond Dark Knowledge: Mixup-Based Distillation for Reliable Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12171)
*José Medina,Paul Honeine,Abdelaziz Bensrhair,Amnir Hadachi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了当mixup仅应用于学生训练时，蒸馏教师模型在未见过的邻域分布上产生分布混淆信号，但学生并未简单模仿教师，而是独立获得了教师所缺乏的更高线性度。KD+mixup一致提升了学生准确率并将过度自信降低一个数量级，校准度独立于准确率传播，温度缩放控制着可测量的准确率-校准权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏和mixup各自能诱导类别边界的平滑性，但两者交互机制尚不清晰，特别是mixup仅用于学生训练时教师被查询在未见过的邻域分布上的影响未被表征。

Method: 在CIFAR和ImageNet上使用不同容量教师模型，仅在学生训练时应用mixup，分析教师监督信号在vicinal分布下的特性及其对学生的影响。

Result: KD+mixup一致提升学生准确率，将过度自信降低一个数量级；学生获得教师所缺乏的更高线性度；校准度独立于准确率传播；温度缩放控制可测量的准确率-校准权衡。

Conclusion: 混合蒸馏并非标准KD的退化版本，而是一种更丰富的传递通道，同时塑造判别性能、不确定性估计和表征几何结构。

Abstract: Knowledge Distillation (KD) and mixup have proven effective at inducing smoothness in class boundaries; KD captures inherent class relationships in probability distributions, and mixup enforces them through convex combinations of inputs. Their interaction, however, remains poorly understood, particularly when mixup is applied only during student training. In this setting, the teacher is queried on inputs drawn from a vicinal distribution it never saw during training, a controlled mismatch whose effect on knowledge transfer has not been characterised. We show that this mismatch causes the teacher's supervisory signal to be dominated by distributional confusion rather than inter-class structure. Despite it, the student does not merely imitate the teacher: it independently acquires greater linearity in the vicinal region, a structural property that the teacher lacks, and goes beyond dark-knowledge transfer. KD with mixup consistently improves student accuracy and reduces overconfidence by an order of magnitude relative to the baseline, across CIFAR and ImageNet with varying-capacity teachers. Crucially, calibration propagates from teacher to student independently of accuracy transfer, and temperature scaling governs a measurable accuracy-calibration trade-off that becomes more pronounced under vicinal training. These results reframe mixup distillation not as a degraded version of standard KD, but as a richer transfer channel that simultaneously shapes discriminative performance, uncertainty estimation, and representational geometry.

</details>


### [118] [DynaTok: Token-Based 4D Reconstruction from Partial Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12189)
*Weirong Chen,Keisuke Tateno,Hidenobu Matsuki,Michael Niemeyer,Daniel Cremers,Federico Tombari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynaTok是一种基于点云的4D重建框架，能够从部分点云序列中无需对应关系地重建完整、时间一致的4D点云序列，通过编码为紧凑潜在令牌、时空Transformer聚合、几何运动解耦和流匹配解码器实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D重建方法大多依赖图像，而基于点云的方法局限于单物体、需要完整输入或显式对应关系，无法处理深度传感器观测中部分、无序且缺乏时间对应关系的点云序列。

Method: DynaTok将帧编码为紧凑潜在令牌，使用基于Transformer的时空编码器聚合不完整观测，通过残差令牌在统一模型中解耦几何与运动，最后用流匹配解码器重建完整且时间一致的4D点云序列。

Result: 在物体级和场景级基准上，DynaTok在重建质量和时间一致性上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: DynaTok有效解决了无对应关系、部分点云序列的4D重建难题，证明了纯点云框架的可行性。

Abstract: We address 4D reconstruction from partial point cloud sequences, where depth-sensor observations are incomplete, unordered, and lack explicit temporal correspondences. This geometry-only setting is challenging due to missing observations and ambiguous dynamics. While recent progress has largely relied on image-based methods, existing point-based approaches typically focus on single objects, assume relatively complete inputs, or require explicit correspondences. To address these limitations, we propose DynaTok, a point-based framework for correspondence-free 4D reconstruction from partial point cloud sequences without images. DynaTok encodes frames into compact latent tokens, aggregates incomplete observations over time with a Transformer-based spatiotemporal encoder, and decouples geometry and motion through residual tokens in a unified model. A flow-matching decoder then reconstructs complete, temporally consistent 4D point-cloud sequences conditioned on the latent tokens. Experiments on object- and scene-level benchmarks demonstrate improved reconstruction quality and temporal coherence from partial point cloud observations. Project page: https://wrchen530.github.io/dynatok/.

</details>


### [119] [InternVideo3: Agentify Foundation Models with Multimodal Contextual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12195)
*Ziang Yan,Sheng Xia,Jiashuo Yu,Yue Wu,Tianxiang Jiang,Songze Li,Kanghui Tian,Yicheng Xu,Yinan He,Kai Chen,Limin Wang,Yu Qiao,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InternVideo3通过多模态上下文推理（MCR）框架，结合KV缓存压缩和分阶段训练，实现了长视频理解中的持续时序感知与迭代交互，在Video-MME等基准上取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放源码的多模态基础模型在长时序、多步骤推理与工具使用的智能体行为方面探索不足，特别是在需要持续时序理解和迭代交互的视频任务中存在明显空白。

Method: 提出多模态上下文推理（MCR），将理解建模为闭环过程；引入多模态多头潜在注意力（M^2LA）通过token保留重参数化压缩KV缓存；采用持续预训练、短到长监督微调、基于规则的强化学习和策略蒸馏的分阶段训练。

Result: 在Video-MME、MLVU、EgoSchema等长视频理解基准上取得优异性能，并实例化为配备检索工具的视频智能体，展现了鲁棒的基于证据的行为。

Conclusion: 高效的上下文处理与闭环推理是使开放多模态模型适应长时序视觉导向智能体的关键。

Abstract: Recent progress in foundation models has shifted toward agentic behavior involving multi-step reasoning and tool use. However, open-source efforts largely focus on text-dominant settings, leaving long-horizon multimodal tasks underexplored. This gap is evident in video tasks requiring sustained temporal understanding and iterative interaction. We present InternVideo3, a framework enhancing these capabilities via Multimodal Contextual Reasoning (MCR). MCR treats understanding as a closed-loop process over a shared, evolving context containing observations, instructions, reasoning, tool actions, and memory. This frames long-video understanding as evidence accumulation and verification. To ensure efficiency, we introduce Multimodal Multi-head Latent Attention (M^2LA), a token-preserving reparameterization compressing KV-cache states while retaining the full token stream. Our staged training includes continued pretraining, short-to-long supervised fine-tuning, rule-based reinforcement learning, and on-policy distillation. Experiments show InternVideo3 achieves strong performance on benchmarks like Video-MME, MLVU, and EgoSchema. We further instantiate the model as a video agent with retrieval tools, demonstrating robust evidence-grounded behavior. Our results suggest that efficient context handling and closed-loop reasoning are vital for adapting open multimodal models toward long-horizon visually grounded agency.

</details>


### [120] [SHERPA: Seam-aware Harmonized ERP Adaptation for Open-Domain 360$^\circ$ Panorama Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12213)
*Jungwoon Kang,Jaehun Kim,Yiwon Yu,Hyungyum Jang,Sanghoon Lee,Jongyoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SHERPA是一种轻量级适配框架，通过频率选择性Circular RoPE、循环潜编码/解码、图像侧FFN适配器和双路径训练方案，使平面预训练的文本到图像扩散/流模型能够生成高质量的360°全景图，支持写实和风格化两种模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景图在游戏、仿真等领域需求日益增长，但现有大规模文本到图像模型基于平面图像训练，与等距柱状投影(ERP)表示的360°全景图的环绕拓扑和极地区域存在结构不匹配问题，导致生成结果存在接缝和极地畸变。

Method: 提出SHERPA框架，包含四个关键组件：(1) 频率选择性Circular RoPE——仅替换对接缝敏感的高频水平RoPE波段为整数周期谐波，保留预训练的低频频谱；(2) 循环潜编码/解码(Circular Latent Encoding/Decoding)；(3) 图像侧FFN适配器；(4) 双路径训练方案——配对全景路径用于几何监督，非配对风格路径利用自监督偏航一致性实现无目标风格化提示。

Result: SHERPA能够在写实全景领域和开放域风格化提示下生成360°全景图，有效解决了接缝和极地畸变问题，同时保留了预训练模型的风格和语义先验。

Conclusion: SHERPA通过轻量级适配而非重新训练，高效地将平面预训练模型的能力迁移到360°全景生成任务，兼顾了写实与风格化两种需求，为全景内容生成提供了一种实用且高质量的解决方案。

Abstract: Panoramic imagery is increasingly used in world-generation, games, and simulation, where users may need not only photorealistic scenes but also stylized and non-photorealistic environments. Large-scale text-to-image diffusion and flow models provide broad style and semantic priors for this goal, but planar image training misaligns them with the wrap-around topology and polar regions of $360^\circ$ panoramas represented in equirectangular projection (ERP). We present SHERPA, a lightweight adaptation framework that combines frequency-selective Circular RoPE, Circular Latent Encoding/Decoding, image-side FFN adapters, and a Dual-Path Training Scheme. Circular RoPE replaces only the seam-sensitive high-frequency horizontal RoPE band with integer-periodic harmonics while preserving the pretrained lower-frequency spectrum. The Paired Panorama Path supervises geometry, while the Unpaired Style Path uses self-supervised yaw consistency for target-free stylized prompts. As a result, SHERPA generates $360^\circ$ panoramas across both photorealistic panorama domains and open-domain stylized prompts.

</details>


### [121] [MLT-Dedup: Efficient Large-Scale Online Video Deduplication via Multi-Level Representations and Spatial-Temporal Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12215)
*David Yuchen Wang,Haoying Li,Hailun Xu,Wei Chee Yew,Zirui Zhu,Sanjay Saha,Hao Hei,Kanchan Sarkar,Kun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MLT-Dedup，一种结合多层级表示与时空匹配的大规模在线视频去重框架。通过多层级视频编码器提取细粒度帧级嵌入和稀疏片段级嵌入，并设计DiF-SiM差分特征增强相似性模块定位重复片段。在真实大规模平台上，该框架在90%精度下将在线重复率降低91%，稀疏检索设计使索引容量提升5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频去重框架面临索引预算限制下难以检索足够高质量候选对象的问题，同时在效率与精度之间存在权衡。用户生成视频内容爆发式增长导致大量近重复视频出现，降低了用户体验并增加了存储和带宽成本。

Method: 提出MLT-Dedup框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 多层级视频编码器(ML-VE)，同时提取细粒度帧级嵌入和稀疏片段级嵌入，稀疏嵌入用于高效候选检索，细粒度嵌入用于精确成对匹配；2) 差分特征增强相似性模块(DiF-SiM)，能够定位重复时间片段并提供可靠的相似性证据以支持策略驱动的去重决策。

Result: 在真实大规模平台实验中，MLT-Dedup在90%精度下将在线重复率降低91%；稀疏检索设计使索引容量提升5倍，实现了更广泛的候选覆盖。

Conclusion: MLT-Dedup通过多层级表示和时空匹配有效解决了大规模在线视频去重中检索效率与精度之间的权衡问题，在实际部署中显著降低了重复率并提升了索引容量，具有较好的实用价值。

Abstract: The explosive growth of user-generated video content on online platforms is accompanied by the emergence of numerous near-duplicate videos--videos that are identical or highly similar but differ by partial edits. These duplicates degrade user experience and increase storage and bandwidth costs, making large-scale video deduplication a critical task. Existing video deduplication frameworks face a fundamental challenge in retrieving sufficient high-quality candidates under a limited index budget, as well as trade-offs between efficiency and precision. To address these issues, we propose MLT-Dedup, an efficient large-scale online video deduplication framework with Multi-Level representations and spatial-Temporal matching. Our approach employs a Multi-Level Video Encoder (ML-VE) to extract both fine-grained frame-level and sparse clip-level embeddings: sparse embeddings support efficient candidate retrieval, while fine-grained embeddings are loaded for precise pairwise matching. During matching, we introduce DiF-SiM, a Differential Feature-enhanced Similarity Module capable of locating duplicated temporal segments and providing reliable similarity evidence to support policy-driven deduplication decisions. Extensive experiments on a real-world large-scale platform demonstrate that MLT-Dedup reduces online repetition rates by 91% at 90% precision. Furthermore, our sparse retrieval design achieves a 5x increase in indexing capacity, enabling broader candidate coverage in real-world deployment.

</details>


### [122] [An Electric Potential-Augmented Benchmark Dataset for Physics-Guided Image Reconstruction of Electrical Capacitance Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12226)
*Xinqi Zhang,Qiming Ma,Lihui Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个融合电场势能信息的电容层析成像（ECT）基准数据集，该数据集在传统电容-介电常数映射基础上，额外提供了每个样本的8个激励电场势能图，并通过全面的分布内/分布外测试验证了引入电场势能信息对提升ECT建模精度与鲁棒性的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECT数据驱动方法将传感器视为黑箱，直接进行电容到介电常数的映射，忽略了电场势能这一连接非线性不适定"软场"效应的基本物理链路。

Method: 基于COMSOL-MATLAB管道，针对八电极传感器生成包含20,000个随机样本的数据集（覆盖四种典型流型），每个样本除常规电容向量和介电常数分布图像外，还保存了8个激励方向的完整电场势能图；并提供了正向与逆向问题的评估协议。

Result: 在分布内（IID）和分布外（OOD）场景下，系统地证明了引入电场势能图能够提升建模精度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 显式引入潜场信息显著降低了将物理定律融入ECT建模的门槛，为未来物理引导的机器学习ECT图像重建建立了标准化基础。

Abstract: While deep learning has significantly advanced image reconstruction of Electrical Capacitance Tomography (ECT), most data-driven methods map directly between capacitance and permittivity distribution, treating the sensor as a black box. This overlooks the electric potential field -- the fundamental physical link governing the nonlinear and ill-posed ``soft-field'' effect. To address this, we propose an electric potential-augmented ECT benchmark dataset designed to explicitly integrate latent physics behind ECT into the learning process. Generated via a COMSOL-MATLAB pipeline for an eight-electrode sensor as an example, the dataset comprises 20,000 randomized samples across four typical flow patterns. Crucially, alongside the conventional capacitance vectors and permittivity distributions depicted as images, each sample preserves eight excitation-wise full-field potential maps. Beyond data release, we provide illustrative evaluation protocols for both forward and inverse problems of ECT. Through comprehensive testing on both in-distribution (IID) and out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios, we systematically demonstrate how the inclusion of electric potential maps enhances modeling accuracy and robustness. Fundamentally, the explicit inclusion of latent field information significantly lowers the barrier to integrating physical laws into ECT modeling, thereby establishing a standardized foundation for future physics-guided machine learning of ECT image reconstruction.

</details>


### [123] [Damage-TriageFormer: A Foundation-Model Framework for Typology-Based Building Damage Assessment from Mono-Temporal Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12248)
*Yiming Xiao,Yu-Hsuan Ho,Sanjay Thasma,Junwei Ma,Ali Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Damage-TriageFormer，一种基于单张灾后影像、以建筑物足迹为条件的损伤类型分类模型，区别于传统的严重程度分级方法。贡献包括：构建了包含五个损伤类型类别的DamageTriage-Bench基准数据集（涵盖飓风Michael、Helene和2025年洛杉矶野火），以及采用DINOv3 ViT-L骨干网络、简单特征金字塔、两阶段门控损伤头与辅助回归目标的模型架构。在验证集和测试集上分别达到0.624和0.619的宏F1，其中无损建筑和整体结构倒塌的F1分别达0.91和0.84。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统建筑损伤评估方法存在两个主要问题：一是将损伤扁平化为单一严重程度等级（无损、轻微、严重、倒塌），无法区分损伤类型（如屋顶损伤与结构损伤、部分损伤与全面损伤）；二是依赖灾前-灾后配对影像，在紧急响应中往往不可用。因此需要一种仅基于单张灾后影像、且能输出损伤类型细粒度分类的方法以支持应急资源分配。

Method: 采用DINOv3 ViT-L作为骨干网络进行特征提取；引入Simple Feature Pyramid（简单特征金字塔）实现更高分辨率的实例池化；设计两阶段门控损伤头（two-stage gated damage head）进行损伤类型分类；添加辅助严重程度回归目标（auxiliary severity-regression objective）以增强学习。模型以建筑物足迹为条件，仅输入单张灾后影像即可输出五种损伤类型标签。

Result: 在验证集上达到宏F1为0.624，在分层留出测试集上达到0.619。其中，无损建筑物F1为0.91，整体结构倒塌F1为0.84，均为高优先级类别。屋顶全面损伤类因样本稀少且标签边界模糊而表现较差。

Conclusion: 单张灾后影像足以支撑可操作的建筑损伤类型分类，无需灾前参考影像即可实现针对性应急响应和资源分配。

Abstract: Decision-relevant building damage assessment is critical for prioritizing resources and recovery after a disaster, yet most automated methods either flatten damage into a single severity scale (no damage, minor, major, destroyed) or require paired pre- and post-event imagery that is often unavailable for emerging hazards. This paper presents Damage-TriageFormer, a single-image, post-event, footprint-conditioned model that produces a damage typology rather than a severity scale. We contribute: (1) DamageTriage-Bench, a new benchmark built from NOAA Emergency Response Imagery across Hurricane Michael (2018), Hurricane Helene (2024), and the 2025 Los Angeles wildfire complex, with five typology classes that distinguish roof damage from structural damage and, within each, partial from total extent; and (2) Damage-TriageFormer, which extends a DINOv3 ViT-L backbone with a Simple Feature Pyramid for higher-resolution instance pooling, a two-stage gated damage head, and an auxiliary severity-regression objective. Our model achieves macro F1 of 0.624 on validation and 0.619 on a held-out stratified test set, performing strongest where operational triage needs it most, with per-class F1 of 0.91 and 0.84 on undamaged buildings and total structural collapse, respectively. While the rare Total Roof Damage class remains difficult due to its limited examples and an inherently ambiguous label boundary, our results show that single-image post-event imagery can support actionable building damage typing, enabling targeted emergency response and resource allocation without a pre-event reference.

</details>


### [124] [Bridging Day and Night: Unsupervised Cross-Domain Re-Identification with Synergistic Prompt and Prototype Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12258)
*Jiyang Xu,Rui Liu,Hang Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无监督的跨域昼夜行人重识别框架，结合提示学习和原型表示学习，通过两阶段训练策略（第一阶段利用视觉-语言模型生成实例级文本提示并进行对齐，第二阶段构建域特定原型记忆库并设计域内身份关联与跨域原型匹配模块），在无标注条件下取得了与全监督方法可比的Rank-1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 昼夜场景间巨大的视觉外观差异使得跨域昼夜行人重识别极具挑战，现有全监督方法依赖昂贵的人工标注且跨域泛化能力有限，因此探索无监督的昼夜行人重识别方法具有重要的实际意义。

Method: 提出两阶段训练框架：第一阶段利用预训练视觉-语言模型以无标注方式生成实例级文本提示，通过实例级对齐机制将视觉特征与文本提示嵌入统一语义空间，并采用实例感知动态偏置适配实现对齐；第二阶段构建域特定原型记忆库，设计域内身份关联模块增强特征判别性，以及跨域原型匹配模块识别正负原型对以建立跨域身份对应关系。

Result: 在公开基准上的大量实验验证了方法的有效性，在无监督设定下达到了与最先进全监督方法可比的Rank-1准确率。

Conclusion: 所提无监督框架有效克服了昼夜跨域行人重识别中视觉差异大、缺乏标注的难题，为实际应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Cross-domain day-night re-identification (ReID) is fundamentally challenged by the substantial visual appearance discrepancies between daytime and nighttime scenes. Existing fully supervised methods rely heavily on labor-intensive annotations, which are costly and exhibit limited generalization across domains. In this work, we investigate unsupervised day-night ReID and propose a novel framework that synergistically combines prompt learning and prototype-based representation learning to associate identities across domains without requiring manual labels. Our approach follows a progressive two-stage training strategy. In the first stage, we exploit the vision-language model to generate instance-specific textual prompts in an annotation-free manner. We employ an instance-level alignment mechanism to embed visual features and textual prompts into a unified semantic space, aligning unlabeled day/night images with learnable prompts via instance-aware dynamic-bias adaptation. In the second stage, we construct domain-specific prototype memory banks and introduce two complementary modules: i) an intra-domain identity association module to enhance feature discriminability within each domain, and ii) a cross-domain prototype matching module to reliably identify positive and negative prototype pairs, thereby establishing robust identity correspondences across day and night. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks validate the effectiveness of our method. Under the unsupervised setting, our framework attains Rank-1 accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art fully supervised methods.

</details>


### [125] [VOID: Defeating Unauthorized Mimicry in Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12263)
*Chunlin Qiu,Ang Li,Tianxiao Huang,Ruilin Gan,Yunjie Ge,Shenyi Zhang,Huayi Duan,Lingchen Zhao,Chao Shen,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对潜在扩散模型（LDMs）被用于未经授权模仿个人肖像的安全问题，本文提出VOID防御框架。该框架通过操纵LDM的内在随机性，从两个方面扰动扩散管道：放大潜在编码错误以破坏图像的语义结构，以及抵消目标引导信号以抑制模型的恢复能力。VOID在有效阻碍未经授权模仿的同时，将扰动限制在人眼不可感知的区域，不损害视觉效用。在5个数据集、10种模仿攻击、24种最先进防御方法的全面评估中，VOID将平均FID从113提升至365，比此前最强防御提升了223%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LDM未经授权模仿的防御方法依赖于注入欺骗性扰动，但其前提假设——微小扰动能在LDM的整个生成过程中保持欺骗效果——是缺乏根据的。实际上，LDM内在的恢复机制会消除这些扰动，导致个体身份在生成图像中重新出现。因此，需要一种能够真正对抗LDM恢复能力的新型防御框架。

Method: VOID通过操纵LDM的内在随机性，在扩散管道中实施两种新型扰动：1) 放大潜在编码错误，破坏图像的语义结构；2) 抵消目标引导信号，抑制模型的恢复能力。这种双重扰动导致语义腐败，从而阻止任何未经授权的模仿行为。同时，VOID将扰动限制在受保护图像的人眼不可感知区域，以维护视觉效用。

Result: 在5个数据集上，针对10种模仿攻击，与24种最先进防御方法对比，VOID将平均FID从113提升至365，比此前最强防御提升了223%，展现了前所未有的保护能力。

Conclusion: VOID通过利用LDM自身的随机性机制，而非依赖脆弱的欺骗性扰动，有效解决了现有防御方法被LDM恢复机制破解的根本问题，在不牺牲视觉质量的前提下实现了更强的防护效果。

Abstract: While Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) have revolutionized visual synthesis, they are increasingly exploited for unauthorized mimicry of individuals. Existing defenses inject deceptive perturbations to steer the generated images toward irrelevant targets. However, this approach hinges on an ungrounded assumption: subtle perturbations can maintain their deceptive efficacy throughout an LDM's extensive generation process. In reality, the model's innate restoration mechanism will remove such perturbations and cause individual identities to re-emerge in the images generated.
  We propose VOID, a defense framework that overcomes this conundrum by manipulating an LDM's intrinsic stochasticity. VOID perturbs the diffusion pipeline in two novel ways: 1) amplifying the latent encoding errors to shatter an image's semantic structure, and 2) counteracting the target guidance signals to suppress the model's restoration capabilities. This results in a semantic corruption that thwarts any unauthorized mimicry. Notably, the security gain does not come at the price of visual utility, as VOID simultaneously manages to confine perturbations to human-imperceptible regions of protected images. Our comprehensive evaluation of 24 state-of-the-art defenses against 10 mimicry attacks on 5 datasets demonstrates VOID's unprecedented protection power: it increases the average Frechet Inception Distance (FID) from 113 to 365, a 223% improvement over the strongest defense to date.

</details>


### [126] [Finding Sparse Subnetworks in One Training Cycle via Progressive Magnitude-Based Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12278)
*Romana Qureshi,Hafida Benhidour,Said Kerrache,Nahlah Aljeraisy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种渐进式幅度剪枝方法作为单周期剪枝替代方案，该方法在训练过程中使用线性调度逐步增加稀疏度，并在CIFAR-10和MNIST数据集上对ResNet、VGG-style和LeNet架构进行系统实验。实验表明，该方法在多个稀疏度水平上优于LTH、SNIP和GraSP等基线方法，在ResNet-18上达到95.12%准确率（稀疏度72.9%），并在70-85%稀疏度范围内准确率与稠密基线相差不超过0.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有剪枝方法如彩票假设(LTH)需要多次完整训练周期的迭代剪枝过程，计算成本高昂。本文旨在探索一种只需单次训练周期即可实现有效网络稀疏化的渐进式幅度剪枝方法。

Method: 采用渐进式幅度剪枝方法，在训练过程中使用线性调度逐步增加网络稀疏度，并根据活跃权重的幅度动态更新剪枝掩码，仅需单次训练周期即可完成剪枝。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，ResNet-18在72.9%稀疏度下达到95.12%准确率（优于LTH的90.5%）；VGG-like架构在97%稀疏度下达到93.13%准确率（优于SNIP的92.0%）；VGG-19在97.97%稀疏度下达到93.44%准确率（优于GraSP的92.19%）。在70-85%稀疏度范围内，准确率与稠密基线相差不超过0.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 渐进式幅度剪枝方法在评估设置下提供了一种有效的单周期神经网络稀疏化方案，能够在保持高精度的同时显著减少模型规模，且训练效率优于需要多周期训练的迭代剪枝方法。

Abstract: Neural network pruning reduces model size by removing less important parameters while aiming to preserve predictive performance. Although the Lottery Ticket Hypothesis (LTH) shows that sparse subnetworks can match dense networks when trained from suitable initializations, its iterative pruning procedure requires multiple complete training cycles. This work evaluates progressive magnitude-based pruning as a single-cycle alternative. The method gradually increases sparsity during training using a linear schedule and updates pruning masks based on active weight magnitudes. We conduct systematic experiments on CIFAR-10 and MNIST across ResNet, VGG-style, and LeNet architectures, comparing the proposed method with representative iterative and initialization-based pruning baselines, including LTH, SNIP, and GraSP. On CIFAR-10, the method achieves 95.12\% accuracy on ResNet-18 at 72.9\% sparsity, compared with 90.5\% reported for LTH. At extreme sparsity, it achieves 93.13\% accuracy on a VGG-like architecture at 97\% sparsity, compared with approximately 92.0\% for SNIP, and 93.44\% accuracy on VGG-19 at 97.97\% sparsity, compared with 92.19\% for GraSP at 98\% sparsity. A sparsity-accuracy analysis on ResNet-18 further shows that accuracy remains within 0.1 percentage points of the dense baseline across 70--85\% sparsity. These results indicate that progressive magnitude-based pruning provides an effective single-cycle approach for neural network sparsification under the evaluated settings.

</details>


### [127] [CellNet -- Localizing Cells using Sparse and Noisy Point Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12286)
*Benjamin Eckhardt,Dmytro Fishman,Stuart Fawke,Andrew Curtis,Bo Fussing,Constantin Pape*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于回归的深度学习算法CellNet，利用稀疏点标注在相衬显微镜图像中检测和计数活细胞，以减少标注工作量。在低数据场景下，该方法相比零样本方法表现更优，已应用于Wellcome Sanger研究所的大规模饱和基因组编辑筛选研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生物学研究中，活细胞计数是许多工作流程的关键步骤，特别是在Wellcome Sanger研究所进行的大规模饱和基因组编辑筛选研究中需要反复计数大量细胞。传统标注方法耗时费力，成为效率瓶颈，因此需要一种自动化且标注成本低的细胞计数方法。

Method: 开发了一种基于回归的深度学习计算机视觉算法（CellNet），使用稀疏点标注（快速易获取）作为监督信号，在相衬显微镜图像中检测和计数细胞，而非依赖密集分割或检测框标注。

Result: 通过与最先进的零样本方法对比，证明基于回归的计数方法在低数据场景下是一种有前景的替代方案，在减少标注工作的同时实现了有效的细胞计数。

Conclusion: 该工作为人类基因组研究提供了有价值的自动化细胞计数工具，降低了标注成本，提高了高通量筛选的效率。代码已开源。

Abstract: Counting living cells is an important step in many biological research workflows. Our collaborators at the Wellcome Sanger Institute study vital genes in humans via large scale saturation genome editing screening, which requires repeatedly counting cells a great number of times. Computer Vision based automation is crucial for high throughput and resource efficiency. In this work, we develop a regression-based deep learning computer vision algorithm to detect and count cells in phase-contrast microscopy images. To reduce annotation effort, which in practice often becomes a bottleneck, we focus on counting cells only using sparse point annotations, which are fast and easy to acquire. By comparison to state-of-the-art 0-shot methods, we show that regression-based counting is a promising alternative in low data regimes. Through developing methods to automatically count living cells in microscopy images, we contribute to valuable research on the human genome. The code is available at https://github.com/beijn/cellnet.

</details>


### [128] [Bridging the Modality Gap in Forensic Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12294)
*Ricardo González-Gazapo,Annette Morales-González,Yoanna Martínez-Díaz,Heydi Méndez-Vázquez,Milton García-Borroto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的多模态检索框架，应用于四项法医关键任务（文身图像检索、基于文本描述的文身检索、手绘素描文身检索、法医面部素描检索），利用多模态大语言模型自动生成结构化文本描述，并通过视觉嵌入、文本嵌入及多模态融合策略提升检索精度与鲁棒性，尤其在视觉信息有限或噪声场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要关注多模态检索系统的开发与优化，但缺乏对其在多样化真实法医场景中适用性的系统评估。

Method: 采用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）自动为所有查询和库图像生成结构化文本描述，再通过sentence-transformer进行文本嵌入比对；同时使用视觉嵌入、文本嵌入以及结合文本与图像相似度得分的多模态融合策略进行检索。

Result: 多模态融合策略在所有任务中一致提升了检索精度与鲁棒性，尤其在视觉信息有限或噪声场景（如素描、局部文身、碎片化目击陈述）下效果显著。

Conclusion: 统一的多模态检索流水线具有法医应用价值，现代MLLM能够将传统依赖人工专家分析的法医任务操作化，多模态检索可成为支持文身、面部合成图像及目击描述调查工作流的有效工具。

Abstract: Automated image retrieval plays an increasingly critical role in modern forensic analysis, supporting investigative workflows that rely on efficient comparison of visual evidence. While prior work has focused primarily on developing and optimizing multimodal retrieval systems, limited attention has been paid to evaluating the forensic applicability of these technologies across diverse real-world scenarios. In this study, we present a unified retrieval framework adapted to four key forensic tasks: (1) tattoo image retrieval given a tattoo query image; (2) tattoo retrieval guided by human-expert textual descriptions, modelling the common situation where a witness verbally describes a tattoo; (3) tattoo retrieval from hand-drawn sketches; and (4) face retrieval from forensic face sketches. Our system leverages a multimodal large language model (MLLM) to automatically generate structured textual descriptions for all queries and gallery images, followed by sentence-transformer embedding for text-based comparison. We evaluate retrieval using visual-only embeddings, text-only embeddings and a multimodal fusion strategy that combines text- and image-based similarity scores derived from state-of-the-art visual feature extractors relevant to each task. The fusion of modalities consistently improves retrieval precision and robustness, especially in scenarios where visual information is limited or noisy (e.g., sketches, partial tattoos, or fragmented witness statements). This work highlights the forensic value of a unified multimodal retrieval pipeline and demonstrates how modern MLLMs can operationalize challenging forensic tasks that traditionally rely on manual expert analysis. Our results position multimodal retrieval as a promising tool for supporting investigative workflows involving tattoos, facial composites, and witness descriptions.

</details>


### [129] [Findings of the MAGMaR 2026 Shared Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12295)
*Alexander Martin,Dengjia Zhang,Joel Brogan,Francis Ferraro,Jeremy Gwinnup,Reno Kriz,Teng Long,Kenton Murray,Andrew Yates,Xiang Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文总结了MAGMaR第二届研讨会共享任务的成果，包含视频检索和基于检索视频的生成两项子任务。检索任务中2个团队提交的17个系统均超越了去年冠军基线；生成任务中4个团队提交的16个系统，每个团队至少有一个被人类标注者评为最佳的报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织多模态检索与生成领域的共享任务，推动视频检索和基于检索视频的生成技术发展，并为不同方法提供公平对比的平台。

Method: 设置两项子任务：(i) 视频检索，(ii) 基于检索到的视频进行文章的接地生成。参赛团队可选择参与其中一项或两项。检索任务以去年冠军作为基线，生成任务由人类标注者评估生成报告的质量。

Result: 检索任务：2个团队提交17个系统，全部超越去年冠军基线。生成任务：4个团队提交16个系统，每个团队均至少有一个生成报告被人类标注者评为最佳。

Conclusion: 该共享任务表明视频检索和基于视频的生成领域取得了显著进展，所有参赛系统均超越基线，且生成质量获得了人类评估者的认可。

Abstract: This overview paper presents the results of the shared task for the second workshop on Multimodal Augmented Generation via Multimodal Retrieval (MAGMaR). In this shared task participants submitted systems focused on either (i) video retrieval or (ii) grounded generation of articles given retrieved videos. Teams could submit to either task. For the retrieval task, we had 2 participating teams that submitted a total of 17 systems -- all of which beat a baseline derived from the winner of last year's shared task. On the generation side, we had 4 teams submit 16 systems. All teams had at least one generated report that was labeled the best by a human annotator.

</details>


### [130] [From 2D Grids to 1D Tokens: Reforming Shared Representations for Multimodal Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12303)
*Yuchen Xian,Yunqiu Xu,Yang He,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于1D token接口的多模态图像融合方法，通过选择性Token编辑（STE）在保持局部细节的同时实现全局外观一致性，在四个基准数据集上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法基于2D特征网格构建共享表示，擅长建模局部结构但难以控制图像级全局外观因素，需要一种能同时兼顾局部保真度和全局一致性的融合框架。

Method: 引入冻结的预训练图像分词器构建紧凑的1D token接口作为全局载体，保留2D空间路径用于局部结构恢复；提出选择性Token编辑（STE），稀疏更新/替换少量关键token，以轻量级方式引导全局外观一致性，无需改变融合骨干网络或添加额外损失。

Result: 在四个常用基准数据集上达到最佳综合性能，在全局一致性和局部保真度上均实现一致的多指标提升。

Conclusion: 1D token空间作为全局载体结合2D局部路径的设计能有效平衡多模态融合中的局部细节保留与全局外观一致性，STE机制提供了一种轻量高效的全局外观控制手段。

Abstract: Multimodal image fusion aims to integrate complementary information from different modalities into a fused image that preserves rich local details while maintaining globally consistent appearance. Existing approaches build shared representations on 2D feature grids, which excel at modeling local structures but offer limited leverage over image-level global appearance factors. To balance these objectives, we introduce a compact 1D token interface based on a frozen pretrained image tokenizer for modeling non-local appearance/base factors. Rather than using the tokenizer as a reconstruction backbone, our design uses the 1D token space as a global carrier while retaining the 2D spatial pathway for local structure restoration. Specifically, we introduce Selective Token Editing (STE), which sparsely updates/replaces a small set of critical tokens, providing a lightweight mechanism to steer global appearance coherence while keeping the fusion backbone unchanged and avoiding extra losses. Experiments on four commonly used benchmarks show that our method achieves the best overall performance, with consistent, multi-metric improvements in both global coherence and local fidelity. Project page: https://zju-xyc.github.io/1D-Fusion-Project-Page/

</details>


### [131] [Slots, Transitions, Loops: Learning Composable World Models for ARC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12316)
*Gege Gao,Bernhard Schölkopf,Andreas Geiger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Loop-OWM是一种面向对象的视觉符号世界建模架构，通过循环转换模型学习ARC任务中的规则，在ARC-1和ARC-2数据集上优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARC任务本质上是视觉符号性的，规则表现为网格中对象、颜色、形状和空间关系的转换，而现有方法多通过语言、代码或符号程序表达规则，未能充分利用其视觉符号特性。

Method: 提出Loop-OWM架构，包含颜色原型槽（color-prototype slots）、演示条件任务摘要（demonstration-conditioned task summaries）、循环转换模型（looped transition model），以及密集传播（dense propagation）和槽条件校正（slot-conditioned correction）机制。

Result: 在ARC-1和ARC-2数据集上，Loop-OWM以相当或更少的参数量，优于非循环和循环基线方法。

Conclusion: ARC规则不仅可以作为语言描述或搜索程序来学习，也可以作为视觉符号世界状态的转换来学习。

Abstract: ARC tests in-context rule induction: given a few input-output demonstrations, a model must infer the hidden rule and apply it to a new query. While many approaches express ARC rules through language, code, or symbolic programs, ARC itself is visual-symbolic: rules appear as grid transitions over objects, colors, shapes, and spatial relations. We introduce Loop-OWM, an object-centric world-modeling architecture that learns these rules as composable transitions over structured states. It combines color-prototype slots, demonstration-conditioned task summaries, and a looped transition model with dense propagation and slot-conditioned correction. On both ARC-1 and ARC-2, Loop-OWM outperforms non-looped and looped baselines with comparable or fewer parameters. These results suggest that ARC rules can be learned not only as language descriptions or searched programs, but also as transitions over visual-symbolic world states.

</details>


### [132] [Anatomically Conditioned Recurrent Refinement for Topology-Aware Circle of Willis Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12319)
*Juraj Perić,Marija Habijan,Dario Mužević,Irena Galić,Danilo Babin,Aleksandra Pižurica*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AC2RUNet，通过双流架构（静态流+动态流）和动态课程学习策略，在MRA的Willis环分割任务中显著减少血管断裂伪影，提升拓扑连接性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CNN在分割Willis环时因复杂拓扑和薄血管结构导致"血管断裂"伪影，现有方法难以捕捉拓扑约束。

Method: 1) 双流架构：静态流提取不变解剖特征，轻量动态流迭代修正拓扑错误；2) 动态课程学习：从高召回几何监督过渡到拓扑感知约束。

Result: 在TopCoW数据集上，Hausdorff距离从9.17mm降至4.72mm，Betti数误差从0.40降至0.19，体积Dice与nnU-Net基线相当。

Conclusion: AC2RUNet在保持分割精度的同时有效改善了拓扑连通性，为脑血管分割中拓扑完整性问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Segmenting the Circle of Willis (CoW) from Magnetic Resonance Angiography (MRA) is challenging due to complex topology and thin vascular structures that are prone to fragmentation. Standard Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) often fail to capture these topological constraints, resulting in "broken vessel" artifacts. To address this, we propose the Anatomically Conditioned Recurrent Refinement U-Net (AC2RUNet). Our architecture decouples segmentation into two streams: a Static Stream that extracts invariant anatomical features and a lightweight Dynamic Stream that iteratively refines topological errors over time. We further introduce a dynamic curriculum learning strategy that transitions from high-recall geometric supervision to topology-aware constraints. Validated on the TopCoW dataset, AC2RUNet substantially reduces Hausdorff Distance (4.72 mm vs 9.17 mm) and Betti number errors (0.19 vs 0.40), improving topological connectivity over the nnU-Net baseline while maintaining comparable volumetric Dice.

</details>


### [133] [Echoes of the Prior: A Computational Phenomenology of Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12340)
*Gege Gao,Bernhard Schölkopf,Andreas Geiger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为"Echoes of the Prior"的交互装置，通过在前馈3D重建模型中诱导受控的突触衰减，模拟人类遗忘的主观现象学体验，将神经网络定位为认知代理而非工程工具，以探索神经形态美学在可视化智能脆弱性方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过艺术化方式可视化遗忘的主观现象学体验，探索当生物记忆衰退时世界如何退化为不可识别的混乱状态。

Method: 在前馈3D重建模型中诱导受控的突触衰减，以此类比大脑预测先验的侵蚀过程，将神经网络作为认知代理来模拟遗忘的体验。

Result: 生成了一个交互装置，通过神经网络的受控结构退化来唤起迷失方向感、诗意且令人恐惧的遗忘体验。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为催化剂，邀请更广泛的社区探索神经形态美学在可视化智能脆弱性方面的未开发潜力。

Abstract: Memory is not merely the storage of data; it is the scaffolding of reality. When biological memory fades, the world does not simply turn black; it regresses into an unrecognizable chaos. Echoes of the Prior is an interactive installation that attempts to visualize this subjective phenomenology of forgetting. By inducing controlled synaptic decay within a Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction model, we create an artistic analogy for the erosion of the brain's predictive priors. We position the Neural Network not as a tool for engineering, but as a cognitive proxy - a silicon brain whose structural degeneration evokes the disorienting, poetic, and terrifying experience of losing one's grip on the world. Ultimately, we offer this framework as a catalyst, inviting the wider community to explore the uncharted potential of neuromorphic aesthetics in visualizing the fragility of intelligence. Interactive demo see https://decart-4d.github.io/.

</details>


### [134] [DepthMaster: Unified Monocular Depth Estimation for Perspective and Panoramic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12368)
*Pengfei Wang,Shihao Wang,Liyi Chen,Zhiyuan Ma,Guowen Zhang,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DepthMaster是一个统一的度量深度估计框架，通过将全景图分解为重叠透视块、引入对应一致性损失（CCL）和虚拟投影相机几何先验，无需专门网络即可在窄视场角和360°全景图上实现通用度量深度估计，仅使用一个全景数据集训练即在13个数据集上达到SOTA零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目深度估计方法往往针对特定相机类型（透视或全景）设计，难以在不同设置间泛化。其核心障碍在于：透视相机与全景相机之间存在固有的几何差异，且带有度量标注的全景训练数据极度稀缺。

Method: 1) 将全景图像分解为重叠的透视块，统一为规范的透视表示，消除几何差异；2) 提出对应一致性损失（CCL）实现无缝拼接，避免专门操作符；3) 注入虚拟投影相机作为几何先验，保持骨干网络与标准Transformer设计兼容；4) 利用大量透视数据集中的度量先验，缓解全景数据稀缺问题。

Result: 在仅包含一个全景数据集的混合数据上训练，DepthMaster在13个多样化数据集上达到SOTA零样本性能，不仅超越通用方法，还领先透视和全景领域的专用模型。

Conclusion: 通过将全景深度估计重新定义为透视块拼接问题，并引入CCL和虚拟投影先验，DepthMaster成功统一了透视与全景的度量深度估计框架，无需专门网络架构即可实现跨场景泛化。

Abstract: While monocular depth estimation has achieved significant progress, achieving generalized metric depth estimation for both narrow field-of-view (FoV) perspectives and $360^\circ$ panoramas remains an unsolved challenge. Existing methods are often tailored to specific camera types and struggle to produce accurate metric depth that generalizes across diverse settings. This limitation stems from two key challenges: the inherent geometric discrepancy between perspective and panoramic cameras, and the scarcity of panoramic training data with metric annotations. In this work, we introduce DepthMaster, a unified metric depth estimation framework. Rather than employing specialized networks to learn spherical distortions, we reformulate the problem by decomposing panoramic images into overlapping perspective patches. Crucially, distinct from prior projection-based methods that rely on ad-hoc architectural modifications to handle boundaries, we introduce a novel Correspondence Consistency Loss (CCL) and inject virtual projection cameras as geometric priors, allowing us to seamlessly stitch the patches while avoiding specialized operators and keeping the backbone largely compatible with standard Transformer designs. This strategy also resolves the geometric differences by unifying all inputs into a canonical perspective representation, and effectively circumvents data scarcity by directly unlocking powerful metric priors from vast perspective datasets. Trained on a mixed dataset that contains only one panorama dataset, DepthMaster achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on 13 diverse datasets, outperforming not only universal methods but also leading specialist models in both perspective and panoramic domains.

</details>


### [135] [A Turbo-Inference Strategy for Object Detection and Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12371)
*Zhen Zhao,Gang Zhang,Xiaolin Hu,Liang Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对自上而下实例分割方法的涡轮推理策略，通过设计涡轮检测头和涡轮分割头两个模块，在检测与分割任务间形成闭环通信，无需重新训练模型即可迭代利用两者间的互补信息，从而在COCO、iFLYTEK和Cityscapes数据集上显著提升检测和分割精度，代价是计算成本增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自上而下的实例分割方法遵循"先检测后分割"范式，检测精度直接影响分割性能，但少有研究探索实例分割任务对目标检测的反向促进作用。作者希望利用检测与分割任务间的互补信息来相互提升。

Method: 提出涡轮推理策略，设计两个模块：涡轮检测头（turbo-detection head）和涡轮分割头（turbo-segmentation head），两者形成闭环，在检测和分割结果之间建立通信，无需重新训练模型即可迭代交互。

Result: 在COCO、iFLYTEK和Cityscapes数据集上的实验表明，该方法在增加一定计算成本的前提下，显著提升了检测和分割的准确性。

Conclusion: 该方法在预测精度和推理速度之间提供了可权衡的方案，实现了检测与分割任务的相互增强。

Abstract: Object detection and instance segmentation tasks are closely related. Existing top-down instance segmentation methods usually follow a detect-then-segment paradigm, where an initial detector is used to recognize and localize objects with bounding boxes, followed by the segmentation of an instance mask within each bounding box. In such methods, the detection accuracy directly influences the subsequent segmentation performance. However, previous research has seldom explored the impact of the instance segmentation task on object detection. In this paper, we present a turbo-inference strategy for the top-down methods that leverages the complementary information between detection and segmentation tasks iteratively. Specifically we design two modules: turbo-detection head and turbo-segmentation head, which facilitate communication between the tasks. The two modules form a closed loop that interlaces the detection and segmentation results without retraining the model. Comprehensive experiments on the COCO, iFLYTEK, and Cityscapes datasets demonstrate that our method substantially enhances both detection and segmentation accuracies with a certain increase in computational cost. The proposed method represents a tradeoff between prediction accuracy and inference speed. Codes are available at https://github.com/zhaozhen2333/Turbo-Learning.git.

</details>


### [136] [VLGA: Vision-Language-Geometry-Action Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12396)
*Jin Yao,Dhruva Dixith Kurra,Tom Lampo,Zezhou Cheng,Danhua Guo,Burhan Yaman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLGA是首个以重建稠密3D驾驶世界为监督信号的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过将几何作为第四模态并引入逐像素点图回归损失，在nuScenes和Bench2Drive数据集上达到了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽然能通过语言描述和理解场景，但在稠密3D世界中执行动作时仍存在 grounding 困难。已有方法要么使用冻结的3D基础模型注入特征但缺乏确保策略利用这些特征的目标函数，要么使用稀疏的边界框和地图损失约束几何但无法提供稠密的空间信号。

Method: VLGA将几何作为第四模态（与视觉、语言、动作并列），通过专用专家网络和逐像素点图回归损失（以LiDAR为真值）进行监督，使模型能够重建驾驶环境中的稠密3D世界。

Result: 在开放循环的nuScenes上达到VLA方法SOTA（平均L2误差0.50m，3秒碰撞率0.18%）；在闭环Bench2Drive上达到驾驶得分79.08，比最强先前VLA高出0.71，且效率和舒适度相当。

Conclusion: 引入稠密3D重建作为监督信号能有效提升VLA模型在自动驾驶环境中的动作grounding能力，证明了几何模态在VLA框架中的价值。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can describe scenes and reason about them in language, yet still struggle to ground their actions in the dense 3D world around them. Existing approaches either inject features from a frozen 3D foundation model without an objective that ensures the policy uses them, or constrain geometry with sparse box and map losses that provide no dense spatial signal. We introduce VLGA, the first vision-language-action model supervised to reconstruct the dense 3D world it drives through. VLGA introduces geometry as a fourth modality alongside vision, language, and action through a dedicated expert supervised by a per-pixel pointmap regression loss against LiDAR. Extensive experiments conducted on challenging nuScenes and Bench2Drive datasets for open-loop and closed-loop evaluations, respectively, show the superiority of VLGA over counterpart VLA methods. In particular, on open-loop nuScenes, VLGA sets a new state of the art among VLA methods without ego status, with the lowest L2 (0.50\,m average) and 3-second collision rate (0.18\%). On closed-loop Bench2Drive, VLGA attains the state-of-the-art driving score of 79.08, +0.71 over the strongest prior VLA, at comparable efficiency and comfort.

</details>


### [137] [How Seemingly Inconsequential Design Choices Dictate Performance of LLMs in Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12407)
*Kian R. Weihrauch,Thomas A. Buckley,William Lotter,Arjun K. Manrai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统分析发现，过往研究中通用大语言模型在病理全切片图像任务上表现不佳，主要是由于输入配置（补丁大小、放大倍数、数量等）选择不当所致。通过优化配置（大补丁、低放大倍数、联合处理），GPT-5在癌症分类任务上从15.1%提升至39.5%，器官分类从38.1%提升至62.9%，且该配置可跨模型泛化到其他模型和数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型在病理WSI任务中一直被视为基线模型且表现低于专用模型，但以往研究未系统评估输入设计因素（如补丁大小、数量、放大倍数等）对性能的影响，可能低估了LLM的实际能力。作者旨在通过系统因素分析揭示这一潜在的方法学偏差。

Method: 采用全因素实验设计，系统分析四个输入设计因素：推理模式（独立投票 vs 联合处理）、补丁大小、放大倍数、补丁数量。在MultiPathQA基准（TCGA和GTEx数据集）上进行评估，并跨模型（GPT-5、Gemini 3 Flash等）和跨数据集（CPTAC）验证泛化性。

Result: 单一均衡配置（大补丁、低放大倍数、联合处理）使GPT-5在TCGA上从15.1%提升至39.5%，在GTEx上从38.1%提升至62.9%。逐任务优化进一步达到43.9%（TCGA）和71.6%（GTEx）。该配置在CPTAC上使Gemini 3 Flash提升23.4个百分点，无需任何任务特定调优。

Conclusion: 先前研究夸大了专用模型与通用LLM之间的差距；通用LLM在病理WSI任务上的潜力被严重低估，主要源于非优化的输入配置。系统评估输入设计因素对于公平比较不同模型至关重要。

Abstract: General-purpose large language models (LLMs) are routinely used as baselines when evaluating specialized pathology models on whole-slide images (WSIs). Because WSIs exceed contemporary model context limits, LLM baselines routinely use small, high-magnification patches processed independently via majority voting, without systematic evaluation of seemingly inconsequential design choices such as patch size, patch count, and magnification. Generalist LLMs have consistently underperformed specialized systems, reinforcing the perception that domain-specific training or architectural adaptation is necessary for pathology tasks involving WSIs. Here, we conduct a systematic factorial analysis of four input design factors: inference mode, patch size, magnification, and patch count. We demonstrate that prior studies have overstated the gap between specialized models and general-purpose LLMs by choosing non-optimized input configurations. On the MultiPathQA benchmark, switching to a single balanced configuration (large patches at lower magnification, processed jointly) raises GPT-5 from 15.1% to 39.5% on cancer-type classification (TCGA) and from 38.1% to 62.9% on organ classification (GTEx). Per-task optimization yields further gains up to 43.9% (TCGA) and 71.6% (GTEx). The same configuration generalizes to two other models and to a fully held-out CPTAC cohort, where it improves Gemini 3 Flash by 23.4 percentage points without any task-specific tuning.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [When Poison Fails After Retrieval: Revisiting Corpus Poisoning under Chunking and Reranking Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11265)
*Xi Nie,Hongwei Li,Shenghao Wu,Mingxuan Li,Jiachen Li,Wenbo Jiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有RAG投毒攻击在真实多阶段检索流水线中效果大幅下降的问题，并提出了一种联合优化检索相关性、重排一致性及分块边界鲁棒性的攻击框架CRCP，实现了更高的攻击成功率和更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG投毒攻击研究多在简化的检索设置下评估，忽略了实际RAG流程中包含的分块、稠密检索、重排和生成等多个阶段，导致攻击效果评估与真实场景存在差距。

Method: 提出CRCP框架，显式建模分块变换过程以生成局部自包含的对抗性段落，同时联合优化检索相关性、重排一致性和分块边界鲁棒性。

Result: 在标准RAG基准上使用多种检索器和重排器的实验表明，现有投毒方法对分块大小和重排策略高度敏感，而CRCP实现了更高的攻击成功率和更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RAG系统的投毒攻击应被视为一个多阶段检索一致性问题而非单纯的检索问题，突出了当前RAG安全评估中存在的现实差距。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems are vulnerable to corpus poisoning attacks that manipulate downstream model outputs through malicious knowledge injection. Existing studies mainly evaluate poisoning under simplified retrieval settings, overlooking practical RAG pipelines involving document chunking, dense retrieval, reranking, and grounded generation. In this paper, we revisit corpus poisoning under realistic multi-stage retrieval pipelines and show that many existing attacks substantially degrade after reranking despite achieving high retrieval-stage relevance. We identify retrieval granularity mismatch as a key reason for this failure: document-level adversarial signals are often fragmented during chunking, while rerankers favor locally coherent and answer-bearing passages rather than globally optimized semantic similarity. Based on this observation, we propose Chunk-aware and Rerank-Consistent Poisoning (CRCP), a poisoning framework that jointly optimizes retrieval relevance, reranker consistency, and chunk-boundary robustness. CRCP explicitly models chunking transformations during optimization to generate locally self-contained adversarial passages that remain effective under varying chunking configurations. Experiments on standard RAG benchmarks with multiple retrievers and rerankers show that existing poisoning methods are highly sensitive to chunk size and reranking strategies, whereas CRCP achieves substantially higher attack success rates and stronger robustness across realistic retrieval pipelines. Our findings highlight an important realism gap in current RAG security evaluation and suggest that poisoning in modern RAG systems should be studied as a multi-stage retrieval consistency problem rather than a retrieval-only problem.

</details>


### [139] [MPC-Patch-Bench: Security-Aware LLM Code Patch for Multi-Party Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11416)
*Yukuan Zhang,Mengxin Zheng,Qian Lou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对安全多方计算（MPC）软件仓库级代码修复的LLM评估缺乏专门基准，现有通用基准（如SWE-bench）因MPC仓库结构偏向通用Python基础设施、缺失标准化测试、以及安全与数值保真度验证需求而无法适用。本文提出MPC-Patch-Bench，包含数据筛选框架（领域特定筛选代理+人机协同补全引擎，生成205个完整验证实例）和MPC验证器（动态差分测试+静态分析规则），最强LLM功能解决率仅22.9%，经验证器后降至17.1%，高达40%的功能通过补丁因密码学或数值违规被拒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MPC代码合成研究仅覆盖算子级或单框架任务，缺乏面向仓库级MPC代码修复的LLM评估基准；通用基准无法处理MPC领域特有的密码学逻辑、测试缺失和安全性验证需求。

Method: 提出MPC-Patch-Bench，由两个框架构成：(1)数据筛选框架：领域特定筛选代理通过三层密码学过滤原始PR，结合人机协同引擎合成问题陈述和Fail-to-Pass/Pass-to-Pass测试，生成205个验证实例；(2)MPC验证器：通过动态差分测试（对比明文中介）和MPC静态分析规则（检测不安全揭示、不安全算术、非法公/私类型转换）提供安全与数值保真度检查。

Result: 最强LLM功能解决率22.9%，经MPC验证器后降至17.1%；高达40%的功能通过补丁因密码学或数值违规被拒绝。

Conclusion: MPC-Patch-Bench有效揭示LLM在MPC仓库级代码修复中的不足，验证器进一步暴露补丁在密码学安全与数值保真度方面的隐蔽缺陷，现有LLM在该领域仍有显著提升空间。

Abstract: Repository-level benchmarks for evaluating Large Language Model (LLM) code repair on Secure Multi-Party Computation (MPC) software do not yet exist, and directly transplanting general-purpose benchmarks such as SWE-bench fails on three structural fronts: (i) MPC repositories are dominated by generic Python infrastructure rather than cryptographic logic; (ii) high-value MPC fixes lack the standardized tests rigid extraction pipelines require; and (iii) standard fail-to-pass evaluation is insufficient for code that must also be cryptographically safe. MPC is increasingly deployed for privacy-preserving machine learning, biomedical collaboration, and secure analytics. Existing MPC-specific code-synthesis efforts cover only operator-level or single-framework tasks; evaluating LLM agents on real repository-level MPC repair instead demands MPC-aware data curation and a verifier matched to the security and numerical-fidelity guarantees MPC programs must obey neither of which existing benchmarks provide. We introduce MPC-Patch-Bench, a repository-level benchmark organised around two frameworks. (1)The Data Curation Framework combines a domain-specific curation agent that filters raw pull requests through three cryptographic layers with a human-AI completion engine that synthesizes missing problem statements and Fail-to-Pass/Pass-to-Pass tests, yielding 205 fully verified instances. (2)The MPC Verifier provides dedicated security and numerical-fidelity checks via dynamic differential testing against plaintext oracles and MPC-specific static analysis rules that flag unsafe reveals, insecure arithmetic, and illegal public/private casts. The strongest evaluated LLM functionally resolves only 22.9% of MPC-Patch-Bench tasks; the MPC Verifier further reduces verified resolution to 17.1%, with up to 40% of functionally-passing patches rejected for cryptographic or numerical-fidelity violations.

</details>


### [140] [JailbreakOPT: Tool-Assisted Iterative Jailbreak Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11425)
*Ge Shi,Jun Yin,Donglin Xie,Fangyi Liu,Yucan Li,Menglin Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: JailbreakOPT是一个工具辅助的迭代式单轮越狱提示优化框架，通过构建攻击工具库和上下文汤普森采样策略，在减少目标查询次数的同时提高了对大型语言模型的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱攻击方法面临两难：手工构造的提示表达力强但静态不变，迭代式提示优化虽能自适应但依赖底层变异操作，需要大量目标模型查询。亟需一种既能表达多样化攻击策略又能高效优化、减少查询次数的方案。

Method: 1) 构建多样化的原子越狱提示攻击工具库；2) 设计统一的回合内优化抽象，组合原子提示生成更强的单轮攻击提示；3) 将工具选择建模为上下文赌博机问题，应用上下文汤普森采样在攻击回合间复用经验，平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在多个目标LLM和攻击目标上，JailbreakOPT相比原子单轮攻击和现有迭代优化基线，提高了攻击成功率（ASR），同时减少了达成攻击成功所需的攻击次数（No.A）。

Conclusion: JailbreakOPT通过工具辅助和跨回合经验迁移，有效提升了迭代式单轮越狱攻击的效率和效果，为LLM安全评估提供了更强的攻击测试手段。

Abstract: Jailbreak attacks expose persistent safety weaknesses in large language models (LLMs), but existing stateless single-turn methods face a trade-off: hand-crafted prompts are expressive but static, while iterative prompt optimization can adapt but often relies on low-level mutations that require many target queries. We propose JailbreakOPT, a tool-assisted framework for improving iterative single-turn jailbreak prompt optimization. JailbreakOPT organizes diverse atomic jailbreak prompts into an attack tool library and composes them through a unified intra-episode optimization abstraction to generate stronger standalone attack prompts. To reuse experience across attack episodes, JailbreakOPT further frames tool selection as a contextual bandit problem and applies contextual Thompson sampling to guide exploration and exploitation based on past outcomes. Experiments across multiple target LLMs and attack goals show that JailbreakOPT improves attack success rate (ASR) while reducing the number of attacks until success (No.A) compared with atomic single-turn attacks and existing iterative optimization baselines. This paper may contain offensive or harmful content.

</details>


### [141] [Evaluating and Combating the Impact of Concept Drift on the Performance of Machine Learning-Based Phishing Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11471)
*Warren Fernando,Nikos Komninos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了数字领域扩张导致电子邮件通信激增，进而引发垃圾邮件和网络钓鱼等恶意活动增加的问题。研究旨在评估垃圾邮件的演变对基于机器学习的检测系统性能的影响，并探索缓解性能下降的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数字领域扩张，电子邮件通信量大幅增加，垃圾邮件和网络钓鱼等恶意攻击日益严重且不断演变，对现有基于机器学习的检测系统构成挑战，因此需要评估这种演变对检测系统的影响并寻找应对策略。

Method: 采用机器学习这一广泛使用的方法来检测恶意软件和垃圾邮件域中的恶意活动，通过评估垃圾邮件演化对机器学习检测系统的影响，探索缓解性能下降的策略。

Result: 目前尚未在摘要中呈现具体实验结果，但研究目标是评估垃圾邮件领域演变对机器学习检测系统的影响效果。

Conclusion: 研究强调需要关注垃圾邮件演化对机器学习检测系统带来的性能退化问题，并探索相应的缓解策略以维持检测系统的有效性。

Abstract: The expansion of the digital domain has resulted in a substantial increase in digital communication, with email emerging as one of the most prominent channels. The proliferation of email communication is apparent in both professional and personal contexts, thereby creating numerous vulnerabilities for malicious actors to exploit. Spam emails, a form of unsolicited correspondence often bearing malicious intent towards recipients, have been an ongoing challenge for email users since the inception of email technology, and this problem has been exacerbated by the growth of the digital landscape. Email spam filters are integral components of email clients, engineered to identify potentially harmful messages and alert users to their malicious content. Phishing, frequently the initial phase of malware-based attacks, is evolving rapidly, with malware becoming increasingly sophisticated over time. A widely adopted approach for detecting malicious activity within malware and spam domains is the application of machine learning. Our aim is to assess the impact of the evolution within the spam email domain on these machine learning-based detection systems and to explore strategies for mitigating associated performance degradation.

</details>


### [142] [Hiding the Trees in the Forest: Building Network Covert Channels with Hash-Based Covert Carrier Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11532)
*Zexiao Zou,Zhiqiang Wang,Baoxu Liu,Yuyang Han,Yan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于哈希的隐蔽载体过滤策略，通过密钥依赖的过滤规则从载体集中随机动态筛选稀疏子集作为隐蔽载体集，将网络隐蔽信道的隐蔽性与密钥安全性紧密耦合，显著提升了抗检测能力。实验表明，当过滤密钥超过6位时，分类器检测效果显著下降，且单包处理延迟低于8μs，适用于高速网络环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络隐蔽信道的隐蔽性主要依赖于隐蔽算法的保密性，但随着研究深入，算法被破解的难度逐渐降低，一旦算法暴露，隐蔽信道极易被检测到。为解决这一问题，需要一种不依赖算法保密性而依赖密钥安全性的隐蔽机制。

Method: 提出基于哈希的隐蔽载体过滤策略，引入密钥依赖的过滤规则，使通信双方能够从载体集中随机动态地过滤出一个稀疏子集作为隐蔽载体集。采用机器学习流量分析方法在两种类型的网络隐蔽信道（网络存储隐蔽信道和时序隐蔽信道）上进行实验验证。

Result: 该策略显著提升了网络隐蔽信道的抗检测能力；当过滤密钥大小超过6位时，对分类器检测效果的影响变得相当显著；单包处理延迟小于8μs，表明可在高速网络环境中部署。

Conclusion: 基于哈希的隐蔽载体过滤策略通过将隐蔽性与密钥安全性耦合，有效解决了算法暴露导致隐蔽信道被检测的问题，兼具高抗检测性和低延迟优势，适合高速网络环境下的实际部署。

Abstract: As an effective anti-censorship mechanism, network covert channels can provide data privacy protection and ensure communication security. However, the covertness of existing network covert channels primarily depends on the secrecy of their covert algorithms. With the increasing depth of research in this field, the difficulty of breaking such algorithms has gradually decreased. Once the algorithm is exposed, the network covert channel can be easily detected by adversaries. To address this issue, this paper proposes a covert carrier filtering strategy based on the hash. In this strategy, a key-dependent filtering rule is introduced during the construction of the network covert channel, enabling the communicating parties to randomly and dynamically filter a sparse subset from the carrier set as the covert carrier set. This strategy not only enhances the randomness of carrier selection but also tightly couples the covertness of the network covert channel with the security of the key. We employ machine learning-based traffic analysis methods to experimentally validate the strategy in two types of network covert channels: network storage and timing covert channels. The experimental results demonstrate that the proposed strategy significantly improves the detection resistance of network covert channels. When the filter key size exceeds six bits, the impact on the detection effect of the classifier becomes quite significant. Furthermore, the processing delay for a single packet is less than 8 $μs$, indicating the feasibility of deploying the proposed strategy in high-speed network environments.

</details>


### [143] [VIPIR: A Versatile GPU Framework for Integrating Private Information Retrieval Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11536)
*Jongmin Kim,Hyesung Ji,Jean-Luc Watson,Charles Gouert,G. Edward Suh,Jung Ho Ahn*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VIPIR提出了一种通用的GPU框架，通过协同设计PIR协议与GPU加速，引入扩展环打包（ExpPack）压缩方法、张量核心执行矩阵乘法、内存高效调度等优化，相比现有系统实现了数量级的吞吐量提升，同时降低了通信和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私信息检索（PIR）在完全隐藏访问模式的同时，对计算吞吐量、内存容量和内存带宽提出了极高的要求，现有系统难以兼顾这些需求。

Method: 1. 建立统一分析模型，将现有PIR协议归为两类并分析其互补局限；2. 提出两种跨类别灵活组合技术的新协议；3. 设计GPU友好的扩展环打包（ExpPack）数据压缩方法；4. 优化NTT和GEMM等核心操作，提出基于张量核心的混合整数类型GEMM执行方法；5. 设计内存高效调度方法，最小化中间缓冲区并支持多GPU扩展。

Result: VIPIR相比现有PIR系统实现了数量级的吞吐量提升，同时降低了通信和内存开销，使大规模PIR变得实用。

Conclusion: 通过GPU与PIR协议的协同设计，VIPIR有效克服了现有协议类别的互补局限，使大规模隐私信息检索在性能上变得可行。

Abstract: While private information retrieval (PIR) enables private database services by fully concealing access patterns, it simultaneously requires high computational throughput, large memory capacity, and substantial memory bandwidth. We introduce VIPIR, a versatile GPU framework that co-designs PIR protocols with GPU acceleration. We develop a unified analytic model showing that state-of-the-art PIR protocols fall into two categories with complementary limitations, and propose two protocols that flexibly combine techniques across these categories, overcoming the limitations of both classes. These protocols incorporate a GPU-friendly data compression method called expansion-based ring packing (ExpPack), which offers a high degree of parallelism and minimal communication cost. VIPIR applies further optimizations to core operations, including number-theoretic transforms (NTTs) and various matrix-matrix multiplications (GEMMs). Notably, we develop a tensor-core-based execution method for database multiplication by interpreting it as a mixed-integer-type GEMM. We also design memory-efficient scheduling methods that minimize intermediate buffers and enable multi-GPU scaling under memory capacity constraints. Overall, VIPIR achieves orders-of-magnitude higher throughput than prior PIR systems while reducing communication and memory overheads, making large-scale PIR practical.

</details>


### [144] [PriME-Deal: Privacy-Preserving Bilateral Data Trading with Efficient Matchmaking and Auditable Fair Exchange on Blockchain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11539)
*Jie Zhang,Xiaohong Li,Shanshan Xu,Hanwei Wu,Ruitao Feng,Guangdong Bai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PriME-Deal 是一个基于公有区块链的非交互式协议，首次同时实现了策略隐藏的双向匹配、高效阈值访问控制以及可审计的公平交换，无需可信第三方。通过将秘密令牌嵌入带伪随机掩码的 oblivious 键值存储，买方通过标签探测本地重构令牌，并辅以零知识证明验证正确性；公平交换通过抵押链上揭示和加密审计实现。实验表明，卖方发布时间比现有最优方案降低两个数量级（500属性策略下8.76s vs 690s），买方令牌重构及证明生成在典型配置下仅需8.9秒，零知识证明部分恒定低于0.6秒，链上成本约28.6M Gas。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据交易中的双边属性访问控制方案要么泄露策略信息，要么产生超线性开销，要么依赖可信第三方进行争议处理，无法同时满足策略隐藏、密码学公平性和无可信第三方这三个需求。

Method: 卖方将秘密令牌嵌入带伪随机掩码的 oblivious 键值存储（OKVS）；买方通过基于标签的探测（tag-based probing）本地重构令牌，避免组合枚举；零知识证明验证重构正确性；公平交换通过抵押机制的链上揭示实现，加密审计用于惩罚不当行为。

Result: 卖方发布时间比最优阈值模糊IB-ME方案降低两个数量级（500属性策略下8.76s vs 690s）；(200,20,5)配置下买方令牌重构和证明生成共8.9秒，ZKP部分恒定<0.6秒；链上Gas约28.6M，在以太坊区块限制内。

Conclusion: PriME-Deal 是首个实用的隐私保护数据交易协议，同时实现了线性卖方开销、双边策略隐藏和可审计公平性，在通用可组合框架下基于标准假设证明了安全性。

Abstract: Bilateral attribute-based access control for data trading must hide policies, provide cryptographic fairness, and avoid trusted third parties. Existing solutions either leak policy information, incur super-linear costs, or rely on trusted dispute resolution. We present PriME-Deal, a non-interactive protocol that simultaneously achieves policy-hiding bilateral matching, efficient threshold access control, and auditable fair exchange on public blockchains. The seller embeds a secret token under the buyer policy into an oblivious key-value store with pseudorandom masking; the buyer reconstructs the token locally via tag-based probing, eliminating combinatorial enumeration, and proves correctness in zero-knowledge. Fair exchange is enforced through a collateralized on-chain reveal with a cryptographic audit that penalizes misbehaviour without trusted parties.
  We prove security in the Universal Composability framework under standard assumptions. Compared with the state-of-the-art threshold fuzzy IB-ME scheme, the seller's publishing time is reduced by two orders of magnitude (e.g., 8.76s vs. 690s for a policy of 500 attributes). For a typical configuration of (200,20,5), the buyer completes token reconstruction and proof generation in 8.9s, with the zero-knowledge proof taking under 0.6s and remaining constant across all parameter scales. The on-chain cost is approximately 28.6M gas, well within Ethereum's block limit. PriME-Deal thus delivers the first practical privacy-preserving data trading protocol that combines linear seller overhead, bilateral policy hiding, and auditable fairness.

</details>


### [145] [Privacy-Preserving Federated Autoencoder for ECG Anomaly Detection on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11556)
*Kaan Arda Akyol,Jakub Kacper Szeląg,Aydin Abadi,Maha Alghamdi,Ghadah Albalawi,Ghouse Ibrahim Kaleelullah,Hilal Tutus,Sarah Al Subaiei,Shardul Kapse,Syed Mohammed Raheeb,Mujeeb Ahmed,Rehmat Ullah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文设计并评估了一个端到端联邦学习系统，结合联邦平均、差分隐私（DP-SGD）、INT8量化及三种自编码器，在PTB-XL数据集上实现无监督12导联ECG异常检测，同时满足GDPR/HIPAA隐私合规、边缘设备实时推理和跨医院非IID数据要求。关键发现：联邦学习性能不低于集中式基线（ConvAE AUROC 0.782）；ε=4为推荐临床操作点；INT8量化使模型减半、树莓派4延迟降低44%，AUROC损失<0.12%；DP与量化惩罚经验上独立，可同时保证强隐私和小型边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续ECG监测可在心律失常恶化前发现异常，但可部署系统必须同时满足：法律级隐私（GDPR、HIPAA）、受限边缘硬件上的实时推理、以及跨医院非IID数据下的检测质量。现有工作未能同时解决这三项需求。

Method: 构建端到端联邦系统：使用三种自编码器（VanillaAE、ConvAE、VAE）进行无监督异常检测；基于Flower框架实现10个模拟医院间的FedAvg；客户端采用DP-SGD并搭配Rényi-DP审计器；使用INT8训练后量化并在树莓派4上测试。在PTB-XL数据集上进行实验。
