Result: 联邦学习在各架构上均匹配或超越集中式基线（ConvAE联邦AUROC=0.782）；ε扫描确定ε=4为推荐临床操作点；INT8量化使模型大小减半、树莓派4延迟降低44%，AUROC损失<0.12%；DP与量化惩罚经验上相互独立。

Conclusion: 这是首个同时结合联邦学习、形式化(ε,δ)-DP、无监督重建检测和量化AArch64部署的系统。实践者无需在强隐私保证和小型边缘部署之间权衡。

Abstract: Continuous electrocardiography (ECG) monitoring could surface rhythm abnormalities before they escalate into cardiovascular events. However, a deployable system must satisfy three requirements simultaneously: legal-grade privacy (GDPR, HIPAA), real-time inference on constrained edge hardware, and detection quality under non-IID cross-hospital data.
  We design and evaluate an end-to-end federated system addressing all three for unsupervised 12-lead ECG anomaly detection on PTB-XL dataset, combining three autoencoder families (VanillaAE, ConvAE, VAE), Flower-based federated averaging (FedAvg) across ten simulated hospitals, client-side differentially private SGD (DP-SGD) with a Rényi-DP accountant, and 8-bit integer (INT8) post-training quantization with Raspberry Pi 4 benchmarking. Our main contributions are: an empirical characterization of how these mechanisms compose, practical DP-specific recommendations, and technical and security insights for a clinically sensitive setting. Federated learning matches or exceeds the centralized baseline across all architectures (ConvAE federated area under the ROC curve, AUROC, $0.782$), and an $\varepsilon$ sweep identifies $\varepsilon=4$ as the recommended clinical operating point. INT8 quantization roughly halves model size and cuts Pi 4 latency by up to $44%$ with $<0.12%$ AUROC loss. Crucially, DP and quantization penalties are empirically independent, so practitioners need not trade a strong privacy guarantee for a compact edge footprint. To our knowledge, this is the first system combining federated learning, formal $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP, unsupervised reconstruction-based detection, and quantized AArch64 deployment.

</details>


### [146] [WHET: Welding Homomorphic Encryption to Accelerator Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11541)
*Jongmin Kim,Hyesung Ji,Wonseok Choi,Hyunah Yu,Jung Ho Ahn*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WHET通过内存中心、架构感知的优化，将密码学/算法设计与FHE加速器硬件对齐，提出细粒度系数到槽转换、明文压缩和中间模数提升等技术，结合轻量级架构改进，实现了1.38-8.74倍的单位面积性能提升和首个亚毫秒级CKKS自举。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往FHE性能优化主要沿密码学/算法改进和硬件加速两条独立路径推进，未能充分利用硬件能力，导致工作集过大、片外内存流量过高。

Method: 提出内存中心、架构感知的优化策略：1）细粒度系数到槽转换；2）明文压缩；3）中间模数提升。在此基础上引入轻量级架构改进，包括专用缓冲区和功能单元扩展。

Result: 相比最先进的FHE加速器，WHET实现了1.38-8.74倍的单位面积性能提升，并首次实现亚毫秒级CKKS自举。

Conclusion: 通过软硬件协同设计，将密码学算法与加速器架构深度对齐，可以有效降低数据占用和片外内存流量，显著提升FHE加速器的性能和面积效率。

Abstract: Fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) enables computations on encrypted data without decryption, offering strong data privacy at the expense of substantial computational and memory overheads. Prior efforts have steadily improved FHE performance through cryptographic and algorithmic enhancements or hardware acceleration, yet these two directions have progressed largely in isolation, hindering the full exploitation of available hardware capabilities. This work presents WHET, which introduces memory-centric, architecture-aware optimizations to better align cryptographic and algorithmic constructions with FHE accelerator architectures. We identify conventional FHE constructions as major sources of excessive working sets and heavy off-chip memory traffic. We propose accelerator-specific techniques, including fine-grained coefficient-to-slot transformation, plaintext compression, and intermediate modulus raising, to reduce the on-chip data footprint by minimizing temporary ciphertexts and plaintext loads. With these techniques applied, we observe additional opportunities to improve on-chip memory efficiency; hence, we introduce lightweight architectural refinements, including a special-purpose buffer and functional unit extensions. With these optimizations, WHET achieves 1.38-8.74$\times$ per-area performance improvements over state-of-the-art FHE accelerators and the first-ever sub-millisecond CKKS bootstrapping.

</details>


### [147] [A Deterministic Forensic Preprocessing Framework for Heterogeneous Network Datasets: Formal Foundations, Implementation, and Empirical Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11565)
*Ravi Chaudhary,Reza Ryan,Nasim Ferdosian,Nickson M. Karie,Qian Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种确定性取证预处理框架，通过模式归一化、时间归一化和来源追踪三种变换，将异构网络证据转换为可复现的规范形式，支持跨运行的一致性验证，并在多个数据集上实现了100%的输出一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字取证调查中异构网络证据（入侵检测、物联网、企业流量日志）存在模式不兼容、时间戳格式不一致等问题，当前临时处理方法无法验证跨运行或跨分析的一致性，导致可复现性缺口，影响证据可接受性。

Method: 框架定义了三种确定性预处理变换：模式归一化、时间归一化和来源追踪，采用集合论定义进行形式化规范，并通过四个定理确立确定性、信息保持性和来源完整性。架构基于分块设计实现O(c)有界内存。

Result: 在UNSW-NB15、IoT-23和TON_IoT数据集上的实验表明：重复运行间100%输出一致性；对异构时间戳格式具有鲁棒的时间归一化完备性；可从数百万到数亿条记录级别实现可扩展性能。

Conclusion: 该框架为异构网络取证证据的预处理提供了可复现、确定性且可扩展的解决方案，填补了当前取证工具链中一致性验证和证据可采性的关键空白。

Abstract: Digital forensic investigations increasingly depend on preprocessing heterogeneous network evidence from intrusion detection systems, IoT devices, and enterprise traffic logs. Incompatible schemas and timestamp formats hinder evidence correlation and timeline reconstruction, while current ad hoc approaches offer no mechanism to verify consistency across runs or analysis, creating reproducibility gaps that challenge evidence admissibility. This paper introduces a deterministic forensic preprocessing framework that converts heterogeneous network datasets into a reproducible canonical form. The framework formalises three preprocessing transformations: schema normalisation, temporal normalisation, and provenance tracking. These transformations are specified using set-theoretic definitions and supported by four theorems establishing determinism, information preservation, and provenance completeness. A chunk-based architecture provides O(c) bounded memory. Empirical evaluation across UNSW-NB15, IoT-23, and TON_IoT demonstrates 100% output consistency across repeated runs, robust temporal normalisation completeness over heterogeneous timestamp formats, and scalable performance from millions to hundreds of millions of records.

</details>


### [148] [Defense Against Prompt Inversion Attacks: An Information-Theoretic Approach for LLM Collaborative Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11592)
*Sayedeh Leila Noorbakhsh,Hossein Khalili,Nader Sehatbakhsh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于信息论的隐私保护框架，通过在协作边缘-云LLM推理中最小化中间激活与输入提示之间的互信息，实现对抗提示反转攻击的防御，并建立了隐私-效用-延迟的理论权衡保证，实验显示攻击成功率降低高达35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作边缘-云推理中，中间激活的传输易受提示反转攻击，而现有防御主要依赖启发式扰动或经验调参，缺乏对隐私泄露及其与效用、延迟约束相互作用的理论理解。

Method: 提出信息论防御框架，通过最小化中间激活与输入提示之间的互信息来学习隐私保护表示，并设计基于低维信息瓶颈的隐私适配器（privacy adapters）。推导了提示重建误差的理论保证，刻画了隐私-效用权衡，建立了下游推理的token级精度界。

Result: 在多种设置下的广泛实验表明，相比现有防御方法，该方法在隐私-效用-延迟权衡上表现更优，攻击成功率降低高达35%。

Conclusion: 该工作为隐私且高效的协作LLM推理提供了原则性的理论基础，在隐私保护与任务效用之间实现了更优的平衡。

Abstract: Collaborative edge-cloud inference enables resource-constrained devices to leverage large language models (LLMs) by offloading partial computation to cloud servers. However, transmitting intermediate activations exposes sensitive user prompts to prompt inversion attacks, where an adversary reconstructs the original input from shared representations. Existing defenses rely largely on heuristic perturbations or empirical tuning, offering limited theoretical understanding of privacy leakage and its interaction with utility and latency constraints. We propose an information-theoretic defense framework for prompt inversion in collaborative LLM inference. Our approach learns privacy-preserving representations by explicitly minimizing the mutual information between intermediate activations and the input prompt while maintaining task utility under computational constraints. We derive theoretical guarantees on prompt reconstruction error, characterize fundamental privacy-utility tradeoffs, and establish token-level accuracy bounds for downstream inference. We then propose a novel defense based on privacy adapters implemented via low-dimensional information bottlenecks. Extensive experiments across multiple settings demonstrate that our method achieves superior privacy-utility-latency tradeoffs compared to existing defenses (up to 35% reduction in attack success), providing a principled foundation for private and efficient collaborative LLM inference.

</details>


### [149] [Can Open-Source LLM Agents Replace Static Application Security Testing Tools? An Empirical Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11672)
*Derek Yohn,Luke Flancher,Mirajul Islam,Khaled Slhoub*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了基于通用开源GenAI LLM的智能代理在SAST扫描中的价值，发现其性能不及现有SAST工具Bandit，表明当前开源GenAI LLM代理不适合实际条件下的SAST任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索通用型生成式AI大语言模型（GenAI LLM）智能代理在网络安全领域，特别是静态应用安全测试（SAST）中的实际应用价值。

Method: 使用三种不同的Ollama托管的通用开源模型驱动GenAI代理，通过精确率、召回率、假阳性和综合评分等指标评估其性能，并与现有的SAST工具Bandit进行基准对比。

Result: 开源GenAI LLM代理在SAST任务上的表现未达到现有工具Bandit的水平，驳斥了其适用于实际SAST场景的观点。

Conclusion: 当前通用开源GenAI LLM代理不适合在实际条件下用于SAST扫描这一专业任务。

Abstract: This paper explores the value of agentic AI tools for cybersecurity purposes. We evaluate the efficacy of a general-purpose GenAI Large Language Model- (GenAI-) based agent when powered by three different Ollama-hosted general-purpose open source models. We assess each agent's performance using precision, recall, false positive count, and a calculated composite score based upon the interplay of the captured metrics, against the baseline performance of an existing, vetted Static Application Security Testing (SAST) tool, Bandit. Our findings refute the notion that a modern open-source GenAI LLM-based agent is currently suitable for the specialized task of SAST scanning under realistic conditions.

</details>


### [150] [Runtime Skill Audit: Targeted Runtime Probing for Agent Skill Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11671)
*Tu Lan,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出运行时技能审计(RSA)方法，通过在目标运行时条件下动态分析LLM代理技能的实际行为，而非静态检查文档或代码，有效检测恶意技能。在OpenClaw平台100个技能上达到90.0%准确率，比最优静态基线高13个百分点，且在自进化攻击下持续有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理技能虽然能复用指令、资源、工具和工作流，但也为恶意行为提供了新的隐藏场所。技能在文档或代码中看似良性，仅在特定用户请求、本地资产、持久状态或多步工具交互下才变得有害，纯静态审查方法脆弱且不可靠。

Method: 提出运行时技能审计(RSA)动态分析方法：1) 分析风险相关接口；2) 准备执行上下文以触发这些接口；3) 从结果追踪证据中分配安全标签。不同于对所有技能使用相同通用任务测试，RSA针对目标运行时条件进行审计。

Result: 在OpenClaw平台100个技能上，RSA达到90.0%准确率、88.0%真阳性率和8.0%假阳性率，准确率比最优静态基线高13个百分点。在自进化攻击场景下，静态检测器在一两轮后崩溃，而RSA持续检测出19-20个恶意技能。

Conclusion: 动态分析方法是检测LLM代理技能中隐藏恶意行为的有效途径，RSA在准确性和对抗鲁棒性上均显著优于静态基线方法。

Abstract: Agent skills let LLM agents reuse instructions, resources, tools, and workflows, but they also create a new place for malicious behavior to hide. A skill may look benign in its documentation or code while becoming harmful only when it is invoked with particular user requests, local assets, persistent state, or multi-step tool interactions. This makes purely static vetting brittle. We present Runtime Skill Audit (RSA), a dynamic analysis method that audits skills by asking what the skill-mediated agent actually does under targeted runtime conditions. Instead of testing every skill with the same generic tasks, RSA profiles risk-relevant interfaces, prepares the execution context needed to exercise them, and assigns security labels from the resulting trace evidence. We instantiate RSA on OpenClaw and evaluate it on 100 skills against representative static baselines. RSA achieves 90.0\% accuracy with an 88.0\% true positive rate and an 8.0\% false positive rate, improving accuracy by 13.0 percentage points over the best static baseline. Under self-evolving attacks, static detectors collapse after one or two rounds, while RSA continues to detect 19--20 out of 20 malicious skills across rounds.

</details>


### [151] [T2S: A Rehearsal-Based Approach for Extraction-Resistant Model Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11698)
*Jian-Ping Mei,Weibin Zhang,Ao Yao,Tiantian Zhu,Jie Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于排练(rehearsal)的模型水印嵌入框架，通过模拟模型提取攻击过程，利用被窃模型在触发集上的损失来微调目标模型中的水印知识，增强水印的可迁移性，从而提升水印对抗模型提取攻击的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型水印面临的最严重威胁是模型提取攻击，攻击者利用预测输出训练替代模型非法复制原模型功能，现有水印方法对此类攻击的鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出排练式水印嵌入框架：模拟模型提取过程，将模拟被窃模型在触发集上的损失作为训练信号，微调目标模型中的水印知识，使水印嵌入方式更有利于迁移到被窃模型中。

Result: 在多种设置下的综合实验表明，该方法显著提升了模型水印对抗模型提取攻击及后续水印移除攻击的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过模拟提取过程并利用被窃模型的损失信号进行排练式微调，能够有效增强水印的可迁移性和持久性，是对抗模型提取攻击的有效防御策略。

Abstract: Model watermarking safeguards AI model intellectual property by embedding distinctive knowledge that induces unique behavioral signatures. The primary technical challenge lies in ensuring watermark robustness against various post-processing attacks on the watermarked model. Model extraction attacks emerge as the most severe threat, where adversaries exploit prediction outputs to train surrogate models that illegally replicate the original model's functionality. In this work, we propose a rehearsal-based watermark embedding framework to enhance the robustness of model watermarks against model extraction attacks. By simulating the extraction process, our method leverages the loss of a \textit{simulated stolen model} on a trigger set as a training signal to fine-tune the watermark knowledge within the target model. This fine-tuning step encourages the watermark to be embedded in a way that boosts transferability, thereby increasing its chances of persisting and remaining detectable in stolen models. Comprehensive experiments conducted under diverse settings demonstrate that the proposed method significantly improves the robustness of model watermarks against both model extraction and subsequent watermark removal attacks.

</details>


### [152] [A VPN-as-a-Service Tailored Enabler for Computing-constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11729)
*Carolina Fernández-Martínez,César Cajas Parra,Shuaib Siddiqui*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种云原生VPN-as-a-Service (VPNaaS)方案，该方案可轻松编排为每个远程租户按需部署独立隧道，集成IAM工具，并能适应计算或熵受限环境，支持RSA和椭圆曲线(EC)密钥算法选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行业已在云原生环境中采用零信任架构原则，通过细粒度身份管理、监控、隔离和最小权限机制来增强安全防护。现有ZT网络实现依赖租户级隔离的安全隧道，但需要一种更灵活、可编排且适应资源受限环境的VPN服务方案。

Method: 设计并实现了一种云原生VPNaaS，具备三个核心特性：(1) 可编排按需为每个远程租户部署独立隧道；(2) 集成常见IAM工具以支持ZT部署；(3) 适应计算或熵受限环境。方案支持RSA和EC密钥算法选择及参数自定义。

Result: 该VPNaaS方案实现了按需隧道编排、IAM集成，并支持RSA/EC密钥算法选择以适应不同安全需求和资源受限场景。

Conclusion: 该云原生VPNaaS方案具备可定制性，能够灵活适应不同安全等级和资源约束环境，是零信任架构在实验基础设施连接场景中的有效实现。

Abstract: Industry has embraced Zero Trust (ZT) architectural tenets and implementations for cloud-native environments, following stricter security requirements to both internal and external tenants. Among others, these approaches combine fine-grained identity management and monitoring for both inventorying and better analysing the devices' security posture for overall protection, along with strict separation of concerns and isolation to enforce minimal privilege. Networking-wise, ZT approaches rely as well on isolation and least privilege; enacted by separate, secure tunnels per tenant connecting to a given infrastructure. Such implementations can also be applied to the connectivity within and towards experimental infrastructures. In this sense, this work contributes the design and evaluation of a cloud-native VPN-as-a-Service (VPNaaS) that can be (i) easily orchestrated to deploy on-the-fly, separate tunnels per each tenant remotely connecting to the infrastructure; (ii) integrated with common Identity and Access Management (IAM) tools, key to ZT deployments; and (iii) adapt to computing- or entropy- constrained environments. This solution is customisable and allows, among others, to select from RSA or Elliptic Curves (EC) as key generation algorithm and their parameters to achieve more secure keys and adapt to resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [153] [MHOT: Height-Optimized Authenticated Data Structure for Blockchain State Commitment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11736)
*Sipeng Xie,Qianhong Wu,Minghang Li,Qiyuan Gao,Bo Qin,Qin Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Mhot，一种高度最优的认证数据结构，通过判别位索引和分层证明机制，在不依赖可信设置的前提下，实现了比以太坊MPT高9倍的写入吞吐量、低4倍的写放大、小2倍的证明，并对Nurgle攻击保持0%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以太坊MPT状态根计算占区块处理时间78%，且易受Nurgle攻击（通过哈希碰撞膨胀路径深度）。现有方案通过增加节点扇出限制树高，但导致证明大小指数增长；向量承诺方案则依赖可信设置或昂贵验证。

Method: 提出Mhot：1）采用判别位（discriminative bits）索引替代固定前缀索引，实现自适应扇出与线性耦合，达到可证明的最小高度；2）引入分层证明（hierarchical proofs），将每节点证明开销从O(k)降至O(log k)。

Result: 以太坊主网负载下：写入吞吐量提升9倍，写放大降低4倍，证明大小减少2倍。Nurgle攻击下攻击成功率为0%（MPT为99.97%）。

Conclusion: 高度最优性（而非新型密码学原语）是实现可扩展且抗攻击的区块链状态承诺的关键抽象。

Abstract: State root computation dominates (78%) blockchain block processing time. Ethereum's canonical authenticated data structure, i.e., Merkle Patricia Trie (MPT), suffers from severe tree-height growth and is vulnerable to \textit{Nurgle attacks} (SP'24), where adversaries inflate path depth via hash collisions and degrade system performance at negligible cost. Existing defenses increase node fanout (span) to bound tree height, but higher span inflates proof size exponentially. Prior work mitigates this trade-off using vector commitments, at the cost of trusted setup or expensive verification.
  We present \textsc{Mhot}, a height-optimal authenticated data structure for blockchain state commitment that preserves standard hash-based verification without trusted setup. Unlike MPT's fixed-prefix indexing, which couples span and fanout exponentially, \textsc{Mhot} indexes by discriminative bits that actually distinguish keys, achieving adaptive span with linear fanout coupling and provably minimal height. To prevent high fanout from inflating proofs, we introduce hierarchical proofs, a two-layer Merkle construction that reduces per-node proof overhead from O(k) to O(log k).
  On Ethereum mainnet workloads, \textsc{Mhot} achieves up to 9X higher write throughput, 4X lower write amplification, and 2X smaller proofs than MPT. Under Nurgle attacks, even when the adversary consumes an entire block's gas budget, \textsc{Mhot} maintains a 0% attack success rate (v.s., 99.97% for MPT). Our results, somewhat surprisingly, show that height optimality (not new crypto primitives!) is the key abstraction for scalable and attack-resilient blockchain state commitment.

</details>


### [154] [SwarmSense-DNN: A Trustworthy and Decentralized Neural Framework for Proactive Anomaly Defense in Consumer IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11803)
*Jing Yang,Vijay Govindarajan,Saad Arif,Xu Xu,Mohamed Kallel,Zaffar Ahmed Shaikh,Zhe Liu,Chunhong Yuan,Lip Yee Por*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SwarmSense-DNN是一种结合群体智能与深度神经网络的去中心化框架，用于分布式IoT环境下的协同异常检测。它采用分层联邦学习、图神经网络和注意力机制，在保护隐私的同时实现95.44%的平均检测准确率，并将通信开销降低67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级IoT设备的快速增长带来了AI驱动网络威胁下的可信异常检测挑战。传统集中式策略面临通信瓶颈、单点故障和隐私脆弱性等问题，亟需实时、隐私保护且可扩展的去中心化防御机制。

Method: 提出SwarmSense-DNN框架，将自主智能体与深度神经网络结合，形成自组织防御系统；采用分层联邦学习结合图神经网络和注意力机制，捕获局部与全局异常行为，同时通过差分隐私保护确保数据安全。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上平均检测准确率达95.44%，通信开销降低67%；在差分隐私保护下展现出对对抗威胁的鲁棒弹性，并在节点故障和AI攻击下保持强容错能力。

Conclusion: SwarmSense-DNN有效解决了集中式异常检测的瓶颈问题，实现了分布式IoT环境下高效、安全、可扩展的协同异常检测，兼具高准确率、低通信成本和强鲁棒性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of consumer IoT devices has introduced unprecedented challenges in trustworthy anomaly detection against AI-enabled cyber threats, requiring real-time, privacy-preserving, and scalable defense mechanisms. Traditional centralized strategies face critical limitations, including communication bottlenecks, single points of failure, and privacy vulnerabilities when processing distributed consumer data. We propose SwarmSense-DNN, a novel decentralized neural framework employing swarm intelligence for secure, cooperative anomaly detection across distributed IoT environments. The framework integrates autonomous agents with deep neural networks to form a self-organizing defense system that detects evolving anomalies without centralized coordination. It utilizes hierarchical federated learning with graph neural networks and attention mechanisms to capture local and global anomaly behaviors while ensuring data privacy. Extensive experiments demonstrate SwarmSense-DNN's superior performance: it achieves 95.44% average detection accuracy across five benchmark datasets while reducing communication overhead by 67%. The framework maintains robust resilience against adversarial threats through differential privacy safeguards and demonstrates strong fault tolerance under node failures and AI-enabled attacks.

</details>


### [155] [Quadratic APN Functions in Dimension 8 via Gröbner Basis Search in a Self-Equivalence Subspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11967)
*Oleksandr Kuznetsov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在8维二次APN函数的结构化自等价子空间中进行计算搜索，通过结合随机采样与Gröbner基计算方法，在40维二进制线性子空间中发现了6个CCZ等价类的566个二次APN函数，其中4个类（500个函数）为先前数据库未见的新函数，并验证了搜索流水线的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找8维二次APN函数，特别是在一个先前被认为不含APN函数的40维自等价子空间中探索是否存在新的APN函数，以填补已知APN函数分类的空白。

Method: 采用随机采样（通过显式RREF参数化，约600次APN正评估/核时）与Magma中Gröbner基计算相结合的方法，在每个中心的24维超平面上枚举所有APN函数（约10分钟/超平面）。从428个超平面计算（覆盖65,536个超平面的0.65%）中获得结果，并利用正交导数不变量进行CCZ等价类分析。

Result: 获得566个二次APN函数，形成6个CCZ等价类。其中4个类（500个函数）未出现在2025年数据库（3,775,599个函数）或2020年前汇编（12,921个实例）中；2个类（66个函数）与Gold函数x³和x⁹ CCZ等价，验证了搜索流水线的正确性。三个新类（B, C, D）完全位于原始子空间之外，仅出现在Gold中心切片中。

Conclusion: 自等价子空间中的APN搜索发现了多个先前未知的二次APN函数类，且正交导数不变量作为二次APN函数的完全CCZ不变量，为CCZ不等价性提供了严格证明。网关现象仅出现在搜索空间的自等价结构特定条件下，而非普遍存在。

Abstract: We describe a computational search for quadratic APN (Almost Perfect Nonlinear) functions in dimension 8 within a structured self-equivalence subspace. The search space is a 40-dimensional binary linear subspace consisting of all functions commuting with a linear automorphism of order 5 (class 22 in the taxonomy of Beierle, Brinkmann, and Leander, 2021), previously reported to contain no APN functions. Our approach combines random sampling via an explicit RREF parameterization (approximately 600 fresh APN-positive evaluations per core-hour) with Gröbner basis computation in Magma to enumerate all APN functions in a 24-dimensional hyperplane through each center (approximately 10 minutes per hyperplane). From 428 hyperplane computations, covering 0.65% of all 65,536 hyperplanes, we obtained 566 quadratic APN functions forming six CCZ-equivalence classes under the ortho-derivative invariant. Four classes, comprising 500 functions, match no entry in the 2025 database of 3,775,599 quadratic APN functions or in the pre-2020 compilation of 12,921 instances. Two classes (66 functions) are CCZ-equivalent to the Gold functions x^3 and x^9, confirming the correctness of the search pipeline. A membership analysis shows that the three new classes (B, C, D) lie entirely outside the original subspace and occur only in Gold-centered slices, demonstrating the essential role of the Gröbner basis stage. In 532 experiments using database functions as slice centers and 20 experiments with random centers, no APN neighbors were found, indicating that the gateway phenomenon is specific to the self-equivalence structure of the search space. Since the ortho-derivative invariant is a complete CCZ-invariant for quadratic APN functions, the absence of matching signatures provides a rigorous proof of CCZ-inequivalence.

</details>


### [156] [InjectV: Modeling Fault Injection Attacks in RISC-V Simulation Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12011)
*Niccolò Lentini,Giorgio Fardo,Stefano Di Carlo,Alessandro Savino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出InjectV，一种基于gem5模拟器的RISC-V平台故障注入攻击框架，支持在安全关键执行点进行精确引导的故障注入，在FISSC安全基准测试中相比传统方法节省95.8%的时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 故障注入攻击对硬件安全构成重大威胁，但评估系统对此类攻击的弹性面临物理实验成本高、复杂度大且可用性有限等挑战，尤其是在硅前开发阶段。

Method: 基于gem5模拟器，在RISC-V平台上实现架构级仿真，支持在控制流决策、计数器、比较等安全关键执行点进行精确引导的故障注入，支持寄存器和内存中的瞬态故障攻击。

Result: 在FISSC安全基准测试套件（包括VerifyPIN应用的加固变体）上，InjectV能够有效识别故障注入点，相比传统故障注入方法实现了95.8%的时间节省优势。

Conclusion: InjectV为硅前开发阶段的RISC-V平台提供了一种高效、精确的故障注入攻击评估框架，能够系统化地探索攻击向量并显著提升评估效率。

Abstract: Fault Injection Attacks (FIAs) are a significant threat to hardware security, capable of compromising systems by inducing malicious faults in computation or storage. Evaluating resilience against such attacks is challenging due to the high cost, complexity, and limited availability of physical fault experiments, particularly during pre-silicon development. Architectural-level simulation offers a developer-oriented, white-box perspective for systematic vulnerability assessment. This paper introduces InjectV, a fault injection attack framework for RISC-V platforms built on the gem5 simulator. InjectV enables precise, guided fault injection at security-critical execution points, such as control-flow decisions, counters, and comparisons, allowing systematic exploration of attack vectors. It currently supports transient fault attacks in registers and memory, broadening its ability to simulate diverse attack scenarios. Experimental results on security benchmarks from the FISSC suite, including hardened variants of the VerifyPIN application, demonstrate InjectV's ability to effectively identify fault-injection points, achieving a 95.8% time-saving advantage over traditional fault injection approaches.

</details>


### [157] [Categorical Robustness Assessment for Machine Learning based Network Intrusion Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12075)
*Mayank Raj,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Lance Fiondella,Gokhan Kul*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种主流分类器架构（1D-CNN、LSTM、随机森林）在FGSM和PGD对抗攻击下的鲁棒性。实验发现，随机森林虽然基线准确率高达99.98%，但在最小扰动下准确率骤降73个百分点；CNN在ε=0.01时仍保持95.5%准确率，且随扰动增加性能下降平缓；LSTM表现介于二者之间。结论建议在对抗环境中部署基于CNN的入侵检测系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽已证明ML模型在孤立环境下的对抗脆弱性，但缺乏在受控攻击条件下对不同架构、不同攻击类别及攻击类型的系统性跨架构比较，导致从业人员在对抗环境中缺乏明确的模型选择指导。

Method: 使用ACI-IoT-2023数据集（120万+样本，12种攻击类型），对1D-CNN、LSTM和随机森林三种架构分别施加FGSM和PGD梯度对抗攻击，扰动预算范围为ε=0.01至ε=0.1，在归一化特征空间中遵循既定对抗ML评估协议进行实验。

Result: 随机森林基线准确率99.98%，但ε=0.01时下降73个百分点；CNN在ε=0.01时保持95.5%准确率，性能退化呈平缓趋势；LSTM表现介于两者之间。

Conclusion: 高基线准确率并不能保证对抗鲁棒性，在对抗环境中推荐部署基于CNN的架构，并提供场景特定的部署指导。

Abstract: Network Intrusion Detection Systems (NIDS) heavily utlize Machine Learning (ML) but ML models can be manipulated via adversarial attacks. These attacks add carefully crafted perturbations to network traffic data that leads to misclassifications. While prior work has demonstrated adversarial vulnerabilities in isolated settings, systematic cross-architecture as well as class and category of attack based comparisons under controlled attack conditions remain limited, leaving practitioners without clear guidance on which models to deploy in adversarial environments. This paper asks a simple question: what type of classifier architectures actually hold up when attackers try to manipulate the systems? We put three popular architectures through their paces: a 1D Convolutional Neural Network, a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network, and a Random Forest (RF) ensemble. Using the ACI-IoT-2023 dataset (over 1.2 million samples spanning 12 attack types), we subject each model with FGSM and PGD adversarial attacks, which apply gradient-based perturbations in normalized feature space consistent with established adversarial ML evaluation protocols, at perturbation budgets ranging from $ε=0.01$ to $ε=0.1$. Surprisingly, Random Forest achieved near-perfect baseline accuracy (99.98\%), yet collapsed catastrophically under attack, dropping 73 percentage points at the smallest perturbation we tested. CNN, on the other hand, retained 95.5\% accuracy at $ε=0.01$ and degraded gracefully as perturbations increased. LSTM fell somewhere in between. These findings flip the conventional wisdom where high baseline accuracy means nothing if a model shatters at the first sign of adversarial pressure. For practitioners deploying intrusion detection in adversarial environments, we recommend CNN-based architectures and provide scenario-specific deployment guidance.

</details>


### [158] [Bridging the Smart City Cybersecurity Data Gap Through AI-Driven Synthetic Dataset Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12225)
*Stephanie Polczynski,John D. Hastings,Varghese Vaidyan,Kyle Korman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于AI的合成数据生成框架，专门用于智慧城市网络安全研究，通过生成高保真合成数据集来弥补真实数据集难以获取的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智慧城市依赖互联的网络安全系统，但其网络安全工具开发需要高质量数据集，而真实数据集存在不完整、含隐私信息、难以获取、缺乏恶意活动样本等关键缺陷。

Method: 利用生成式AI模型构建合成数据生成框架，生成高保真的合成网络安全数据集，模拟真实设备行为、网络交互和网络攻击场景。

Result: 生成的合成数据集在协议标准符合性、与原数据集的统计相似性以及在常用安全工具中的实用性三个维度进行评估。

Conclusion: 该框架和评估指标能够帮助研究人员更有效地建模威胁、更全面地评估防御技术，从而更好地保护智慧城市关键基础设施。

Abstract: Smart cities rely on interconnected cyber-physical systems that integrate sensors, IoT devices, cloud platforms, and AI-driven services and decision-making. While these systems enhance city services, they also introduce complex cybersecurity challenges due to their large attack surfaces, heterogeneous data flows, and evolving threat vectors. Developing and validating cybersecurity tools for smart cities requires high-quality datasets that accurately represent real operational conditions. However, real-world datasets are often incomplete, contain privacy-sensitive data, are difficult to access, or lack sufficient malicious activity to support tool development. This research addresses this critical gap by proposing an AI-based synthetic data generation (SDG) framework designed specifically for smart city cybersecurity research. The proposed framework leverages generative artificial intelligence models to produce high-fidelity synthetic cybersecurity datasets that replicate realistic device behaviors, network interactions, and cyber-attack scenarios. The synthetic datasets are evaluated for conformity to protocol standards, statistical similarity to original datasets, and utility in common security tools. The resulting synthetic data generation framework and evaluation metrics are expected to advance smart city cybersecurity by enabling researchers to model threats more effectively and evaluate defensive techniques more comprehensively to better protect critical smart city infrastructures.

</details>


### [159] [Partitioned Tags, Shared Data: Reconciling Strict Cache Isolation with Write-Shared Coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12259)
*Kartik Ramkrishnan,Stephen McCamant,Antonia Zhai,Pen Chung Yew*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SCP（安全与一致性分区），通过仅对标签分区、共享数据池并控制数据池大小防止跨分区容量驱逐，结合时间混淆和写阈值机制，在实现严格驱逐隔离和写入共享一致性的同时，有效抵御基于驱逐的缓存侧信道攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的缓存分区技术虽然能防御驱逐型缓存侧信道攻击，但面临一个长期未解决的难题：写入共享一致性在严格分区下会崩溃，这阻碍了其在共享操作系统环境中的广泛部署。

Method: SCP采用三种关键技术：1）仅对标签（tags）进行分区而共享统一的数据池（data pool），并通过合理设置数据池大小避免容量驱动的跨分区驱逐；2）引入时间混淆（timing obfuscation）技术保护跨分区查找路径；3）针对共享可写行的一致性泄露，设定泄露阈值，一旦超过该阈值则将写入请求路由到LLC，使攻击者的写入探测延迟与受害者活动无关。

Result: 基于gem5实现，SCP成功缓解了Prime+Probe、Flush+Reload以及共享可写行攻击，所有攻击结果不优于随机猜测。硬件成本仅增加+2.8% LLC SRAM，性能与DAWG方案相差在0.3% IPC以内（SPEC CPU2017基准测试），共享密集型微基准测试展示了基于系统指定泄露阈值的可调安全-性能权衡。

Conclusion: SCP在保持写入共享一致性的前提下实现了严格驱逐隔离，以较低的硬件开销和性能代价有效防御多种缓存侧信道攻击，为共享操作系统环境下的缓存安全提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Cache partitioning is among the strongest structural defenses against eviction-based cache side channels, yet a decade-old design issue has blocked its widespread deployment in secure shared-OS settings. The issue is that write-shared coherence collapses under strict partitioning. We present SCP (Secure and Coherent Partitioning), which combines strict eviction isolation with write-shared coherence by partitioning only the tags, sharing a single data pool, and sizing the data pool so capacity-driven cross-partition eviction cannot occur. Timing obfuscation extends protections to the inter-partition lookup path. Coherence-based leakage on shared-writeable lines is mitigated by routing those writes through to the LLC once a leakage threshold is crossed, which makes attacker write probe latency independent of victim activity.
  Using gem5 for implementation, SCP mitigates Prime+Probe and Flush+Reload, which are the basis for more sophisticated cache attacks. We also demonstrate that a shared-writeable-line attack is mitigated. All these attacks yield results no better than random guessing. SCP's hardware cost is a modest +2.8% LLC SRAM. Performance matches DAWG within 0.3% IPC on the SPEC CPU2017 benchmarks that we evaluated. Sharing-intensive microbenchmarks demonstrate a tunable security-performance tradeoff based on a system-specified leakage threshold.

</details>


### [160] [Selection Integrity for LLM Graph Memory: An Accumulability Criterion for Information-Flow-Blind Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12290)
*Zeming Fei,Hongming Fei,Xiaoyang Wang,Yang yang,Prosanta Gope,Biplab Sikdar,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示基于图谱的智能体长期记忆系统中，现有所有溯源性防御存在根本性盲区：由于记忆系统包含全局选择步骤，不可信主体写入的结构会改变哪些经过认证的事实被选中，而引用的证据本身仍保持完全认证。这使得忠实的信息流控制（IFC）与无防御时做出字节完全相同的决策。作者提出AuthSelect防御，在认证子图上重新计算选择，以零过阻塞和2-3%延迟代价完全阻断该隐蔽信道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆系统正向图结构演进，所有针对溯源的安全防御仅检查检索到的记录的来源。作者发现这类防御存在根本性构造缺陷：长期图记忆运行全局选择步骤，不可信主体写入的结构会改变哪些认证事实被选中，而引用的证据保持完全认证，导致现有防御完全失效。

Method: 1. 分析图记忆系统的信息流，证明忠实IFC与无防御决策一致；2. 通过真实多会话智能体记忆实验和文档QA基底验证攻击有效性；3. 最极端案例中，无源结构写入在499个实时操作中静默误导向28次不可逆账本转账，忠实IFC允许全部而AuthSelect阻止全部；4. 理论刻画暴露的记忆类型：当结构项可以重新分配top-k成员资格的Ω(1)份额超过所选事实的边界时，选择器就允许该信道；5. 证明个性化PageRank易受攻击（无源写入重新路由守恒随机游走质量），内容固定重排序器不受影响，Graphiti节点距离免疫；6. 证明免疫情况的通解以及存在瓶颈条件下的可解情况。

Result: 1. 证实基于图谱的智能体长期记忆存在隐蔽信道，现有所有溯源性防御（包括忠实IFC）对此完全盲目；2. 在真实多会话智能体记忆场景中，无源结构写入可成功实现不可逆转账误导向；3. AuthSelect防御以零过阻塞和仅2-3%延迟代价完全阻断该信道；4. 精准刻画了哪些记忆系统暴露、哪些免疫：可重分配性（reallocatability）而非依赖性是预测因子。

Conclusion: 任何溯源性防御必须强制在认证子图上重新计算选择过程才能关闭该信道。AuthSelect实现了这一要求，以极小的性能开销提供了完全的安全保障。可重分配性是判断记忆系统是否易受此类攻击的关键指标，而非对结构的依赖程度。

Abstract: Agent memory is moving to graphs, and the provenance defenses now being built for it all check one thing: the provenance of the records an agent retrieves. We show that this entire class of defense is blind by construction. A long-term graph memory runs a global selection step over writable graph structure, so structure that an untrusted principal writes changes \emph{which} authenticated facts are selected while the cited evidence stays fully authenticated; faithful information-flow control (IFC), checking the provenance of what the reader uses (all of it authenticated), makes the byte-identical decision to no defense at all, across document-QA substrates and real multi-session agent memory. In the most consequential instance, a no-source structural write silently misdirects $28$ irreversible ledger transfers over $499$ live actions: faithful IFC permits every one, and \authselect\ prevents every one. We then characterize exactly which memories are exposed: a selector admits the channel when its structural term can reallocate an $Ω(1)$ share of top-$k$ membership past a selected fact's margin. Personalized PageRank can, since a sourceless write reroutes conserved random-walk mass; a content-fixed reranker cannot, and Graphiti's node-distance, which leans on structure \emph{more} than PageRank does, stays immune. Reallocatability, not reliance, is the predictor. We prove the immune case in general and the open case under a chokepoint condition we verify. Closing the channel forces any provenance defense to recompute selection on the authenticated subgraph, which is what \authselect\ does, at zero over-block and $2$--$3\%$ latency.

</details>


### [161] [Grammar-Constrained Decoding Can Jailbreak LLMs into Generating Malicious Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11817)
*Yitong Zhang,Shiteng Lu,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种名为CodeSpear的新型越狱攻击，利用语法约束解码（GCD）诱导LLM生成恶意代码；并提出防御方法CodeShield，通过生成蜜罐代码来安全对齐模型。实验表明CodeSpear显著提升攻击成功率，CodeShield能有效恢复安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语法约束解码（GCD）被广泛用于提升LLM代码生成的可靠性，但本研究发现这一可靠性导向技术本身可成为攻击面，即GCD可能被利用来诱导LLM生成恶意代码，这是一个反直觉的安全风险。

Method: CodeSpear攻击：利用GCD的语法约束来越狱LLM，使其生成恶意代码。CodeShield防御：通过在代码模态下对齐模型，教会它在GCD约束下生成语义无害且结构多样的蜜罐代码，同时保留自然语言拒绝能力。

Result: 在10个流行LLM和4个基准上的实验表明，CodeSpear比代表性越狱基线方法平均提高超过30个百分点的攻击成功率；CodeShield能有效恢复CodeSpear下的安全性，同时保持良性用例的效用。

Conclusion: 这项研究揭示了GCD存在根本性安全风险，呼吁对其潜在安全影响给予更多关注。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation, raising concerns that they may be misused to produce malicious code. Meanwhile, Grammar-Constrained Decoding (GCD) has been widely adopted to improve the reliability of LLM-generated code by enforcing syntactic validity. In this paper, we reveal a counterintuitive risk: this reliability-oriented technique can itself become an attack surface. We uncover a new jailbreak attack, termed CodeSpear, that exploits GCD to induce LLMs into generating malicious code. Our experiments show that simply applying a benign code grammar constraint can effectively jailbreak LLMs.
  To address this vulnerability, we propose CodeShield, a safety alignment approach that robustly preserves safe behavior even under attacker-controlled grammar constraints. CodeShield aligns the model in the code modality by teaching it to generate honeypot code under GCD. Such code is semantically harmless, so it does not implement the malicious request, and structurally diverse, so it is difficult to suppress through grammar tightening. At the same time, CodeShield still preserves natural-language refusals when natural language is available. Experiments on 10 popular LLMs across 4 benchmarks show that CodeSpear outperforms representative jailbreak baselines and increases the attack success rate by more than 30 percentage points on average. CodeShield also restores safety under CodeSpear while preserving benign utility. Our findings reveal a fundamental risk of GCD and call for greater attention to its potential security implications.

</details>


### [162] [OCELOT: Inference-Leakage Budgets for Privacy-Preserving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12341)
*Jin Xie,Songze Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: OCELOT是一种运行时中介，通过后验风险控制来管理LLM代理在轨迹中的隐私泄露，采用证人验证的解分类机制，在保持任务效用的同时显著降低累积性、双向性和任务依赖性的隐私泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在执行用户任务时，会跨越信任边界泄露个人身份信息（PII），且隐私泄露具有累积性（无害信息累积可推断出秘密）、双向性（恶意观测可注入指令操控代理）和任务依赖性（同一字段对不同接收者意义不同）。现有方法（上下文完整性过滤器、信息流控制、后验泄露监控）均无法在运行时控制基于推理的累积性泄露。

Method: 提出OCELOT，一种运行时隐私中介，将代理隐私重新定义为后验风险控制，通过证人验证的解分类机制运作：不可信的本地微调防御模型检查候选释放并生成结构化证据（标注原子和提议的解分类操作符），确定性验证器审计这些证据，为所选变体收取认证的最小熵代价，并在防篡改账本上记录基于接收者信任权重的预算消耗，授权最少披露的有用释放。

Result: 在多种代理基准和最新防御方法对比中，OCELOT在更高任务效用下实现了显著更低的泄露，能够抵抗自适应注入、越狱攻击、累积推理和接收者合谋，且仅增加适度的计算开销。

Conclusion: OCELOT通过后验风险控制和证人验证解分类机制，有效解决了LLM代理轨迹中隐私泄露的累积性、双向性和任务依赖性问题，为运行时隐私保护提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly act on a user's behalf -- reading personal files, calling tools, transacting with external services -- possibly leaking personally identifiable information (PII) across trust boundaries at every step. Privacy here is a property not of a single output but of an entire trajectory, and three properties make it hard: leakage is cumulative, as individually innocuous releases accumulate across honest-but-curious or colluding sinks into inferences about a protected secret; bidirectional, as a malicious observation can inject instructions that turn the agent's own reasoning model against the user; and task-dependent, as the same field is necessary for one recipient yet gratuitous for another. Per-release contextual-integrity filters, information-flow controls, and posterior-leakage monitors each address part of this but none controls cumulative, inference-based leakage at runtime. We recast agent privacy as \emph{posterior-risk control} and present OCELOT, a runtime mediator that budgets how much an adversary's belief about a secret may improve across a trajectory, rather than filtering outputs. Its mechanism, \emph{Witness-Verified Declassification}, separates judgment from trust: an untrusted, locally fine-tuned defender model inspects each candidate release and emits structured evidence -- labeled atoms and proposed declassification operators -- which a deterministic verifier audits, charging a certified min-entropy cost for the chosen variant and authorizing the least-disclosing useful release under a sink-trust-weighted budget recorded on a tamper-evident ledger. Across diverse agent benchmarks and recent defenses, OCELOT attains significantly lower leakage at higher task utility, resists adaptive injection, jailbreak, cumulative inference, and sink collusion, and adds only modest overhead.

</details>


### [163] [ECYSAP EYE: From Cyber Situational Awareness to Mission-Centric Decision Support for Enhanced Cyberspace Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12354)
*Pantaleone Nespoli,Daniel Díaz-López,Sergio Lopez Bernal,Francisco Oliva Bermejo,Pedro González Megías,Jorge Maestre Vidal,Víctor Sobrino García,Gregorio Martínez Pérez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ECYSAP EYE提出了一种面向采用的系统之系统架构，通过七类任务导向的工件（RCyP、CySR、WIAR、OPRE、DSH、AE、AAR）实现从感知到决策再到执行学习的完整网络态势感知循环，支持异构工具链的增量部署与集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运营组织需要超越孤立技术警报的网络态势感知能力，能够提供与任务相关的工件并嵌入异构工具链和网络安全/防御流程。

Method: 采用面向采用的系统之系统（SoS）架构，定义七组任务导向的工件，构建从感知（全谱RCyP视图）到决策推理（WIAR/CySRs/OPRE）再到执行学习（DSH/AE/AAR）的结构化转换流程，并提供显式集成接口支持增量部署与验证。

Result: 形成了更新的SoS架构、七类工件的功能定义，以及网络态势对任务规划与执行中决策过程影响的预期效果。

Conclusion: ECYSAP EYE从技术转移视角展示了该创新项目，明确了架构、工件角色及其对网络态势决策过程的预期影响。

Abstract: Operational organizations increasingly require Cyber Situational Awareness (CySA) capabilities that go beyond isolated technical alerts, providing mission-relevant artefacts that can be embedded into heterogeneous toolchains and cyber security or cyber defense processes. ECYSAP EYE addresses this need through an adoption-oriented System-of-Systems (SoS) architecture centered on seven groups of mission-focused artefacts: the Recognized Cyberspace Picture (RCyP), Cyber Situational Reports (CySRs), the What-If Analysis Report (WIAR), Option Recommendations (OPRE), an operator Dashboard/HMI (DSH), Action Enforcement (AE), and After-Action Reports (AAR). The ECYSAP EYE architecture structures the transition from perception (full-spectrum RCyP views), to decision-oriented reasoning (WIAR/CySRs/OPRE), and to operational execution and learning (DSH/AE/AAR), with explicit integration surfaces that support incremental deployment and validation. This paper presents this innovative project from a technology transfer perspective, summarizing the updated architecture, the functional role of seven groups of artefacts, and the expected impact of cyber situations on the decision-making process in the context of a mission planning and execution.

</details>


### [164] [MARCIM-WG: A cyber wargame proposal based on math modeling applied in a naval scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12395)
*Diego Cabuya-Padilla,Daniel Díaz-López,Carlos Castaneda-Marroquín*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了MARCIM-WG，一种基于北约兵棋推演方法论的学习型海事网络防御兵棋，结合物理棋盘与计算模拟，验证表明干预组在网络安全态势感知能力上提升34个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海事操作日益依赖互联数字生态系统，网络事件可在海事网络中传播并降级关键服务。需要训练机制让决策者暴露于演变的攻击动态、资源约束和事件响应程序中，以增强战略网络态势感知能力。

Method: 遵循北约兵棋推演方法论，设计为混合桌面体验：结合物理棋盘（代币、指示器、特殊卡牌）与计算模拟支持的辅助裁决。通过高层设计（HLD）和低层设计（LLD）规范说明，并在虚构海事网络危机场景中实例化。

Result: 干预组在CSA能力上提升34.0个百分点，理解相关能力增益最大。在三种配置（悲观、中立/最可能、乐观）下验证了决策敏感性和结果一致性。

Conclusion: MARCIM-WG能有效提升海事网络防御中的态势感知能力，是支持结构化决策和可测量后果的学习型兵棋工具。

Abstract: As maritime operations increasingly depend on interconnected digital ecosystems, cyber incidents can propagate across maritime networks and degrade critical services. Strengthening strategic Cyber Situational Awareness (CSA) therefore requires training mechanisms that expose decision-makers to evolving attack dynamics, constrained resources, and the need to align actions with incident-response procedures. This paper introduces MARCIM-WG, a learning-oriented maritime cyberdefense wargame designed following the NATO wargaming methodology and implemented as a hybrid tabletop experience combining a physical board (tokens, indicators, and special cards) with analytically-assisted adjudication supported by a computational simulation model. The proposal is specified through High-Level Design (HLD) and Low-Level Design (LLD) specifications and instantiated in a fictional maritime cyber crisis scenario to enable structured decision cycles, friction, and measurable consequences. Validation combines (i) an operational scenario-based assessment under three configurations (pessimistic, neutral/most likely, optimistic) to verify decision sensitivity and outcome coherence, and (ii) a CSA competency and learning-outcome evaluation using a comparative design against an equivalent control group. Results show a +34.0 percentage-point improvement in the intervention group, with the largest gains in comprehension-related competencies.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Annealed Entropic Allocation for Ranking and Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11347)
*Xin Fei,Juergen Branke*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出退火熵分配（Annealed Entropic Allocation）框架，通过加权对数-求和-指数（log-sum-exp）替代非光滑的最大-最小大偏差率目标，并结合鞍点逼近改进有限预算下的区分能力，在多个竞争者接近时表现出竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最大-最小大偏差率目标在处理多个接近活跃的竞争者时存在硬切换问题，导致预算分配不连续且区分效果差。

Method: 采用加权对数-求和-指数（log-sum-exp）替代非光滑的最大-最小目标函数，通过软最小权重聚合挑战者特定的配对得分；引入鞍点逼近作为亚指数修正项；将平滑参数退火至零以保持一阶大偏差目标的一致性。

Result: 证明替代函数一致收敛到硬最小值；软最小权重集中在活跃挑战者上；固定权重下诱导的目标分配映射在单纯形内部连续；在正态和指数实例上表现出竞争性能，尤其是在多个挑战者接近绑定时。

Conclusion: 退火熵分配框架在保持大偏差最优性的同时，有效缓解了硬切换问题，提高了有限预算下的区分能力。

Abstract: We propose Annealed Entropic Allocation, an annealed weighted soft-min framework for sequential budget allocation in ranking and selection. The central idea is to replace the non-smooth maximin large-deviation rate objective with a weighted log-sum-exp surrogate that aggregates challenger-specific pairwise scores through soft-min weights, mitigating hard switching when several challengers are nearly active. To improve finite-budget discrimination, we incorporate the saddlepoint approximation -- a sub-exponential correction derived from refined pairwise tail asymptotics. Because these corrections are sub-exponential and the smoothing parameter is annealed to zero, the surrogate preserves the same first-order large-deviation target as the classical maximin formulation. We show that the surrogate converges uniformly to the hard minimum, that the soft-min weights concentrate on the active challengers, and that, under fixed weights, the induced target allocation map is continuous on the simplex interior. Numerical experiments on Gaussian and exponential instances demonstrate competitive performance, especially when multiple challengers are nearly tied.

</details>


### [166] [Enhancing Spectral Embedding through Robust and Flexible Knowledge Transfer in Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11570)
*Feiqing Huang,Zongqi Xia,Rong Ma,Tianxi Cai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于谱分解的无监督表示学习框架，通过从大样本人群中提取部分重叠子空间的知识矩阵，解决罕见病电子健康记录中高维小样本的嵌入问题。方法采用两步谱嵌入：先移除知识矩阵中的无关成分，再通过投影分别恢复共享和特异性成分。在模拟和真实多发性硬化症队列中，该方法尤其在弱信号、部分对齐的挑战场景下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病队列中电子健康记录数据维度高但样本量极其有限，传统方法难以有效学习低维表示。现有方法假设潜在数据矩阵与知识矩阵之间存在严格的一对一信号对齐，这种假设在实际罕见病场景中过于严格且不现实。

Method: 提出两步谱嵌入流程：第一步，通过谱分析识别并从知识矩阵中移除与目标数据无关的成分；第二步，采用基于投影的方法分别恢复共享成分（与知识矩阵对齐）和异质性成分（特异性信息）。该方法放宽了现有方法对知识矩阵与目标数据之间信号对齐的严格假设。

Result: 模拟实验和在真实多发性硬化症队列上的分析表明，所提方法在多种场景下优于对比方法，尤其在共享信号弱且仅部分对齐的挑战性场景中表现突出，而这正是罕见病数据的典型特征。

Conclusion: 所提出的无监督谱嵌入框架通过灵活利用外部知识矩阵，有效解决了罕见病高维小样本的表示学习难题，放宽的对齐假设更贴近实际数据特征，为罕见病临床概念和患者嵌入提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a spectral-based, unsupervised representation learning framework to derive low-dimensional embeddings for clinical concepts and patients in rare disease cohorts from electronic health records, where data are high-dimensional but sample sizes are limited. To overcome this challenge, we incorporate a knowledge matrix extracted from a broader population that shares a partially overlapping subspace with the rare-disease cohort. Our method departs from existing approaches by relaxing restrictive one-to-one signal-alignment assumptions between the latent data matrix and knowledge matrix, allowing more flexible and realistic forms of structured sharing. We introduce a novel two-step spectral embedding procedure: first, we identify and remove irrelevant components from the knowledge matrix; then, we apply a projection-based method to separately recover shared and heterogeneous components. Simulations and an analysis of a real-world multiple sclerosis cohort show that the proposed method outperforms competing approaches, particularly in challenging scenarios where shared signals are weak and only partially aligned, as is common in rare-disease data.

</details>


### [167] [Renewable Lasso without Batch-Number Constraints: A Gradient-Enhanced Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11738)
*Junzhuo Gao,Ling Peng,Xu Guo,Heng Lian*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对高维广义线性模型的流式数据，提出基于梯度增强的替代损失方法，消除了对批次数量的严格约束，并扩展到分布式流式数据场景，推导出非渐近误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可再生估计方法在高维流式数据中受到批次数量的严格约束，且分布式场景下直接应用Jordan等人的方法需要客户端计算完整替代损失，效率不高。

Method: 提出梯度增强替代损失（gradient-enhanced surrogate loss），仅利用历史摘要近似累积损失；在分布式架构中，仅交换梯度向量摘要，客户端无需计算完整替代损失。

Result: 推导了高维缩放下的非渐近误差界，消除了对批次数量的严格约束；在线性和逻辑回归模型上的仿真及真实数据应用显示，精度优于现有可再生估计器。

Conclusion: 所提方法在高维流式数据场景下更高效、约束更少，且精度更优，适用于非分布式和分布式设置。

Abstract: We study online estimation for high-dimensional generalized linear models with streaming data. First, for the non-distributed setting, we propose a gradient-enhanced surrogate loss that approximates the cumulative loss using only historical summaries, which modifies and improves upon the existing renewable estimation approach for the same model in the high-dimensional setting, and removes the batch-number constraint in previous studies. We then extend the method to distributed streaming data under the master-client architecture, where batches are partitioned across sites and only summaries (gradient vectors) are exchanged. Instead of directing applying the popular method of Jordan et al. (2019) to the surrogate quadratic loss, our adjusted approach does not require the clients to compute the full surrogate loss. We derive non-asymptotic error bounds under the high-dimensional scaling, without the stringent constraint on the number of batches in the previous studies. Simulation results under linear and logistic models, together with a real-data application, show improved accuracy over existing renewable estimators.

</details>


### [168] [From Persistence to Survival: Hypothesis Testing, Effect Sizes and Vectorisation for Topological Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11911)
*Juliette Murris,Bernadette Stolz,Karsten Borgwardt*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: STRAND将持久性图视为生存数据，通过持久性生存函数统一实现了假设检验、效应量计算和特征向量化，是首个从单一可解释表示中同时提供假设检验和向量化的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久性图作为拓扑数据分析的常见表示，不天然存在于向量空间中，其统计比较工具与下游预测工具发展相对独立，缺乏一个统一的框架。

Method: 将持久性图视为生存数据：每个拓扑特征的持久值p=d-b被视为完全观察到的事件时间，以持久性生存函数S(t)=P(p>t)为核心对象，从中导出非参数两样本检验、可解释效应量和1-Wasserstein稳定的特征向量。

Result: 在合成流形上验证了检验的校准性和统计功效；在14个图和3D点云基准上展示了向量化的竞争力；并应用于fMRI/神经科学中的功能性脑连接分析。

Conclusion: STRAND是首个从单一连贯可解释表示中同时提供持久性图假设检验和向量化的方法。

Abstract: Persistence diagrams are common representations in topological data analysis, but they do not naturally live in a vector space, and the statistical tools developed for comparing them have largely evolved separately from those used for downstream prediction. We introduce STRAND (Survival Topological Representation ANalysis of Diagrams), which treats (collections of) PDs as survival data: each topological feature with persistence value $p = d - b$ is a fully observed time-to-event, and the persistence survival function $S(t) = \mathbb{P}(p > t)$ is the central object for comparing diagrams. From this single representation we derive (i) a non-parametric two-sample test with calibrated Type I error and high power from a small number of diagrams; (ii) interpretable effect sizes; and (iii) a 1-Wasserstein-stable feature vector for downstream machine learning. We validate calibration and power on synthetic manifolds with controlled topology, demonstrate competitive vectorisation across 14 graph and 3D point cloud benchmarks, and apply the method to study functional brain connectivity in fMRI/neuroscience data. To our knowledge, STRAND is the first method to provide hypothesis testing and vectorisation for persistence diagrams from a single coherent and interpretable representation.

</details>


### [169] [Phase Transitions in Attention: A Bayesian Theory of Copy Head Emergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12058)
*Itay Lavie,Kirsten Fischer,Andrey Lekov,Frederic Van Maele,Zohar Ringel,Moritz Helias*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从贝叶斯理论角度分析了Transformer中注意力机制的特征学习，发现拷贝子电路的学习存在相变现象：softmax注意力呈现一阶相变，而线性注意力先经历二阶相变后平滑过渡到结构化模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释Transformer中注意力模式在训练过程中突然涌现（abrupt emergence）的内在机制，尤其是拷贝子电路（copy subcircuit）的学习过程。

Method: 采用贝叶斯理论框架分析单层softmax注意力网络在拷贝任务上的训练过程，推导注意力矩阵的闭式后验分布，并将其降维至低维序参数空间进行分析。

Result: 发现训练数据量存在相变阈值；softmax注意力表现为一阶相变（first-order phase transition），而线性注意力先出现二阶相变（second-order phase transition），随后平滑连续演化为结构化注意力模式（crossover）。

Conclusion: 从第一性原理出发，理论解释了大型语言模型训练中观察到的注意力模式突然涌现现象，揭示了softmax与线性注意力在学习动力学上的本质差异。

Abstract: Attention is the key mechanism underlying in-context learning in transformers, and attention patterns have been observed empirically to emerge abruptly during training. We present a Bayesian theory of feature learning in attention; we then focus on how the copy subcircuit in the first layer of an induction head is learned by analyzing a single-layer softmax attention network trained on a copy task. We derive a closed-form posterior over the attention matrix and reduce it to a low-dimensional order parameter space. This reduction reveals a phase transition in the amount of training data, which we verify using both Bayesian sampling and standard training with Adam. We contrast our results with linear attention and find that softmax attention exhibits a \emph{first-order phase transition} while in linear attention an initial \emph{second-order phase transition} is followed by a smooth, continuous evolution toward the structured attention pattern (\emph{crossover}). Our work provides a first-principles theoretical account of the abrupt emergence of the copy subcircuit, reminiscent of the one observed in training large language models.

</details>


### [170] [Conformal Bayes under Label Shift: Post-Hoc Calibration vs. In-Training Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11865)
*Seungjin Choi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出两种应对标签偏移的共形贝叶斯方法：后验校准（通过重要性加权修正预测分布和共形阈值）和训练中适应（通过调整参数后验本身）。实验表明无偏训练下两种方法均有效，而在引导优化场景中训练中适应能减少区间宽度同时保持覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将共形贝叶斯方法扩展到标签偏移场景，确保预测集在目标域上既有统计有效性又有几何效率。

Method: 提出两种互补方法：1）后验校准：通过重要性加权倾斜后验预测分布并修正共形阈值；2）训练中适应：直接调整参数后验以适应目标域，利用HPD区域构建预测集。

Result: 无偏训练模式下两种方法均能实现有效覆盖；在引导优化模式下，训练中适应作为去偏算子，在保持覆盖的同时减少区间宽度。

Conclusion: 两种策略各有适用场景，训练中适应在特定偏差场景下具有去偏优势，能提升预测效率。

Abstract: Conformal Bayes combines Bayesian posterior predictives with conformal calibration to produce prediction sets that are both statistically valid and geometrically efficient. We study conformal Bayes under label shift from a unified perspective, identifying two complementary approaches that restore nominal target-domain coverage through importance-weighted conformal calibration but operate through independent mechanisms. \emph{Post-hoc calibration} tilts the posterior predictive toward the target domain and corrects the conformal threshold via an importance-weighted quantile, leaving the parameter posterior unchanged. \emph{In-training adaptation} tilts the parameter posterior itself to the target domain, producing a corrected predictive whose highest predictive density region serves as the highest predictive density (HPD) based prediction set under the fitted target predictive; efficiency is model-dependent and does not imply finite-sample conditional optimality. Two controlled experiments show that in an unbiased training regime both strategies achieve valid coverage equally, while in a lead-optimization regime in-training adaptation acts as a debiasing operator, reducing interval width at unchanged coverage.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Unbiased Derivative Estimation for Stationary Mean of Parameterized Markov chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11487)
*Jeffrey Wang,Chang-han Rhee*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的无偏估计方法，用于估计参数化马尔可夫链平稳均值的梯度，特别适用于混合速度慢的链，且无需对密度函数有额外知识，适合神经网络参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在马尔可夫链混合速度慢时，梯度估计效率低，且通常需要特定的参数化形式或对密度函数的完整知识，限制了在神经网络等复杂参数化场景中的应用。

Method: 提出一种新的无偏梯度估计器，仅需一个能评估转移密度及其梯度在黑箱数据点处的Oracle，无需对密度函数本身有额外了解，适用于任意参数化（包括神经网络）。

Result: 理论证明了该估计器在混合慢的马尔可夫链上能显著提升效率；数值实验验证了理论预测的良好性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为慢混合马尔可夫链的梯度估计提供了一种高效、通用且易与神经网络结合的无偏估计方案。

Abstract: We propose a new approach to unbiased estimation of the gradients of the stationary means associated with parametrized families of Markov chains. Our estimators are particularly efficient when the Markov chains have slow mixing rate. Our approach does not require a specific parametrization except for an oracle to evaluate the transition density and its gradient at a given data point without any additional knowledge about the density function itself. It makes our estimator suitable for parametrizations associated with neural networks. The estimator can potentially achieve large improvement in terms of efficiency. Numerical experiments confirm the good performance predicted by the theory.

</details>


### [172] [Geometric bias in eigenspace perturbation under random heterogeneous noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11263)
*Fengkai Liu,Ke Wang,Wanjie Wang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏异质噪声下信号加噪声矩阵的谱扰动问题，发现经典Davis-Kahan/Wedin界无法捕捉信号几何与噪声分布的精细交互，导致在异质方差场景下存在系统性几何偏差。通过二次向量方程（QVE）和精细化的各向同性局部律，推导了算子范数和2→∞范数下近最优的非渐近扰动界，分离了信噪比贡献、随机波动和结构化几何偏差项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典谱方法依靠Davis-Kahan和Wedin定理来刻画特征空间在随机扰动下的稳定性，但这些最坏情形界在低秩信号加随机噪声场景下过于保守，尤其无法捕捉信号几何与噪声分布之间的精细交互。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究异质方差噪声下谱扰动中系统性的几何偏差机制。

Method: 利用二次向量方程（QVE）技术，建立精细化的各向同性局部律，从而推导非渐近的扰动界。该框架将扰动误差分解为信噪比贡献、随机波动项和由信号特征空间与行方差谱对齐决定的结构化几何偏差项。

Result: 获得了算子范数和2→∞范数下近最优的非渐近谱扰动界，揭示了异质噪声方差下经验特征向量存在经典界无法检测的系统性确定性几何偏差，并定量刻画了该偏差与信号-方差谱对齐的关系。

Conclusion: 经典谱扰动界在异质噪声场景下存在根本性局限，本文提出的基于QVE的新框架能更精确地刻画实际扰动行为，为理解和发展更鲁棒的谱方法提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Spectral methods rely fundamentally on the stability of principal eigenspaces under random perturbations. Classically, this stability is quantified by the Davis-Kahan and Wedin theorems, which bound the eigenspace error using the operator norm of the noise and the relevant spectral gaps. While these worst-case bounds are sharp for arbitrary deterministic perturbations, they can be wasteful in the low-rank signal-plus-random-noise setting, as they fail to capture the fine-grained interaction between the signal geometry and the noise distribution. In this paper, we study the spectral perturbation of signal-plus-noise matrices corrupted by sparse, random noise with an arbitrary, inhomogeneous variance profile. We demonstrate that under heterogeneous noise variances, the empirical eigenvectors suffer a systematic, deterministic geometric bias that is entirely invisible to classical perturbation bounds. By leveraging the Quadratic Vector Equation (QVE) and establishing fine-grained isotropic local laws, we derive near-optimal, non-asymptotic perturbation bounds for the leading eigenspaces in the operator and $2\to\infty$ norms. The bounds separate the usual signal-to-noise contribution, stochastic fluctuations, and structured geometric bias terms determined by the alignment between the signal eigenspaces and the row-wise variance profile.

</details>


### [173] [Posterior consistency of Pólya trees for deconvolution under the linear model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11406)
*Nakul Shenoy,Asaf Weinstein*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了Weinstein等人(2025)提出的贝叶斯非参数反卷积方法，在Pólya树先验下证明了后验分布在超范数下围绕真实密度g0的集中性，扩展了Castillo(2017)关于密度估计的后验一致性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线性模型反卷积问题中，目标是从含噪观测Y = Xβ + ε中估计未知分布G0（密度g0），此前方法缺乏严格的理论保证，本文旨在为Pólya树先验下的贝叶斯非参数反卷积方法提供后验一致性理论依据。

Method: 采用Pólya tree先验Π作用于未知密度g0，基于后验分布Π(·|Y)进行反卷积估计。主要分析工具建立在Castillo(2017)的密度估计结果之上，并对其进行了扩展以适用于反卷积场景。关键条件涉及XᵀX的最小特征值。

Result: 在真实模型（固定未知的g0）下，当XᵀX的最小特征值满足适当条件时，后验分布Π(·|Y)在超范数（sup-norm）意义下围绕g0集中。

Conclusion: 本文为Pólya tree先验下的贝叶斯非参数反卷积方法提供了后验一致性理论保证，将Castillo(2017)从密度估计问题拓展到了更一般的反卷积问题。

Abstract: Several recent works have addressed the problem of deconvolution under a linear model, where the goal is to estimate a completely unknown $G_0$ from a vector of noisy observations $\boldsymbol{Y} = X\boldsymbolβ + \boldsymbolε$, assuming the coefficients $β_j$ are i.i.d. unobserved realizations from $G_0$. Assuming $G_0$ has a density $g_0$, we study theoretically a Bayesian nonparametric method proposed in Weinstein et al. (2025) that postulates a Pólya tree prior $Π$ on $g_0$ and bases a deconvolution estimate on the posterior distribution $Π(\cdot|\boldsymbol{Y})$. Our main result asserts that under the true model (fixed and unknown $g_0$), and under a suitable condition on the minimum eigenvalue of $X^\top X$, the posterior $Π(\cdot|\boldsymbol{Y})$ concentrates around $g_0$ in sup-norm. The analysis presented builds on and extends results from Castillo (2017), where posterior consistency of Pólya trees was proved for density estimation, the simpler problem of estimating $g_0$ when observing the coefficients $β_j$ directly.

</details>


### [174] [Notes on the Theory of Statistical Symbol Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11726)
*Nils Lid Hjort*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文是Nils Lid Hjort于1986年撰写的《统计符号识别理论笔记》专著（挪威计算中心报告No.778/1986）的PDF重制版，系统阐述了为适应当时技术条件（1986年）而开发的统计模式识别理论与算法，应用于噪声图像中的符号识别与分类分析，涉及地图、文档、卫星成像等领域，并与SysScan公司、挪威皇家研究委员会等工业项目密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足多个工业项目中从噪声图像进行符号识别与分类分析的实际需求，需要一套适合当时计算和机器系统限制的统计模式识别理论与算法。

Method: 基于统计模式识别理论，开发适应1986年技术条件（有限计算资源、扫描文档转换为向量表示）的符号识别算法与系统方法。

Result: 形成了207页的统计符号识别理论专著，以及配套的系统开发报告（No.777/1986），实现了统计方法与技术实现的双轨并行发展。

Conclusion: 该研究在统计方法和工程技术两个前沿紧密协作，成功构建了适应当时技术条件的符号识别理论与实用系统。

Abstract: This document is a pdf generated from old plain-TeX files of 1986, of Nils Lid Hjort's `Notes on the Theory of Statistical Symbol Recogntion', a limited circulation 207-pages monograph published at the Norwegian Computing Centre, as Report no. 778/1986. It gives the basics of the statistical pattern recognition theory developed to suit the needs of several industrial projects, related to contracts with the Norwegian-German firm SysScan, the Royal Norwegian Research Council, and yet others, involving symbol recognition and classification analysis from noisy images, related to maps, documents, satellite imaging, etc.
  The methods and algorithms developed also needed to fit the technology of that time, anno c. 1986, with machines scanning documents, converting these to vector representation, within computational and machine system boundaries. There is an accompanying and also limited circulation booklet, `Statistical Symbol Recognition: Development of a System', by Knut Bråten, Erik Holbæk-Hanssen, and Torfinn Taxt (Report No.777/1986, Norwegian Computing Centre, Oslo), detailing the system developments. Thus developments took form and shape on two frontiers, in close collaboration, Hjort's statistical methods and getting the technology to work, with its multiple components, hardware and software.

</details>


### [175] [Testing axial symmetry in multivariate location-scale linear regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11933)
*Šárka Hudecová,Miroslav Šiman*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文在多元线性异方差回归框架下，提出了基于积分秩得分的条件轴向对称性检验方法，推导了渐近分布，并通过模拟和真实经济数据验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多元线性异方差回归模型中条件轴向对称性的检验问题，填补现有方法在回归设定下的空白。

Method: 基于积分秩得分构建新的检验统计量，推导其渐近分布，将多元数据的轴向对称性检验扩展到回归框架下。

Result: 通过模拟研究和真实经济数据验证了所提检验方法的有效性和应用价值，同时提供了若干关于轴向对称性的理论结果。

Conclusion: 提出的检验方法不仅能有效检验回归模型中的条件轴向对称性，还可用于评估误差项分布性质的具体假设，具有独立理论意义。

Abstract: The article deals with the problem of testing conditional axial symmetry within a~multivariate linear heteroscedastic regression framework. A new test based on integrated rank scores is introduced and its asymptotic distribution is derived. The proposed method extends a similar procedure developed for multivariate data to the regression setting. The test may also be employed to assess specific hypotheses concerning distributional properties of the error term. Its performance and application is illustrated in a small simulation study and with real economic data. The article also contains a few theoretical results regarding axial symmetry that may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [176] [A Discrete Cumulative Distribution Transform via Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12131)
*Harbir Antil,Gustavo Rohde,Aryan Saxena*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对实直线上的原子概率测度的全离散累积分布变换（CDT），通过单调分位数映射定义，仅基于累积质量匹配实现前向变换和逆重构的显式线性时间算法，并建立了精确有限分辨率恢复的累积质量兼容性准则，同时证明了参考细化下的弱收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典连续CDT无法直接适用于离散原子测度（如有限点集），且确定性传输在离散情形下不能分裂质量，导致有限分辨率下精确重构可能失败。因此需要建立一套严格的离散CDT理论框架。

Method: 通过单调分位数映射定义离散CDT，采用累积质量匹配实现线性时间的前向变换与逆重构；提出累积质量兼容性准则判定精确重构条件；引入阈值稳定化技术将框架扩展至带符号的离散累积分布变换。

Result: 建立了精确有限分辨率恢复的充分必要条件（累积质量兼容性准则）；证明了参考细化下重构测度的弱收敛性；推导了平移、复合、缩放等结构性质；数值实验验证了平移线性化、兼容性控制的重构、细化一致性及符号变换的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该离散CDT框架避免了连续插值，为离散数据提供了一种简洁的固定参考传输表示，兼具理论严格性和计算高效性。

Abstract: This paper develops a fully discrete cumulative distribution transform (CDT) for atomic probability measures on the real line. The transform is defined through monotone quantile maps and admits explicit linear-time algorithms for both forward transformation and inverse reconstruction based solely on cumulative mass matching. Unlike the classical continuous setting, deterministic transport between atomic measures cannot generally split masses, so exact reconstruction may fail at finite resolution. We establish a precise cumulative-mass compatibility criterion for exact finite-resolution recovery and prove weak convergence of reconstructed measures under reference refinement. Several structural properties of the discrete CDT are derived, including translation, composition, and scaling laws, and the framework is extended to a discrete signed cumulative distribution transform with thresholded stabilization near zero crossings. By avoiding continuous interpolation, the proposed framework provides a simple fixed-reference transport representation for discrete data. Numerical examples illustrate translation linearization, compatibility-controlled reconstruction, refinement consistency, and stabilization of the signed transform.

</details>


### [177] [Integrated expectile-based measures of inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12333)
*Ignacio Cascos,Marco Tarsia*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一族基于期望分位数的积分型泛函，用于衡量风险、离散度和不平等性，这些泛函可表示为期望分位数在不对称水平上的积分，并具有星形集加权面积的几何解释，从而构建了一类新的基于期望分位数的不平等指数，作为经典基尼系数的自然对应物，并自然推广到多元情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 期望分位数作为一类非对称位置泛函，能够纳入偏离幅度并具有自然的几何解释，但其在风险、离散度和不平等性测量方面的积分型构造尚未被系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立基于期望分位数积分泛函的统一理论框架。

Method: 基于期望分位数与凸随机序的结构一致性，定义一族积分型期望分位数泛函；通过将泛函表示为期望分位数关于不对称水平的积分，建立分析表示；对于子类构造，利用星形集加权面积给出几何表示；推导出闭式经验估计量及其有限样本分解；通过方向性期望分位数构造将框架扩展到多元情形。

Result: 得到了一族新的基于期望分位数的不平等指数，该指数保持了理想的单调性和一致性性质；经验对应量具有闭式解和显式样本分解；多元扩展能够捕捉联合形式的多元离散度和不平等性。

Conclusion: 所提出的积分型期望分位数泛函为风险、离散度和不平等性测量提供了统一的理论框架，尤其是新构建的不平等指数作为基尼系数的自然替代方案，在保持良好公理化性质的同时具有解析可处理性和几何直观性，且可自然推广到多元场景。

Abstract: Expectiles provide a class of asymmetric location functionals that incorporate the magnitude of deviations and admit a natural geometric interpretation. Building on their structural consistency with the convex stochastic order, this paper introduces a family of integrated expectile functionals for measuring risk, dispersion, and inequality. The proposed functionals admit analytical representations as integrals of expectiles across asymmetry levels. For a distinguished subclass of these constructions, a geometric representation is available: the resulting quantities can be expressed as weighted areas of star-shaped sets encoding the distributional asymmetry of a random variable. This approach yields a new class of expectile-based inequality indices, constituting a natural counterpart to classical Gini-type measures while preserving desirable monotonicity and consistency properties. Empirical counterparts are derived in closed form and admit explicit decompositions over finite samples. The framework extends naturally to multivariate settings through directional expectile constructions, leading to measures capable of capturing genuinely joint forms of multivariate dispersion and inequality.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Signed Compression Progress on a Sealed Audit is Goodhart-Resistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11417)
*Ayush Mittal,Dhruv Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并证明了“压缩进步”（compression progress）作为内在奖励信号的可靠性。核心定理表明：如果内在奖励定义为固定密封审计损失（sealed-audit loss）的符号下降量（r_t = E(θ_{t-1}) - E(θ_t)），则累积奖励精确等于端到端的审计改进量，任何策略都无法在真实审计表现停滞或退化的情况下无限推高奖励。对于有限审计面板，累积经验奖励最多等于真实审计改进加上模型类均匀审计偏差的2倍，且该上界是无限视界的——随时间自适应不会增加额外成本。实验在ARC-TGI网格变换生成器上验证了理论：有限审计偏差按n^{-0.527}标度，符号进步抵抗了clip-farming、流泄漏和噪声-TV好奇心等攻击，而标准发布防御可将攻击控制在2Δ_n阈值以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩进步作为内在动机的机制提出已久，但其“可信性”（即只奖励真正的学习）缺乏严格的形式化证明。本文旨在将该直觉精确化并给出数学证明，同时识别失效模式并实验验证。

Method: 1) 理论推导：通过望远镜求和将累积内在奖励映射为端点审计改进；2) 有限审计面板上引入均匀审计偏差（uniform audit deviation）给出概率上界；3) Lean 4机械化核心结构（望远镜性质、有限审计界、有限Gibbs、熵下界）；4) ARC-TGI网格变换生成器实验，测试clip-farming、流泄漏、噪声-TV好奇心以及可复用审计攻击。

Result: 1) 定理成立：符号压缩进步作为密封审计损失差异时，累积奖励等于真实改进；2) 有限审计面板上奖励上界为真实改进+2Δ_n；3) 实验显示攻击无法突破2Δ_n阈值；4) 揭示了失效模式：裁剪进步、在智能体自身流上评分、高容量模型可复用面板、神经类导致Δ_n变平庸。

Conclusion: 密封审计上的符号压缩进步是一种真实改进的会计信号（accounting signal），可作为可信的内在动机使用。核心见解是“审计损失差异”形式保证了奖励的可靠性，而常见变体（如非符号、非密封、大容量模型）会破坏这一保证。

Abstract: Compression progress is a long-standing proposal for intrinsic motivation: reward an agent when its world model becomes better at predicting or compressing experience. The folk claim is that this reward is "credible" because it is paid only for learning. We make this precise and prove it. If intrinsic reward is the signed decrease of a fixed sealed-audit loss, r_t = E(theta_{t-1}) - E(theta_t), then cumulative reward telescopes exactly to endpoint audit improvement, so no policy can push reward up indefinitely while true audit performance stagnates or degrades. For finite audit panels the same result holds with a sharp false-positive budget: cumulative empirical reward is at most true audit improvement plus 2 Delta_n(F, delta), the uniform audit deviation of the model class. This is horizon-free: adaptivity over time costs nothing once the sealed panel uniformly controls the class.
  The theorem also identifies the failure modes: the guarantee disappears if progress is clipped, scored on the agent's own stream, exposed to a high-capacity model on a reusable panel, or applied to a neural class that makes Delta_n vacuous. We give a Lean 4 mechanization of the structural core (telescoping, the finite-audit bound, finite Gibbs, and the entropy floor) and an experiment suite on ARC-TGI grid-transformation generators with adaptive holdout attacks. Experiments confirm the theory: finite-audit deviation scales as n^{-0.527}; signed progress resists clip-farming, stream leakage, and noisy-TV curiosity; naive reusable audits are exploitable by black-box scalar feedback, while standard release defenses keep the attack below the 2 Delta_n threshold. Signed compression progress on a sealed audit is an accounting signal of genuine improvement.

</details>


### [179] [CRUMB: Efficient Prior Fitted Network Inference via Distributionally Matched Context Batching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11473)
*Jamie Heredge,Mattia J. Villani,Pranav Deshpande,Akshay Seshadri,Niraj Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CRUMB方法，通过聚类测试查询、基于MMD选择分布匹配的小训练子集，使PFN类表格基础模型能够高效处理大规模训练集，在51个数据集上优于现有上下文选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PFN模型的自注意力机制导致推理成本随训练集大小呈二次增长，无法有效处理大规模训练数据。

Method: 三阶段推理框架：①聚类测试查询；②通过贪心最小化最大均值差异(MMD)为每个簇选择分布匹配的小训练子集；③对每个缩减后的上下文批次执行精确PFN推理。

Result: 在TabArena基准的51个数据集上，对TabPFNv2、TabICLv1、TabICLv2三种架构均优于现有上下文选择策略，且对协变量漂移具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CRUMB是一种架构无关、无需重训练的推理包装方法，通过分布对齐的上下文选择有效解决PFN在大规模数据上的推理可扩展性问题。

Abstract: Prior-fitted networks (PFNs) are a promising class of tabular foundation models that perform in-context learning, whereby the entire labelled training set is supplied as context, and predictions for test queries are produced in a single forward pass. However, the quadratically scaling self-attention mechanism in many PFN architectures makes inference prohibitive for very large training datasets. We propose CRUMB (Clustered Retrieval Using Minimised-MMD Batching), a three-stage inference wrapper that (i) clusters the test queries, (ii) selects a small, distributionally matched training subset for each cluster by greedily minimising the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD), and (iii) runs exact PFN inference on each reduced-context batch. CRUMB is architecture-agnostic and requires no retraining. On the 51-dataset TabArena benchmark, evaluated across three PFN architectures (TabPFNv2, TabICLv1, TabICLv2), we show that CRUMB outperforms similar state-of-the-art context selection strategies. We also show that CRUMB is resilient to covariate drift, as the MMD-minimisation step naturally helps align the training context distribution to match the current test batch distributions.

</details>


### [180] [Range-Aware Bayesian Optimization for Discovering Diverse Designs within Target Property Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11574)
*Shengli Jiang,Jason Wu,Charles M. Schroeder,Michael A. Webb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种范围感知的贝叶斯优化框架，通过直接评估候选解满足目标范围的后验概率，有效恢复多样化的有效设计方案，适用于多规格并行搜索场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际材料与产品设计中，候选方案往往需要落在可接受范围内而非单一最优值，且需要多个不同的可行解以兼顾成本、可加工性等难以直接编码的因素，现有方法在多样化有效解恢复方面存在不足。

Method: 开发了范围感知贝叶斯优化框架，其采集函数直接计算候选解满足目标范围的后验概率；该框架自然扩展到在共享搜索空间上并行追求多个不同规格的目标。

Result: 在基准任务中，范围感知采集一致地恢复出比标准BO基线和近期目标追求方法更大且更多样化的有效设计集；在聚合物合成反应条件优化和序列定义低聚物光学吸收带发现两个实际案例中验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: 范围感知BO可为规格驱动的设计提供实用且样本高效的框架，尤其在设计灵活性和解多样性至关重要时表现出显著优势。

Abstract: In many materials and product design problems, desirable candidates exhibit properties that fall within an acceptable range rather than achieve a single optimum. Recovering multiple, distinct solutions that satisfy such specifications is also practically valuable, as some candidates may be preferred for reasons of cost, processability, or robustness that are difficult to encode directly in an objective function. Here, we develop a range-aware Bayesian optimization (BO) framework in which the acquisition function directly scores the posterior probability that a candidate satisfies a target range. The framework naturally extends to parallel pursuit of multiple distinct specifications over a shared candidate space. Across benchmark tasks, range-aware acquisition consistently recovers larger and more diverse sets of valid designs than standard BO baselines and recent goal-seeking methods. Its utility is further demonstrated in two practically motivated design case studies involving optimizing reaction conditions for polymer synthesis and sequence-defined oligomer discovery for prescribed optical absorption bands, supported by quantum chemical calculations. These results suggest that range-aware BO can provide a practical and sample-efficient foundation for specification-driven design, particularly when design flexibility and solution diversity are important considerations.

</details>


### [181] [Tree-Structured Orthonormal Decomposition of the Aitchison Simplex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11646)
*Daisuke Yamada,Qijun Zhang,Travis Pence,Barbara B. Bendlin,Federico Rey,Vikas Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolyILR提出了一种与任意树拓扑结构对齐的Aitchison切空间规范正交分解方法，在微生物组和单细胞数据上实现了稳定、可解释的多尺度特征表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的成分数据分析方法要么忽略特征间的已知层次结构（如分类学、系统发育、本体论），要么丢弃内在的Aitchison几何结构，或者仅适用于二叉树、生成不完整的坐标系统，缺乏一种能同时保留层次结构与Aitchison几何性质的规范方法。

Method: PolyILR通过在Aitchison切空间中构建与任意树拓扑对齐的规范正交基：首先在每个内部节点定义加权局部几何结构以捕获完整的分支信息，然后将这些局部结构提升为全局正交基，使得每个坐标对应树中特定的位置。

Result: 在微生物组和单细胞基准测试中，PolyILR产生了稳定且可解释的特征，并支持多尺度树分辨率下的推理。

Conclusion: PolyILR提供了一种通用的成分数据层次分解框架，建立了与softmax分类器之间的新颖理论联系，暗示了在概率建模中的潜在应用。

Abstract: Compositional data -- vectors encoding relative proportions -- arise across scientific domains, including ecology, geochemistry, and genomics. The features in these data often come with known hierarchical structure (e.g., taxonomies, phylogenies, ontologies), yet existing methods either ignore this structure, discard the intrinsic Aitchison geometry, are designed for binary trees, or yield incomplete coordinate systems. We describe PolyILR, a canonical orthonormal decomposition of the Aitchison tangent space aligned with any tree topology. Our construction defines a weighted local geometry at each internal node capturing full branching structure, then lifts these to a global orthonormal basis where every coordinate corresponds to a specific tree location. On microbiome and single-cell benchmarks, PolyILR yields stable, interpretable features and enables inference at multiscale tree resolution. We also establish a novel theoretical connection to softmax classifiers, suggesting possible applications to probabilistic modeling.

</details>


### [182] [Capacity-Constrained Online Convex Optimization with Delayed Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11711)
*Alexander Ryabchenko,Idan Attias,Daniel M. Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在硬容量约束下的延迟在线凸优化问题，即最多只能同时跟踪C个未决轮次。通过引入半先验延迟模型，将问题规约到一种新的“延迟加权”OCO问题，并提出了Delayed-Weighted FTRL及其赌博机变体，首次为容量约束OCO提供了凸损失和强凸损失下的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际场景中跟踪资源有限，无法跟踪所有未决轮次，未被跟踪的反馈会永久丢失。现有延迟OCO研究通常假设可以跟踪所有未决轮次，这不符合现实约束。本文旨在解决在硬容量约束下如何进行有效的在线学习。

Method: 1) 提出半先验延迟模型（semi-clairvoyant model），在线观察延迟到期而非事先已知延迟。2) 通过随机化跟踪决策的调度器和对观测结果进行重要性加权的规约方法，将问题转化为“延迟加权”OCO问题。3) 针对该基础问题提出Delayed-Weighted FTRL及其赌博机变体，分析时变权重与延迟反馈的交互。4) 组合调度器与基础学习器得到完整算法。

Result: 一阶反馈下，容量C = Ω(log T)即可恢复标准延迟OCO的遗憾率（至多对数因子）。赌博机反馈下，遗憾率由(1 + σ_max/C)的幂次调控，其中σ_max为最大未决观测数，当C < σ_max时遗憾界优雅退化但仍保持次线性。

Conclusion: 本文首次为容量约束下的延迟OCO提供了凸损失和强凸损失的遗憾保证，涵盖一阶和赌博机反馈。理论结果刻画了容量C与延迟特征之间的明确关系，展示了即使跟踪资源有限，仍可通过精心设计的调度与加权机制实现有效学习。

Abstract: Online learning with delayed feedback typically assumes that the learner can track all pending rounds until their feedback arrives. In practice, tracking resources are finite, and feedback from untracked rounds is permanently lost. In this paper, we study delayed online convex optimization (OCO) under a hard capacity constraint, where at most $C$ pending rounds can be tracked at any time. To model delay information, we introduce a semi-clairvoyant model that refines the clairvoyant assumption from prior work: rather than requiring delays to be known at prediction time, the learner observes delay expirations online, consistent with the classical unconstrained delayed setting. Our approach proceeds via a reduction to a novel ``delayed and weighted'' OCO problem, using a scheduler that randomizes tracking decisions and importance-weights the resulting observations. For this base problem, we propose and analyze Delayed-Weighted FTRL and its bandit analogue, establishing regret bounds that explicitly characterize the interaction between time-varying weights and delayed feedback. Combining these base learners with our schedulers yields the first regret guarantees for capacity-constrained OCO under convex and strongly convex losses, for both first-order and bandit feedback. For first-order feedback, capacity $C = Ω(\log T)$ suffices to recover standard delayed OCO rates up to logarithmic factors. For bandit feedback, the regret rates are modulated by powers of $(1 + σ_{\text{max}}/C)$, where $σ_{\text{max}}$ is the maximum number of pending observations at any time. This allows the regret bound to degrade gracefully when $C < σ_{\text{max}}$, while remaining sublinear.
